frontmatter_stores.rs

1//! K11 — a raw class's title-page STORES reroute to the frontmatter API.
2//!
3//! A class loaded raw keeps its title page in stores: `\newcommand\inst[1]
4//! {\gdef\@inst{#1}}` (ptptex.cls:616), `\long\def\abst#1{\long\gdef\@abst{#1}}`
5//! (jpsj2.cls:841-847), read only by `\@maketitle`, which LaTeXML's locked
6//! `\maketitle` never runs. Perl drops every such store (its ~200 class
7//! bindings — and the generic `OmniBus.cls.ltxml:62-247` table, bypassed
8//! under raw class loading — are its only route); the Rust
9//! `\lx@deposit@maketitle` surpass typeset them as body paragraphs. This
10//! module is OmniBus's table applied after the raw load, keyed on what the
11//! class actually defined — a setter a binding defined is the binding's, even under a raw class
12//! that loads it (`latexml_core::binding::store_setters`): a setter is rerouted only when (a) its NAME is in
13//! the surveyed table (`~/data/pk_agents/w23/frontmatter_stores/`, 655 TL
14//! classes; an explicit synonym table, never a name pattern — `ead` matches
15//! `\setoddhead`, `date` matches `\cref@updatelabeldata`) and (b) its macro
16//! BODY is a pure store of its argument (`#1`, or `#2` of an `[optional]{mandatory}` setter) — a
17//! check on the tokens, never a guess.
18//! Everything else the class defined is left alone, but its contact setters that print their argument as a title-page
19//! line (`reroute_in_place_contact_setters`, after a raw class or package: OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #936); the
20//! kernel's own locked `\title`/`\author`/`\date`/`\thanks` are not in the table. A store is handed
21//! to the API as it is set, except one that annotates a creator (an affiliation,
22//! address, e-mail, ORCID) or makes one (an editor, a speaker), which is read at
23//! `\maketitle` where the author list exists. The class's `\@maketitle` is deposited with the handed stores read as
24//! empty (`\lx@deposit@maketitle`, sect05.rs), so it adds only what the
25//! frontmatter does not carry.
26//!
27//! The table's keywords setters reach `\lx@add@keywords` in two more shapes a class gives them (68n), each found
28//! by a check on what the class defined, never a name pattern: a one-argument setter the class defines only for
29//! its abstract's body (`abstract_setter_definitions`: wiley-article.cls:446-489 inside the `\NewEnviron` its
30//! `\abstract` becomes, astlb.cls:199-205 inside the `{pazhabstract}` its `\@maketitle` typesets the abstract in,
31//! mtt.cls:176-181 inside its own `{abstract}`), which the kernel's locked `{abstract}` never runs, so `\keywords`
32//! was undefined (330 of 373 run-353 papers: 1706.00599, 1801.08881, 1202.2889, astro-ph0306137, 1507.06302,
33//! 2103.05801, 1310.0585); and an environment whose begin code only typesets a heading carrying the class's label
34//! (`label_only_begin_code`: paper.cls:621-627 `\def\keywords{\small\paragraph*{\keywordname:}}`), which became a
35//! run-in `<paragraph>` where OmniBus made keywords (OmniBus.cls.ltxml:126-156). OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #634.
36//!
37//! Three more shapes of the table's stores (95d): a table name a raw file made a token register that only printed code
38//! prints — in a macro the title page reaches or a page style it selects (`toks_store`: googlecloud.cls's
39//! `\newtoks\correspondingauthor`, printed in its `\fancypagestyle{firststyle}` foot, 2607.06973; OD #939); the table
40//! setters a raw PACKAGE defines, rerouted at its end as a raw class's are, unless a raw class's read loads it
41//! (`reroute_raw_package_stores`: medima.sty's dates under elsarticle, 2307.16262; OD #940); and a table setter that
42//! prints a mark before its pure store (`table_setter_stores`: astlb.cls's `\email`, 1202.2889; OD #941). A raw package's
43//! setter's own `\@<name>` is a store only when code that never runs here reads it
44//! (`ClassReaders::read_where_nothing_runs`: rlj.sty's `\@keywords`, read by an uncalled `\makeCover`, 2605.31044);
45//! a date or classification is named only by a label the code its title page reaches prints, in a branch's `\else` too
46//! (medima.sty's `\finalformhead~\@finalform`), or before the test (`Received: \ifx…\else\@received\fi`), a phrase
47//! ending at a `;` or `,` (pazha2.cls's `…; revised \@revised`), else by its role's default only where that word is
48//! printed right before the store (wiley2sp.cls's `\receivedname`), else unnamed (OD #940).
49//!
50//! The unclaimed-text net (98c, OD #953; FIDELITY_CHARTER §4): any other store the class's title-page code reads that
51//! the document set, that the title page prints and no kept run typeset, is a note of the author block, kept only for
52//! lines of 3 or more words no frontmatter field holds (`net_candidates`, `hand_net_stores`; insert.rs
53//! `keep_unclaimed_net_notes`): copernicus's `\AB@affillist`, read only by its own `\@author` (sect05.rs
54//! `keep_class_author_reader`; 0712.1312), sig-alternate's `\tntoks`, read only by its refused `{abstract}` (1206.6940).
55//! Below that floor (105a) a line of addresses alone is the e-mail contacts of the note's creator (aaskaiid.sty's
56//! `\email@toks`, 2606.27706, 2607.25721), a code's or a date's line is kept, and any other is counted in a Warning
57//! naming its store and words (2103.14934).
58//!
59//! Displaced text (99b, OD #955): a raw file's affiliation or address setter that stores its argument for its title page
60//! among code of its own, which K11's pure-store test leaves (rstransa's `\gdef\@address{#1\ifaffbreak…\fi}`, iau's
61//! numbered `\csname affil\the\affilcount\endcsname`), runs under a meaning recorder recording what it stored
62//! (`reroute_displaced_setters`); at `\maketitle` what the title page prints of it is the creators' contacts, taken out of
63//! the deposit by that record (`route_and_hand_displaced`). An in-place setter with no line break of its own (iopjournal's
64//! `\affil`) is the API's where its call stands as a line (`run_in_contact_setter`). Each line of either is placed by
65//! its leading label (`contact_line_call`).
66use std::collections::VecDeque;
67
68use latexml_core::{
69  binding::store_setters::{
70    STORE_SETTERS, defined_by_a_binding, defined_by_a_raw_file, in_a_raw_class_read,
71  },
72  definition::origin::{DefinitionOrigin, with_origin},
73  document::tag::TagContent,
74};
75use rustc_hash::FxHashSet;
76
77use crate::{
78  base_utilities::{FmArgSeparator, FmSepMeaning, argument_separators},
79  prelude::*,
80};

82/// The definers a store is made with: `\gdef\@name{#1}`, `\def`, `\xdef`/`\edef`, LaTeX's `\protected@xdef`, and
83/// `\g@addto@macro\@name{#1}` (an accumulating store).
84const STORE_DEFINERS: &[&str] = &[
85  "\\gdef",
86  "\\def",
87  "\\xdef",
88  "\\edef",
89  "\\protected@xdef",
90  "\\protected@edef",
91  "\\g@addto@macro",
92];
93/// Prefixes and grouping a setter's definitions may carry, which typeset nothing.
94const DEFINITION_FRAME: &[&str] = &[
95  "\\long",
96  "\\global",
97  "\\protected",
98  "\\relax",
99  "\\begingroup",
100  "\\endgroup",
101];
102/// LaTeX's command definers: `\renewcommand{\thanks}[1]{}` (tufte-common.def:612).
103const COMMAND_DEFINERS: &[&str] = &[
104  "\\newcommand",
105  "\\renewcommand",
106  "\\providecommand",
107  "\\DeclareRobustCommand",
108];
109
110/// The control sequences a setter's body stores argument `#<arg>` in, when the body only makes definitions: each a
111/// pure store of the argument (`\gdef\@name{#1}`, any [`STORE_DEFINERS`]) or a side definition — a `\def`-family
112/// definition, a `\let`, a LaTeX `\renewcommand`-family one, in any [`DEFINITION_FRAME`] — that typesets nothing:
113/// tufte-common.def:608-614's `\date` stores `\@date` and also `\protected@xdef`s `\thedate` in a group whose
114/// `\renewcommand{\thanks}[1]{}` swallows a `\thanks`. `None` when the body does anything else (a `\RequirePackage`,
115/// apa6.cls:780 `\journal`; a flag its title page reads, tADR2e.cls:450 `\global\keywordstrue`; any typesetting).
116fn pure_stores(body: &[Token], arg: usize) -> Option<Vec<Token>> {
117  let mut stores = Vec::new();
118  // (a store made inside `\begingroup` … `\endgroup` lasts only when it is global)
119  let (mut depth, mut global) = (0usize, false);
120  let mut i = 0;
121  while i < body.len() {
122    let t = body[i];
123    if t == T_CS!("\\begingroup") {
124      depth += 1;
125    } else if t == T_CS!("\\endgroup") {
126      depth = depth.checked_sub(1)?;
127    } else if t == T_CS!("\\global") {
128      global = true;
129    }
130    if t.get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE || cs_in(&t, DEFINITION_FRAME) {
131      i += 1;
132      continue;
133    }
134    let is_global = std::mem::take(&mut global)
135      || t.with_str(|s| {
136        matches!(
137          s,
138          "\\gdef" | "\\xdef" | "\\protected@xdef" | "\\g@addto@macro"
139        )
140      });
141    if t.get_catcode() != Catcode::CS {
142      return None;
143    }
144    if t == T_CS!("\\let") {
145      // `\let\cs = token`
146      let mut k = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
147      body.get(k).filter(|c| c.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS)?;
148      k = skip_spaces(body, k + 1);
149      if body.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("=")) {
150        k = skip_spaces(body, k + 1);
151      }
152      body.get(k)?;
153      i = k + 1;
154      continue;
155    }
156    if cs_in(&t, COMMAND_DEFINERS) {
157      // `\renewcommand*{\cs}[n][default]{body}` or `\renewcommand\cs…`
158      let mut k = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
159      if body.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("*")) {
160        k = skip_spaces(body, k + 1);
161      }
162      if body
163        .get(k)
164        .is_some_and(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN)
165      {
166        let past = tokens_group_end(body, k)?;
167        (past == k + 3 && body[k + 1].get_catcode() == Catcode::CS).then_some(())?;
168        k = past;
169      } else {
170        body.get(k).filter(|c| c.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS)?;
171        k += 1;
172      }
173      for _ in 0..2 {
174        k = skip_spaces(body, k);
175        if body.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("[")) {
176          k += body[k..].iter().position(|b| *b == T_OTHER!("]"))? + 1;
177        }
178      }
179      i = tokens_group_end(body, skip_spaces(body, k))?;
180      continue;
181    }
182    if !cs_in(&t, STORE_DEFINERS) {
183      return None;
184    }
185    // `\def\cs<parameter text>{body}`
186    let k = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
187    let target = *body.get(k).filter(|c| c.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS)?;
188    let mut open = k + 1;
189    while body.get(open).is_some_and(|b| {
190      matches!(
191        b.get_catcode(),
192        Catcode::PARAM | Catcode::ARG | Catcode::OTHER
193      )
194    }) {
195      open += 1;
196    }
197    let past = tokens_group_end(body, open)?;
198    let stored = &body[open + 1..past - 1];
199    if open == k + 1
200      && (depth == 0 || is_global)
201      && stored.len() == 1
202      && stored[0].get_catcode() == Catcode::ARG
203      && stored[0].with_str(|s| s == arg.to_string())
204    {
205      stores.push(target);
206    }
207    i = past;
208  }
209  Some(stores)
210}
211
212/// The store a setter `\name` keeps its argument in, `@affil` for `\@affil`; its own `\@name` unless rule (a) found
213/// another ([`reroute_raw_class_stores`]).
214fn store_of(name: &str) -> String {
215  let store = lookup_string(&s!("lx_store_cs_{name}"));
216  if store.is_empty() {
217    s!("@{name}")
218  } else {
219    store
220  }
221}
222
223/// An argument read whole, not up to a delimiter (`\def\x#1\par{…}` reads `#1` up to `\par`).
224fn undelimited(param: &Parameter) -> bool { !with(param.spec, |spec| spec.contains("Until")) }
225
226/// Does the store's frontmatter API annotate a creator (`\lx@annotate@frontmatter{ltx:creator}…`)?
227/// Such an annotation is placed only on a queued creator.
228fn annotates_a_creator(name: &str) -> bool {
229  STORE_SETTERS.iter().any(|(n, api)| {
230    *n == name
231      && [
232        "\\lx@add@store@affiliations",
233        "\\lx@add@email",
234        "\\lx@add@address",
235        "\\lx@add@orcid",
236      ]
237      .iter()
238      .any(|creator_api| api.starts_with(creator_api))
239  })
240}
241
242/// Does the store's frontmatter API make a creator of its own (an editor, a speaker)? Read with the
243/// creator-annotating stores at `\maketitle`, after them, so their annotations stay the authors'.
244fn creates_a_creator(name: &str) -> bool {
245  STORE_SETTERS.iter().any(|(n, api)| {
246    *n == name
247      && ["\\lx@add@editor", "\\lx@add@creator"]
248        .iter()
249        .any(|c| api.starts_with(c))
250  })
251}
252
253/// A store read at `\maketitle` rather than as it is set.
254fn read_at_maketitle(name: &str) -> bool { annotates_a_creator(name) || creates_a_creator(name) }
255
256/// A setter of one argument: a macro storing `#1`, or an `[optional]{mandatory}` one storing `#2` (bfhlayout.sty:738
257/// `\providecommand*{\institute}[2][]{\def\@institute{#2}}`) — its body, the stored argument's number and the
258/// control sequences its body stores that argument in ([`pure_stores`]).
259fn setter_stores(cs: &Token) -> Result<Option<(Tokens, usize, Vec<Token>)>> {
260  Ok(
261    setter_body(cs)?.and_then(|(body, arg)| {
262      pure_stores(body.unlist_ref(), arg).map(|stores| (body, arg, stores))
263    }),
264  )
265}
266
267/// The body of a one-argument setter ([`setter_stores`]) and the number of the argument it stores.
268fn setter_body(cs: &Token) -> Result<Option<(Tokens, usize)>> {
269  let Some(defn) = lookup_definition(cs)? else {
270    return Ok(None);
271  };
272  let stored_arg = defn.get_parameters().and_then(|p| {
273    let params = p.get_parameters();
274    match params.as_slice() {
275      [only] if !only.optional && undelimited(only) => Some(1),
276      [first, second] if first.optional && !second.optional && undelimited(second) => Some(2),
277      _ => None,
278    }
279  });
280  let (Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)), Some(arg)) = (defn.get_expansion(), stored_arg) else {
281    return Ok(None);
282  };
283  Ok(Some((body.clone(), arg)))
284}
285
286/// [`setter_stores`] of a table setter, which may also print a mark before its pure stores — a mark of the forms a
287/// marked line leads with ([`leading_mark`]: `\textsuperscript{*}`, `$^{*}$`, `\inst{1}`) that does not use the
288/// argument: astlb.cls:78 `\def\email#1{\textsuperscript{*}\gdef\@email{#1}}` (1202.2889; the pazh/astl family's
289/// pazhb, pazhb_eng, pazha2, pazhastl copies), whose store only its `\endabstract`'s `\maketitle` reads, which the
290/// kernel's locked `{abstract}` never runs, so the e-mail was lost (0 errors; Perl gives the paper OmniBus's `\email`).
291/// The rerouted setter keeps the whole body ([`reroute_raw_class_stores`]): the mark still prints where it is called.
292fn table_setter_stores(cs: &Token) -> Result<Option<(Tokens, usize, Vec<Token>)>> {
293  let Some((body, arg)) = setter_body(cs)? else {
294    return Ok(None);
295  };
296  let tokens = body.unlist_ref();
297  let stores = pure_stores(tokens, arg).or_else(|| {
298    let (_, Some(past)) = leading_mark(tokens)? else {
299      return None;
300    };
301    if tokens[..past]
302      .iter()
303      .any(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::ARG)
304    {
305      return None;
306    }
307    pure_stores(&tokens[past..], arg).filter(|stores| !stores.is_empty())
308  });
309  Ok(stores.map(|stores| (body, arg, stores)))
310}
311
312/// The title-page code of the raw class — what its refused `\maketitle` (`\lx@dropped@maketitle`, state.rs) and
313/// its `\@maketitle` run, and every macro a raw file defined that only they call, transitively — and the readers
314/// of each macro: for every macro a raw file defined (`DefinitionOrigin::File`: the class and what it loaded, as
315/// the class ends; the scan is bounded to them), the control sequences it reads ([`reads_of`]). A macro two
316/// title-page macros call is title-page code; one any other macro calls too is not.
317struct ClassReaders {
318  /// The title-page code, by name.
319  title_page:        FxHashSet<SymStr>,
320  /// The title-page code with the class's own kernel fields among its roots (`\@title`, `\@author`, `\@date`, which
321  /// LaTeX's `\@maketitle` prints): [`survey_off_table_stores`].
322  title_page_fields: FxHashSet<SymStr>,
323  /// Every macro a raw file defined that code which never runs here reaches: the title page's roots, the refused
324  /// definitions (`\lx@dropped@<name>`, state.rs: a locked kernel definition the raw file redefined — astlb.cls:176's
325  /// `\endabstract`, whose `\maketitle` and e-mail footnote the kernel's locked `{abstract}` never reaches, 1202.2889)
326  /// and the page styles (`\ps@…`, `\@oddhead`, …, a fancyhdr style: page furniture no conversion sets)
327  /// ([`ClassReaders::read_where_nothing_runs`]).
328  never_runs:        FxHashSet<SymStr>,
329  /// What the class prints: every macro a raw file defined that the title-page roots — of any origin, a binding's
330  /// `\@maketitle` too (titling_sty.rs's, which `\lx@deposit@maketitle` runs) — or the class's page styles (`\ps@…`,
331  /// `\@oddhead`, …) reach, and what those roots read themselves.
332  printed:           FxHashSet<SymStr>,
333  /// For each control sequence, the macros a raw file defined that read it (itself, when it reads itself).
334  readers:           HashMap<SymStr, Vec<SymStr>>,
335}
336
337/// Does `code` step a counter or print a mark — a superscript, math, `\textsuperscript`, a counter's value
338/// (`\the…`)? What makes a separator a class's numbering of its entries ([`ClassReaders::separators_redefined`]).
339fn numbers_or_marks(code: &[Token]) -> bool {
340  code.iter().any(|t| {
341    matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::SUPER | Catcode::MATH)
342      || (t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS
343        && t.with_str(|s| {
344          matches!(
345            s,
346            "\\stepcounter"
347              | "\\refstepcounter"
348              | "\\addtocounter"
349              | "\\advance"
350              | "\\textsuperscript"
351              | "\\the"
352          ) || s.starts_with("\\the")
353        }))
354  })
355}
