357/// The definers whose next control sequence is written, not read.
358const WRITERS: &[&str] = &[
359  "\\def",
360  "\\gdef",
361  "\\edef",
362  "\\xdef",
363  "\\protected@xdef",
364  "\\protected@edef",
365  "\\let",
366  "\\g@addto@macro",
367  "\\newcommand",
368  "\\renewcommand",
369  "\\providecommand",
370  "\\DeclareRobustCommand",
371];
372
373/// The control sequences `body` reads: each one not written by a definer before it ([`WRITERS`]), and those named
374/// by characters — `\@ifundefined{@affil}` (apa6.cls:793), `\@nameuse{X}`, `\csname X\endcsname` — as `\X`.
375fn reads_of(body: &[Token]) -> Vec<Token> { reads_of_branches(body, false) }
376
377/// The constant switch `t` is, with the branch it takes: an `\ifX` whose meaning is `\iftrue` or `\iffalse`, as
378/// `\newif` and kvoptions make them, `\iftrue`/`\iffalse` themselves included.
379fn constant_switch(t: &Token) -> Option<bool> {
380  match lookup_meaning(t) {
381    Some(Stored::Conditional(defn)) if defn.cs == T_CS!("\\iftrue") => Some(true),
382    Some(Stored::Conditional(defn)) if defn.cs == T_CS!("\\iffalse") => Some(false),
383    _ => None,
384  }
385}
386
387/// Is `t` a conditional — one that opens a `\fi` level, not `\else`, `\or`, `\fi` or the prefix `\unless`?
388fn opens_a_conditional(t: &Token) -> bool {
389  match lookup_meaning(t) {
390    Some(Stored::Conditional(defn)) => !["\\fi", "\\else", "\\or", "\\unless"]
391      .into_iter()
392      .any(|cs| defn.cs == T_CS!(cs)),
393    _ => false,
394  }
395}
396
397/// The branch an `\if` or `\ifx` at `body[at]` takes when its operands are known without running code, and the index
398/// past them — the fallback of the speculative title-page run (`before_title_page_runs`), which lets TeX decide: `\ifx`
399/// of two tokens that are the same, both undefined, or both parameterless macros of the same body; `\if` of the two
400/// character tokens that the tokens after it give when each parameterless macro a raw file defined is expanded (tex.web
401/// §506) — a class's home-made boolean, sigplanconf.cls:87-90 `\def\@true{TT}`/`\def\@false{FL}`
402/// and :1118 `\if \@copyrightwanted \@copyrightspace \fi` (1603.03011). `None` for anything else.
403fn evaluated_switch(body: &[Token], at: usize) -> Option<(bool, usize)> {
404  let Some(Stored::Conditional(defn)) = lookup_meaning(body.get(at)?) else {
405    return None;
406  };
407  let parameterless = |t: &Token| -> Option<Option<Vec<Token>>> {
408    match lookup_meaning(t) {
409      None => Some(None),
410      Some(Stored::Expandable(d))
411        if d
412          .get_parameters()
413          .is_none_or(|p| p.get_parameters().is_empty()) =>
414      {
415        match d.get_expansion() {
416          None => Some(Some(Vec::new())),
417          Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(b)) => Some(Some(b.unlist_ref().to_vec())),
418          Some(_) => None,
419        }
420      },
421      _ => None,
422    }
423  };
424  if defn.cs == T_CS!("\\ifx") {
425    let (a, b) = (body.get(at + 1)?, body.get(at + 2)?);
426    if a == b {
427      return Some((true, at + 3));
428    }
429    if a.get_catcode() != Catcode::CS || b.get_catcode() != Catcode::CS {
430      return None;
431    }
432    return Some((parameterless(a)? == parameterless(b)?, at + 3));
433  }
434  if defn.cs != T_CS!("\\if") {
435    return None;
436  }
437  let is_char = |t: &Token| matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER);
438  let mut pending: VecDeque<Token> = VecDeque::new();
439  let mut operands: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
440  let mut next = at + 1;
441  // (a bounded number of expansions: `\def\x{\x}` never gives an operand)
442  let mut expansions = 0;
443  while operands.len() < 2 {
444    let t = match pending.pop_front() {
445      Some(t) => t,
446      None => {
447        let t = *body.get(next)?;
448        next += 1;
449        t
450      },
451    };
452    if is_char(&t) {
453      operands.push(t);
454      continue;
455    }
456    expansions += 1;
457    if t.get_catcode() != Catcode::CS || file_body(&t).is_none() || expansions > 32 {
458      return None;
459    }
460    for t in parameterless(&t)??.into_iter().rev() {
461      pending.push_front(t);
462    }
463  }
464  // (what an expansion leaves after the two operands would be typeset in the branch: not known here)
465  pending.is_empty().then(|| {
466    (
467      operands[0].with_str(|a| operands[1].with_str(|b| a == b)),
468      next,
469    )
470  })
471}
472
473/// Does `t` write the control sequence after it — a definer of [`WRITERS`], or `\newif` — rather than read it?
474fn writes_next(t: &Token) -> bool { cs_in(t, WRITERS) || *t == T_CS!("\\newif") }
475
476/// The positions of `body` that are data rather than code: the name a definer writes ([`writes_next`]), and the
477/// source of a `\let` (`\let\ifX\iftrue`, `\let\a=\b`). A conditional there opens no level.
478fn data_positions(body: &[Token]) -> Vec<bool> {
479  let mut data = vec![false; body.len()];
480  let mut i = 0;
481  while i < body.len() {
482    let t = body[i];
483    i += 1;
484    if !matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::CS | Catcode::ACTIVE) || !writes_next(&t) {
485      continue;
486    }
487    let is_let = t == T_CS!("\\let") || t == T_CS!("\\futurelet");
488    // (`\renewcommand{\cs}`: the brace before the written name)
489    let mut written = 0;
490    while i < body.len() && written < if is_let { 2 } else { 1 } {
491      let next = body[i];
492      let skip = matches!(next.get_catcode(), Catcode::BEGIN | Catcode::SPACE)
493        || (is_let
494          && written == 1
495          && next.get_catcode() == Catcode::OTHER
496          && next.with_str(|s| s == "="));
497      if !skip {
498        data[i] = true;
499        written += 1;
500      }
501      i += 1;
502    }
503  }
504  data
505}
506
507/// [`reads_of`]; with `live`, a branch a constant switch does not take is not read ([`constant_switch`], `\unless`
508/// inverting it), and a conditional written as data opens no level ([`data_positions`]): the title page a copy's
509/// switches leave ([`copy_title_page_prints`]).
510fn reads_of_branches(body: &[Token], live: bool) -> Vec<Token> {
511  let named = |from: usize, stop: &dyn Fn(&Token) -> bool| -> Option<(Token, usize)> {
512    let mut name = String::new();
513    let mut k = from;
514    while let Some(t) = body.get(k) {
515      if stop(t) {
516        return Some((T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}")), k + 1));
517      }
518      if !matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER) {
519        return None;
520      }
521      t.with_str(|s| name.push_str(s));
522      k += 1;
523    }
524    None
525  };
526  let data = if live {
527    data_positions(body)
528  } else {
529    Vec::new()
530  };
531  let mut out = Vec::new();
532  let mut written = false;
533  let mut i = 0;
534  while i < body.len() {
535    let t = body[i];
536    i += 1;
537    if !matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::CS | Catcode::ACTIVE) {
538      // (`\renewcommand{\cs}`: the brace before the written name)
539      written &= t.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN || t.get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE;
540      continue;
541    }
542    if written {
543      written = false;
544      continue;
545    }
546    if cs_in(&t, WRITERS) {
547      written = true;
548      continue;
549    }
550    if live && !data[i - 1] {
551      // (`\unless\ifX`: the switch after it, inverted)
552      let (switch_at, inverted) = if t == T_CS!("\\unless") {
553        (i, true)
554      } else {
555        (i - 1, false)
556      };
557      let switch = body
558        .get(switch_at)
559        .filter(|_| !data[switch_at])
560        .and_then(|t| {
561          constant_switch(t)
562            .map(|taken| (taken, switch_at + 1))
563            .or_else(|| evaluated_switch(body, switch_at))
564        });
565      if let Some((taken, start)) = switch {
566        // the switch's `\else` and `\fi`, at its own depth
567        let (mut depth, mut else_at, mut fi_at) = (0usize, None, None);
568        for (k, token) in body.iter().enumerate().skip(start) {
569          if data[k] || !matches!(token.get_catcode(), Catcode::CS | Catcode::ACTIVE) {
570            continue;
571          }
572          match lookup_meaning(token) {
573            Some(Stored::Conditional(defn)) if defn.cs == T_CS!("\\fi") => {
574              if depth == 0 {
575                fi_at = Some(k);
576                break;
577              }
578              depth -= 1;
579            },
580            Some(Stored::Conditional(defn)) if defn.cs == T_CS!("\\else") => {
581              if depth == 0 && else_at.is_none() {
582                else_at = Some(k);
583              }
584            },
585            _ if opens_a_conditional(token) => depth += 1,
586            _ => {},
587          }
588        }
589        if let Some(fi_at) = fi_at {
590          let branch = if taken != inverted {
591            &body[start..else_at.unwrap_or(fi_at)]
592          } else {
593            else_at.map_or(&body[fi_at..fi_at], |at| &body[at + 1..fi_at])
594          };
595          out.extend(reads_of_branches(branch, live));
596          i = fi_at + 1;
597          continue;
598        }
599      }
600    }
601    if (t == T_CS!("\\@ifundefined") || t == T_CS!("\\@nameuse"))
602      && body
603        .get(i)
604        .is_some_and(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN)
605      && let Some((cs, past)) = named(i + 1, &|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::END)
606    {
607      out.push(t);
608      out.push(cs);
609      i = past;
610      continue;
611    }
612    if t == T_CS!("\\csname")
613      && let Some((cs, past)) = named(i, &|b| *b == T_CS!("\\endcsname"))
614    {
615      out.push(cs);
616      i = past;
617      continue;
618    }
619    out.push(t);
620  }
621  out
622}
623
624/// Is `name` a page style's or a running head's (`\ps@…`, `\@oddhead`, …), which prints as the title page does? Not a
625/// fancyhdr style ([`fancyhdr_page_style`]): its fields are page furniture, whose class defaults the frontmatter does
626/// not take — nbdt-article.cls:292-325's `firstpage` foot prints its `\@jname` default "Neurons, Behavior, Data Analysis
627/// and Theory" only under a `\@paperreceived` the paper leaves empty (2211.12591) —; only a printed token register
628/// reads one ([`ClassReaders::printed_register`]).
629fn page_style_name(name: &str) -> bool {
630  (name.starts_with("\\ps@") && !fancyhdr_page_style(name))
631    || matches!(
632      name,
633      "\\@oddhead" | "\\@evenhead" | "\\@oddfoot" | "\\@evenfoot"
634    )
635}
636
637/// Is `name` (`\ps@<style>`) a page style `\fancypagestyle` made (fancyhdr.sty:710's
638/// `\f@nch@ps@<style>-is-fancyhdr` marker; fancyhdr_sty.rs)?
639fn fancyhdr_page_style(name: &str) -> bool {
640  name.strip_prefix("\\ps@").is_some_and(|style| {
641    lookup_definition(&T_CS!(&s!("\\f@nch@ps@{style}-is-fancyhdr")))
642      .ok()
643      .flatten()
644      .is_some()
645  })
646}
647
648/// Does a stand-in's copy print the store `store` (`\@author`, `\@date`, `\@title`) — on its own title page (the
649/// `\maketitle`/`\@maketitle` it defined, `state::copy_provides_title_pages`) or in a page style a raw file defined, and
650/// every macro they reach that the copy (its own version of a name the binding owns, `copy_provides_overlaid_meaning`)
651/// or another raw file defined, read as `ClassReaders` reads a raw class (`reads_of`: `\csname…\endcsname`,
652/// `\@nameuse`), under the switches it set (a branch of a `\newif`/kvoptions flag that is now false is not printed,
653/// `reads_of_branches`)? A title-page name it reaches with neither a copy's nor a raw file's meaning is the
654/// binding's, which prints the frontmatter: the copy's `\renewcommand\maketitle{\begingroup\@maketitle\endgroup}`.
655/// `None` when no copy defined a title page (the binding's prints the frontmatter). atlasdoc.cls (2019, 1902.05892)
656/// prints `\@date` only in its `\ATLAS@TitleHeader` (:567, under `\ifADOC@datetop`), which its `\maketitle` (:716)
657/// calls only without `cernpreprint`; `\@author` under the title.
658pub fn copy_title_page_prints(store: &Token) -> Option<bool> {
659  let roots = copy_provides_title_pages();
660  let id = roots.first()?.0;
661  let target = store.get_cs_name();
662  let from_a_file = |stored: &Stored| match stored {
663    Stored::Expandable(defn) => defn.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File,
664    _ => false,
665  };
666  let mut work: Vec<(u32, Tokens)> = roots;
667  for (key, stored) in current_meanings_where(from_a_file) {
668    if let Stored::Expandable(defn) = stored
669      && let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion()
670      && with(key, page_style_name)
671    {
672      work.push((id, body.clone()));
673    }
674  }
675  let mut seen: FxHashSet<SymStr> = FxHashSet::default();
676  while let Some((id, body)) = work.pop() {
677    for cs in reads_of_branches(body.unlist_ref(), true) {
678      let key = cs.get_cs_name();
679      if key == target {
680        return Some(true);
681      }
682      if !seen.insert(key) {
683        continue;
684      }
685      let meaning =
686        copy_provides_overlaid_meaning(id, &cs).or_else(|| lookup_meaning(&cs).filter(from_a_file));
687      match meaning {
688        Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) => {
689          if let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(callee)) = defn.get_expansion() {
690            work.push((id, callee.clone()));
691          }
692        },
693        None if cs == T_CS!("\\maketitle") || cs == T_CS!("\\@maketitle") => return Some(true),
694        _ => {},
695      }
696    }
697  }
698  Some(false)
699}
700
701/// The label a class's title page prints right before a store, as read off its code ([`ClassReaders::label_in`]).
702struct StoreLabel {
703  /// The title-page macro it is in.
704  reader:      String,
705  /// The tokens the label takes up there (balanced; the store itself with them when the label is in the store's group).
706  part:        Tokens,
707  /// The label's text ([`LabelText`]).
708  text:        Tokens,
709  /// Is it in the store's own group, which is the argument of a control sequence (not a font command's)?
710  in_argument: bool,
711}
712
713impl ClassReaders {
714  /// One pass over the raw files' macros builds the caller index; the title page then grows from its roots along the
715  /// callees of each macro it takes in, a macro joining once every other caller of it has (a worklist: each macro's
716  /// callees are visited once, when it joins).
717  fn survey() -> Self {
718    let mut readers: HashMap<SymStr, Vec<SymStr>> = HashMap::default();
719    let mut callees: HashMap<SymStr, Vec<SymStr>> = HashMap::default();
720    let from_a_file = |stored: &Stored| match stored {
721      Stored::Expandable(defn) => defn.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File,
722      _ => false,
723    };
724    for (key, stored) in current_meanings_where(from_a_file) {
725      let Stored::Expandable(defn) = stored else {
726        continue;
727      };
728      let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion() else {
729        continue;
730      };
731      // (the meaning table's key is the name: a control sequence's when it starts with a backslash)
732      if !with(key, |k| k.starts_with('\\')) {
733        continue;
734      }
735      let caller = key;
736      let body = body.unlist_ref();
737      let mut seen = FxHashSet::default();
738      let called = callees.entry(caller).or_default();
739      for read in reads_of(body) {
740        let callee = read.get_cs_name();
741        if seen.insert(callee) {
742          readers.entry(callee).or_default().push(caller);
743          called.push(callee);
744        }
745      }
746    }
747    const ROOTS: [&str; 3] = [
748      "\\lx@dropped@maketitle",
749      "\\@maketitle",
750      "\\lx@dropped@@maketitle",
751    ];
752    let mut printed: FxHashSet<SymStr> = FxHashSet::default();
753    let mut reached: Vec<SymStr> = Vec::new();
754    for root in ROOTS {
755      let root = T_CS!(root);
756      let name = root.get_cs_name();
757      if callees.contains_key(&name) {
758        reached.push(name);
759      } else if let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(&root)
760        && let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion()
761      {
762        for read in reads_of(body.unlist_ref()) {
763          reached.push(read.get_cs_name());
764        }
765      }
766    }
767    // The page styles print too: the running heads and feet a class defines (gGAF2e.cls:1085-1099 `\ps@plain`'s head
768    // prints its `\@jname` default, "Geophysical and Astrophysical Fluid Dynamics": 2304.14584, 2512.18461) — not a
769    // fancyhdr style's fields ([`page_style_name`]).
770    reached.extend(
771      callees
772        .keys()
773        .copied()
774        .filter(|cs| with(*cs, page_style_name)),
775    );
776    while let Some(cs) = reached.pop() {
777      if printed.insert(cs) {
778        reached.extend(callees.get(&cs).into_iter().flatten().copied());
779      }
780    }
781    let grow = |roots: &[&str]| -> FxHashSet<SymStr> {
782      let mut title_page: FxHashSet<SymStr> = FxHashSet::default();
783      let mut joined: Vec<SymStr> = roots
784        .iter()
785        .map(|root| T_CS!(*root).get_cs_name())
786        .filter(|root| callees.contains_key(root) && title_page.insert(*root))
787        .collect();
788      while let Some(cs) = joined.pop() {
789        for callee in callees.get(&cs).into_iter().flatten().copied() {
790          if title_page.contains(&callee) || !callees.contains_key(&callee) {
791            continue;
792          }
793          let mut callers = readers
794            .get(&callee)
795            .into_iter()
796            .flatten()
797            .filter(|caller| **caller != callee)
798            .peekable();
799          if callers.peek().is_some() && callers.all(|caller| title_page.contains(caller)) {
800            title_page.insert(callee);
801            joined.push(callee);
802          }
803        }
804      }
805      title_page
806    };
807    let title_page = grow(&ROOTS);
808    let mut with_fields: Vec<&str> = ROOTS.to_vec();
809    with_fields.extend(KERNEL_FIELDS);
810    let title_page_fields = grow(&with_fields);
811    // Code that never runs here: the title page's roots, the refused definitions (of whatever origin the lock kept them
812    // in: cesj.sty's `\def\maketitle{\cesjmaketitle}`, physics0510010) and the page styles — their heads and feet are
813    // page furniture no conversion sets, and a fancyhdr style's selection runs nothing. Reached, not grown: a macro an
814    // unused variant also calls is still reached (cesj.sty:874's `\institutename`, called by its `\cesjmaketitle` and
815    // the alternative `\cejpmaketitle`).
816    let mut roots: Vec<SymStr> = ROOTS
817      .iter()
818      .map(|root| T_CS!(*root).get_cs_name())
819      .collect();
820    roots.extend(
821      current_meanings_where(|stored| matches!(stored, Stored::Expandable(_)))
822        .into_iter()
823        .map(|(key, _)| key)
824        .filter(|key| with(*key, |name| name.starts_with("\\lx@dropped@"))),
825    );
826    roots.extend(callees.keys().copied().filter(|cs| {
827      with(*cs, |name| {
828        page_style_name(name) || fancyhdr_page_style(name)
829      })
830    }));
831    let mut never_runs: FxHashSet<SymStr> = FxHashSet::default();
832    let mut reached: Vec<SymStr> = Vec::new();
833    for root in roots {
834      if callees.contains_key(&root) {
835        reached.push(root);
836      } else if let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) =
837        lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(&with(root, ToString::to_string)))
838        && let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion()
839      {
840        reached.extend(reads_of(body.unlist_ref()).iter().map(Token::get_cs_name));
841      }
842    }
843    while let Some(cs) = reached.pop() {
844      if never_runs.insert(cs) {
845        reached.extend(callees.get(&cs).into_iter().flatten().copied());
846      }
847    }
848    ClassReaders {
849      title_page,
850      title_page_fields,
851      never_runs,
852      printed,
853      readers,
854    }
855  }
856
857  /// Is `store` read where nothing runs here — by a macro that code which never runs reaches
858  /// ([`ClassReaders::never_runs`]: the title page's roots, a refused definition, a page style) —, or by no macro? A raw
859  /// package's setter's own store is rerouted only then (`store_setter_body`): rlj.sty:73's `\@keywords` is read only by
860  /// its `\makeCover`, which 2605.31044 never calls, so it prints nowhere; one only a `\printkeywords` the document
861  /// calls reads, or a body `\printhistory`, or an `\AtEndDocument` hook, prints there. Whether such a macro runs is not
862  /// read off the code: cesj.sty's alternative title page `\cejpmaketitle` (its `CEJP` option's `\maketitle`;
863  /// physics0510010 selects `CESJ`) reads `\@institute` as the refused `\cesjmaketitle` does, and the store is the
864  /// frontmatter's. A store no macro reads is printed, if at all, by code the file ran as it was read (a running head's
865  /// field set at its top level).
866  fn read_where_nothing_runs(&self, store: &Token) -> bool {
867    self
868      .readers
869      .get(&store.get_cs_name())
870      .is_none_or(|readers| readers.iter().any(|cs| self.never_runs.contains(cs)))
871  }
872
873  /// [`ClassReaders::read_only_by_the_title_page`], the class's own kernel fields counted as title-page code.
874  fn read_only_by_the_title_page_or_fields(&self, store: &Token) -> bool {
875    self
876      .readers
877      .get(&store.get_cs_name())
878      .is_some_and(|readers| {
879        !readers.is_empty() && readers.iter().all(|cs| self.title_page_fields.contains(cs))
880      })
881  }
882
883  /// Is `store` read by the title-page code and by nothing else a raw file defined?
884  fn read_only_by_the_title_page(&self, store: &Token) -> bool {
885    self
886      .readers
887      .get(&store.get_cs_name())
888      .is_some_and(|readers| {
889        !readers.is_empty() && readers.iter().all(|cs| self.title_page.contains(cs))
890      })
891  }
892
893  /// Does a macro a raw file defined that is not title-page code read `store` (itself aside, an accumulator reading its
894  /// own value)? The store is then that code's too (K11's rule (a), [`store_setter_body`]): letter.cls:155's `\address`
895  /// for `\opening`, a class's `\signature` printing `\@where` (displaced setters, [`displaced_store_stays_the_classs`]).
896  fn read_by_other_code(&self, store: &Token) -> bool {
897    let name = store.get_cs_name();
898    self.readers.get(&name).is_some_and(|readers| {
899      readers
900        .iter()
901        .any(|cs| *cs != name && !self.title_page.contains(cs))
902    })
903  }
904
905  /// Which of `separators` a title-page macro reading `store` defines anew to number or mark the entry after it
906  /// ([`ClassReaders::title_page_readers`]: a [`WRITERS`] definer before it, braced or not, whose new meaning steps a
907  /// counter or prints a mark, [`numbers_or_marks`]) — `\def\\{\par\stepcounter{inst}$^{\theinst}$}`, as caosp.sty's
908  /// `\institutename` does to `\and` —, so the store's entries are labelled by the class where it prints the store,
909  /// which the kernel's lines do not carry (`displaced_argument_in_doubt`). A separator still a line break (`\let\\
910  /// \newline`, `\def\\{, }`) separates the lines as the kernel's does.
911  fn separators_redefined(&self, store: &Token, separators: &[Token]) -> Vec<Token> {
912    let mut redefined = Vec::new();
913    for body in self.title_page_readers(store) {
914      let body = body.unlist_ref();
915      for (at, t) in body.iter().enumerate() {
916        if !(t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS && t.with_str(|s| WRITERS.contains(&s))) {
917          continue;
918        }
919        let next = skip_spaces(body, at + 1);
920        let (target, past) = match body.get(next) {
921          Some(open) if open.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN => (body.get(next + 1), next + 2),
922          other => (other, next + 1),
923        };
924        let Some(target) = target.filter(|target| separators.contains(target)) else {
925          continue;
926        };
927        // (its new meaning: a `\let`'s right side, else the group the definition's parameter text leads to)
928        let meaning: Vec<Token> = if *t == T_CS!("\\let") {
929          let k = skip_spaces(body, past);
930          let k = if body.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("=")) {
931            skip_spaces(body, k + 1)
932          } else {
933            k
934          };
935          match body.get(k).map(lookup_meaning) {
936            Some(Some(Stored::Expandable(defn))) => match defn.get_expansion() {
937              Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(tokens)) => tokens.unlist_ref().to_vec(),
938              _ => Vec::new(),
939            },
940            _ => Vec::new(),
941          }
942        } else {
943          let open = (past..body.len()).find(|&k| body[k].get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN);
944          match open.and_then(|open| tokens_group_end(body, open).map(|end| (open, end))) {
945            Some((open, end)) => body[open + 1..end - 1].to_vec(),
946            None => Vec::new(),
947          }
948        };
949        if numbers_or_marks(&meaning) && !redefined.contains(target) {
950          redefined.push(*target);
951        }
952      }
953    }
954    redefined
955  }
956
957  /// The title-page macros that read `store`, with their bodies.
958  fn title_page_readers(&self, store: &Token) -> Vec<Tokens> {
959    let Some(readers) = self.readers.get(&store.get_cs_name()) else {
960      return Vec::new();
961    };
962    readers
963      .iter()
964      .filter(|cs| self.title_page.contains(*cs))
965      .filter_map(
966        |cs| match lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(&with(*cs, |name| name.to_string()))) {
967          Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) => match defn.get_expansion() {
968            Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) => Some(body.clone()),
969            _ => None,
970          },
971          _ => None,
972        },
973      )
974      .collect()
975  }
976
977  /// The label the title page prints right before `store`, in the same line: in the first title-page macro reading it
978  /// (by name) where the code from the line's start — the macro's start, a `\par`, a `\\`/`\newline` (with its `*`
979  /// and `[<skip>]`), another parameterless value of a raw file's, or the open brace of the store's group — up to the
980  /// store is label material and layout only ([`label_only_begin_code`]): `Resumen: \@resumen`, `\par KW: \@keywords`.
981  /// When the store's own group holds only layout before it (`{\itshape \@abst}`), the line goes on before that group.
982  /// Static, from the code: the label is the class's only when TeX's run of the title page prints its text right before
983  /// the store (`verify_title_page_labels`).
984  fn title_page_label(&self, store: &Token) -> Option<StoreLabel> {
985    self.label_in(store, &self.title_page)
986  }
987
988  /// [`ClassReaders::title_page_label`] in the title-page code `code`: the class's own kernel fields counted
989  /// ([`ClassReaders::title_page_fields`]) for an off-table store (jfpc.cls:176-177 `{\footnotesize\it\sffamily Actes
990  /// JFPC \@lannee}` in its `\def\@title`, 1503.05530).
991  fn label_in(&self, store: &Token, code: &FxHashSet<SymStr>) -> Option<StoreLabel> {
992    let mut readers: Vec<String> = self
993      .readers
994      .get(&store.get_cs_name())?
995      .iter()
996      .filter(|cs| code.contains(*cs))
997      .map(|cs| with(*cs, ToString::to_string))
998      .collect();
999    readers.sort();
1000    for reader in readers {
1001      let Some(body) = parameterless_body(&T_CS!(&reader)) else {
1002        continue;
1003      };
1004      let body = body.unlist_ref();
1005      for (at, t) in body.iter().enumerate() {
1006        if t != store
1007          || body[..at]
1008            .iter()
1009            .rev()
1010            .find(|b| b.get_catcode() != Catcode::SPACE)
1011            .is_some_and(|b| cs_in(b, WRITERS))
1012          || in_unprinted_argument(body, at)
1013        {
1014          continue;
1015        }
1016        if let Some((start, end, text, in_argument)) = line_label_before(body, at)
1017          && Tokens::slice_is_balanced(&body[start..end])
1018        {
1019          return Some(StoreLabel {
1020            reader,
1021            part: Tokens::new(body[start..end].to_vec()),
1022            text,
1023            in_argument,
1024          });
1025        }
1026      }
1027    }
1028    None
1029  }
1030
1031  /// Is the default name of the date or classification `api` makes (`\lx@date@<role>@name`, `\lx@classification@name`:
1032  /// "Received~", "Classification:~") the word a raw file's macro prints right before `store` — its letters, case and a
1033  /// trailing colon aside ([`text_right_before`])? Where no label is read ([`labelled_api`]), the role's default stands
1034  /// only then (FIDELITY_CHARTER: a role's default stands only where the class prints that label; review rev95d-r2).
1035  fn prints_default_name_before(&self, store: &Token, api: &str) -> bool {
1036    let default = if api.starts_with("\\lx@add@classification") {
1037      T_CS!("\\lx@classification@name")
1038    } else {
1039      let Some(role) = api
1040        .split_once("role=")
1041        .and_then(|(_, rest)| rest.split([',', ']']).next())
1042      else {
1043        return false;
1044      };
1045      T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@date@{role}@name"))
1046    };
1047    let Some(word) = parameterless_body(&default).map(|body| {
1048      alphanumerics(
1049        &body
1050          .unlist_ref()
1051          .iter()
1052          .map(Token::to_string)
1053          .collect::<String>(),
1054      )
1055      .to_lowercase()
1056    }) else {
1057      return false;
1058    };
1059    if word.is_empty() {
1060      return false;
1061    }
1062    let Some(readers) = self.readers.get(&store.get_cs_name()) else {
1063      return false;
1064    };
1065    readers.iter().any(|cs| {
1066      let Some(body) = parameterless_or_any_body(&T_CS!(&with(*cs, ToString::to_string))) else {
1067        return false;
1068      };
1069      let body = body.unlist_ref();
1070      body.iter().enumerate().any(|(at, t)| {
1071        if t != store
1072          || body[..at]
1073            .iter()
1074            .rev()
1075            .find(|b| b.get_catcode() != Catcode::SPACE)
1076            .is_some_and(|b| cs_in(b, WRITERS))
1077        {
1078          return false;
1079        }
1080        let text = text_right_before(body, at).to_lowercase();
1081        let text =
1082          text.trim_end_matches(|c: char| c.is_whitespace() || matches!(c, ':' | ',' | ';'));
1083        text.strip_suffix(word.as_str()).is_some_and(|before| {
1084          !before
1085            .chars()
1086            .next_back()
1087            .is_some_and(char::is_alphanumeric)
1088        })
1089      })
1090    })
1091  }
1092
1093  /// Does the class print `store` — its title page or a page style ([`ClassReaders::printed`])? A store left at the
1094  /// class's default is printed only there, if at all: tlp.cls:697-728's `\@volume`, `\@doi`, `\@journal`
1095  /// placeholders ("TLP 1 (1): 1–000, January 20XX") are read by its `\@journal` running head, which the class's own
1096  /// `\@j@urnal` switch (:760-764) does not print without its `prodtf` option (1209.2617); lion-msc.cls:200's
1097  /// `\@affiliation` default is printed by its `\posttitle` (:244-275), which titling's `\@maketitle` reads; gGAF2e's
1098  /// `\@jname` by its `\ps@plain` head.
1099  fn read_by_the_title_page(&self, store: &Token) -> bool {
1100    let name = store.get_cs_name();
1101    self.printed.contains(&name)
1102      || self
1103        .readers
1104        .get(&name)
1105        .is_some_and(|readers| readers.iter().any(|cs| self.printed.contains(cs)))
1106  }
1107
1108  /// The page styles the class's title page selects: `\thispagestyle{<name>}` or `\pagestyle{<name>}` in its refused
1109  /// `\maketitle` or `\@maketitle` or in any printed macro ([`ClassReaders::printed`]) — googlecloud.cls:197/:216's
1110  /// `\maketitle` selects `firststyle` (2607.06973), cmu.cls:261/:285 (2608.26053).
1111  fn selected_page_styles(&self) -> FxHashSet<String> {
1112    let mut bodies: Vec<Tokens> = [
1113      "\\lx@dropped@maketitle",
1114      "\\@maketitle",
1115      "\\lx@dropped@@maketitle",
1116    ]
1117    .iter()
1118    .filter_map(|root| parameterless_or_any_body(&T_CS!(*root)))
1119    .collect();
1120    bodies.extend(
1121      self
1122        .printed
1123        .iter()
1124        .filter_map(|cs| parameterless_or_any_body(&T_CS!(&with(*cs, ToString::to_string)))),
1125    );
1126    let mut styles = FxHashSet::default();
1127    for body in &bodies {
1128      let body = body.unlist_ref();
1129      for (k, t) in body.iter().enumerate() {
1130        if !cs_in(t, &["\\thispagestyle", "\\pagestyle"]) {
1131          continue;
1132        }
1133        let open = skip_spaces(body, k + 1);
1134        if body
1135          .get(open)
1136          .is_some_and(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN)
1137          && let Some(past) = tokens_group_end(body, open)
1138        {
1139          styles.insert(Tokens::new(body[open + 1..past - 1].to_vec()).to_string());
1140        }
1141      }
1142    }
1143    styles
1144  }
1145
1146  /// The label a printed token register ([`toks_store`]) carries, when only printed code reads it
1147  /// ([`ClassReaders::printed`], or a fancyhdr page style, [`fancyhdr_page_style`]) and some of it prints the register's value — a `\the\X` that is no conditional's
1148  /// operand (`\if\relax\the\X\relax` only tests it: nvidiatechreport.cls's copy in 2507.09850 prints "* Equal
1149  /// contribution." instead) — in a macro the title page reaches or a page style it selects
1150  /// ([`ClassReaders::selected_page_styles`]): the text printed right before the value in its line
1151  /// ([`printed_text_before`]; "Corresponding author(s): " of googlecloud.cls:114, "*" of deepseek.cls:170), empty when
1152  /// there is none. `None` when the register is not such a store.
1153  fn printed_register(&self, register: &Token) -> Option<Vec<Token>> {
1154    let readers = self.readers.get(&register.get_cs_name())?;
1155    if readers.is_empty()
1156      || !readers
1157        .iter()
1158        .all(|cs| self.printed.contains(cs) || with(*cs, fancyhdr_page_style))
1159    {
1160      return None;
1161    }
1162    let selected = self.selected_page_styles();
1163    let mut names: Vec<String> = readers
1164      .iter()
1165      .map(|cs| with(*cs, ToString::to_string))
1166      .collect();
1167    names.sort();
1168    for name in names {
1169      if let Some(style) = name.strip_prefix("\\ps@")
1170        && !selected.contains(style)
1171      {
1172        continue;
1173      }
1174      let Some(body) = parameterless_or_any_body(&T_CS!(&name)) else {
1175        continue;
1176      };
1177      let body = body.unlist_ref();
1178      for (k, t) in body.iter().enumerate() {
1179        if *t != T_CS!("\\the") || body.get(skip_spaces(body, k + 1)) != Some(register) {
1180          continue;
1181        }
1182        // (an operand of a conditional's test, `\if\relax\the\X\relax`: not printed)
1183        let before: Vec<&Token> = body[..k]
1184          .iter()
1185          .rev()
1186          .filter(|b| b.get_catcode() != Catcode::SPACE)
1187          .take(2)
1188          .collect();
1189        let tested = |b: Option<&&Token>| b.is_some_and(|b| opens_a_conditional(b));
1190        if tested(before.first())
1191          || (before.first().is_some_and(|b| **b == T_CS!("\\relax")) && tested(before.get(1)))
1192        {
1193          continue;
1194        }
1195        return Some(printed_text_before(body, k));
1196      }
1197    }
1198    None
1199  }
1200
1201  /// How the title page prints `store` among the class's printed text, read off its code: is an occurrence glued to
1202  /// the class's text ([`glued_to_class_text`]), and which other values share its lines — in the innermost group
1203  /// around the store, between the line breaks around it ([`ends_a_line`]: `\par`, `\\`, `\newline`, `\break`,
1204  /// `\linebreak`, `\vskip`), each other `\@` macro not one of `carried`. A store printed in one line with a value
1205  /// the frontmatter does not carry is part of the class's line and stays with it: tlp.cls:627 `{\affilsize\it
1206  /// submitted \@submitted; revised \@revised; accepted \@accepted}`, where `\submitted` is no title-page setter of
1207  /// the table — handing on the other two left "submitted 30 November 2010; revised ; accepted" in the body
1208  /// (1209.2617); uiucthesis.cls:177 `for the degree of \@degree\ in \@dept\\`. Only printed text is a value (user
1209  /// ruling 2026-10-22, OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #662), decided by expansion ([`prints_as_a_value`]): a macro whose
1210  /// expansion is font, size or shape switches, spacing, rules or nothing ([`prints_text`]) is the line's
1211  /// typesetting, as in `{\@addrfont\@address\par}` with `\def\@addrfont{\itshape}` (review 68x r2 probe ka;
1212  /// bgteubner, umich-thesis, philosophersimprint). The other values are judged twice: as the class leaves them, when
1213  /// it is loaded (`reroute_raw_class_stores`), and with the document's, when `\maketitle` prints the title page
1214  /// (`lx_store_line_values_<name>`, [`harvest_stores`]): a store the class leaves empty, `\gdef\@submitted{}`, prints
1215  /// the document's `\submitted{1 May 2026}` there, which makes `{\itshape submitted \@submitted; e-mail
1216  /// \@email\par}` one line of the class's (review rev79a Q1). A value the line ends before is in another line:
1217  /// `\vbox{…\@jinfo\par\@author\par\@address\par}` (probe kb).
1218  fn store_lines(&self, store: &Token, carried: &dyn Fn(&Token) -> bool) -> StoreLines {
1219    let other = |t: &Token| {
1220      t != store
1221        && t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS
1222        && t.with_str(|s| s.starts_with("\\@"))
1223        && !carried(t)
1224    };
1225    let mut lines = StoreLines::default();
1226    for body in self.title_page_readers(store) {
1227      let body = body.unlist_ref();
1228      let furniture = running_head_arguments(body);
1229      let mut open: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
1230      for (k, t) in body.iter().enumerate() {
1231        // (a store a running head prints is in no line of the title page: preprint.cls's `\maketitle`
1232        // `\ihead{\@shortauthor:~\@shorttitle}`, 2512.04983, whose short title is the frontmatter's `<toctitle>`)
1233        if furniture.iter().any(|(from, to)| (*from..*to).contains(&k)) {
1234          continue;
1235        }
1236        // (a store written, ptptex.cls:110 `\gdef\@abst{}`, is not printed there)
1237        let written = || {
1238          body[..k]
1239            .iter()
1240            .rev()
1241            .find(|b| b.get_catcode() != Catcode::SPACE)
1242            .is_some_and(|b| cs_in(b, WRITERS))
1243        };
1244        match t.get_catcode() {
1245          Catcode::BEGIN => open.push(k),
1246          Catcode::END => {
1247            open.pop();
1248          },
1249          _ if t == store && !written() => {
1250            if glued_to_class_text(body, k, open.last().copied()) {
1251              lines.glued = true;
1252            }
1253            if let Some(&start) = open.last()
1254              && let Some(end) = tokens_group_end(body, start)
1255            {
1256              let from = body[start..k]
1257                .iter()
1258                .rposition(ends_a_line)
1259                .map_or(start, |b| start + b + 1);
1260              let to = body[k + 1..end]
1261                .iter()
1262                .position(ends_a_line)
1263                .map_or(end, |b| k + 1 + b);
1264              for value in body[from..to].iter().filter(|t| other(t)) {
1265                if !lines.others.contains(value) {
1266                  lines.others.push(*value);
1267                }
1268              }
1269            }
1270          },
1271          _ => {},
1272        }
1273      }
1274    }
1275    lines
1276  }
1277}
1278
1279/// The spans of `body` that set page furniture — the arguments of LaTeX's marks (`\markboth`, `\markright`,
1280/// `\@mkboth`), of fancyhdr's head and foot commands (`\fancyhead[<places>]{…}`, `\lhead{…}`, …), of
1281/// scrlayer-scrpage's (`\ihead[<plain>]{…}`, `\ohead*{…}`, …) and of titleps's (`\sethead[…][…][…]{…}{…}{…}`,
1282/// `\setfoot*{…}{…}{…}`, titleps.sty:420-433), and the bodies of the head and foot macros a definition there sets
1283/// (`{\def\@oddhead{\@shortauthor: \@shorttitle\hfil}}`, `\renewcommand{\@evenfoot}{…}`; ptptex.cls's `\maketitle`
1284/// head, review rev79a2 finding 1): the running heads and feet LaTeXML drops, printed on no line of the title page
1285/// ([`ClassReaders::store_lines`]), and no text a store there is glued to ([`glued_to_class_text`]).
1286fn running_head_arguments(body: &[Token]) -> Vec<(usize, usize)> {
1287  const HEADS: &[(&str, usize)] = &[
1288    ("\\markboth", 2),
1289    ("\\markright", 1),
1290    ("\\@mkboth", 2),
1291    ("\\fancyhead", 1),
1292    ("\\fancyfoot", 1),
1293    ("\\fancyhf", 1),
1294    ("\\lhead", 1),
1295    ("\\chead", 1),
1296    ("\\rhead", 1),
1297    ("\\lfoot", 1),
1298    ("\\cfoot", 1),
1299    ("\\rfoot", 1),
1300    ("\\ihead", 1),
1301    ("\\ohead", 1),
1302    ("\\ifoot", 1),
1303    ("\\ofoot", 1),
1304    ("\\lehead", 1),
1305    ("\\cehead", 1),
1306    ("\\rehead", 1),
1307    ("\\lohead", 1),
1308    ("\\cohead", 1),
1309    ("\\rohead", 1),
1310    ("\\lefoot", 1),
1311    ("\\cefoot", 1),
1312    ("\\refoot", 1),
1313    ("\\lofoot", 1),
1314    ("\\cofoot", 1),
1315    ("\\rofoot", 1),
1316    ("\\sethead", 3),
1317    ("\\setfoot", 3),
1318  ];
1319  let mut spans = Vec::new();
1320  for (i, t) in body.iter().enumerate() {
1321    if let Some(span) = head_definition_body(body, i) {
1322      spans.push(span);
1323      continue;
1324    }
1325    let Some((_, nargs)) = HEADS.iter().find(|(cs, _)| cs_in(t, &[cs])) else {
1326      continue;
1327    };
1328    let mut k = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
1329    if body.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("*")) {
1330      k = skip_spaces(body, k + 1);
1331    }
1332    // The span starts before any optional arguments: titleps's three are the even-page heads, scrlayer-scrpage's
1333    // `[<plain>]` and fancyhdr's old `\lhead[<even>]` are printed heads too (fancyhdr's `[<places>]` hold no stores).
1334    let start = k;
1335    match skip_optional_arguments(body, k) {
1336      Some(past) => k = past,
1337      None => continue,
1338    }
1339    for _ in 0..*nargs {
1340      match tokens_group_end(body, skip_spaces(body, k)) {
1341        Some(past) => k = past,
1342        None => break,
1343      }
1344    }
1345    if k > start {
1346      spans.push((start, k));
1347    }
1348  }
1349  spans
1350}
1351
1352/// The span of the body a definition at `body[i]` gives LaTeX's head or foot macro (`\@oddhead`, `\@evenhead`,
1353/// `\@oddfoot`, `\@evenfoot`): `\def`, `\gdef`, `\edef`, `\xdef` with any parameter text, `\renewcommand` with its
1354/// `*`, the name braced or not, and its `[<n>]` ([`running_head_arguments`]).
1355fn head_definition_body(body: &[Token], i: usize) -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
1356  const HEAD_MACROS: &[&str] = &["\\@oddhead", "\\@evenhead", "\\@oddfoot", "\\@evenfoot"];
1357  let t = body.get(i)?;
1358  let mut k = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
1359  if cs_in(t, &["\\def", "\\gdef", "\\edef", "\\xdef"]) {
1360    if !cs_in(body.get(k)?, HEAD_MACROS) {
1361      return None;
1362    }
1363    // (the parameter text, up to the body's brace)
1364    k = k
1365      + 1
1366      + body[k + 1..]
1367        .iter()
1368        .position(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN)?;
1369  } else if cs_in(t, &["\\renewcommand"]) {
1370    if body.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("*")) {
1371      k = skip_spaces(body, k + 1);
1372    }
1373    if cs_in(body.get(k)?, HEAD_MACROS) {
1374      k += 1;
1375    } else if body.get(k)?.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN
1376      && body.get(k + 1).is_some_and(|name| cs_in(name, HEAD_MACROS))
1377      && body
1378        .get(k + 2)
1379        .is_some_and(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::END)
1380    {
1381      k += 3;
1382    } else {
1383      return None;
1384    }
1385    k = skip_optional_arguments(body, skip_spaces(body, k))?;
1386  } else {
1387    return None;
1388  }
1389  Some((k, tokens_group_end(body, k)?))
1390}
1391
1392/// The index past the optional arguments (`[…]`, each with its braced groups whole) from `body[k]` on, spaces
1393/// skipped; `None` when one never closes.
1394fn skip_optional_arguments(body: &[Token], mut k: usize) -> Option<usize> {
1395  while body.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("[")) {
1396    let mut depth = 0usize;
1397    let close = body[k + 1..].iter().position(|b| {
1398      match b.get_catcode() {
1399        Catcode::BEGIN => depth += 1,
1400        Catcode::END => depth = depth.saturating_sub(1),
1401        _ => {},
1402      }
1403      depth == 0 && *b == T_OTHER!("]")
1404    })?;
1405    k = skip_spaces(body, k + 1 + close + 1);
1406  }
1407  Some(k)
1408}
1409
1410/// How the title page prints a store among the class's text ([`ClassReaders::store_lines`]).
1411#[derive(Default)]
1412struct StoreLines {
1413  /// Is an occurrence glued to the class's printed text ([`glued_to_class_text`])?
1414  glued:  bool,
1415  /// The other `\@` macros in its lines the frontmatter does not carry, values when they print text
1416  /// ([`prints_as_a_value`]).
1417  others: Vec<Token>,
1418}
1419
1420/// Is the macro `t` a value of a class's line: a parameterless macro of a raw file's or the document's whose expansion
1421/// prints text ([`prints_text`]), read with its current meaning — the class's default when the class is loaded, the
1422/// document's at `\maketitle`?
1423fn prints_as_a_value(t: &Token) -> bool {
1424  match lookup_meaning(t) {
1425    Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) => {
1426      matches!(
1427        defn.get_origin(),
1428        DefinitionOrigin::File | DefinitionOrigin::Document
1429      ) && defn
1430        .get_parameters()
1431        .is_none_or(|p| p.get_parameters().is_empty())
1432        && matches!(defn.get_expansion(), Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(value))
1433          if prints_text(value.unlist_ref(), PRINTS_TEXT_DEPTH))
1434    },
1435    _ => false,
1436  }
1437}
1438
1439/// Can the macro `t`, a value of a store's line ([`ClassReaders::store_lines`]) that prints nothing as the class leaves
1440/// it, print one when `\maketitle` judges it again ([`prints_as_a_value`])? Only a placeholder the document can fill:
1441/// undefined, `\relax` (which LaTeX finds undefined, `\@ifundefined`, `\newcommand`: `\let\@volume\relax`), or a
1442/// parameterless macro that expands to nothing (`\gdef\@submitted{}`, `\let\@shortauthor\empty`; review rev79a
1443/// Q1) — a store the class never defaulted still waits for `\maketitle`. The line's typesetting code is no
1444/// placeholder: a macro with parameters (`\@setfontsize`, `\@ifundefined`), a primitive, a register, or a font
1445/// macro (USG.cls:1590 `\smash{\@journalfont\@journal}`, whose `\journal` deferred for it, 2605.00042's pubnote
1446/// order; review rev79a2 finding 5).
1447fn may_become_a_value(t: &Token) -> bool {
1448  match lookup_meaning(t) {
1449    None => true,
1450    Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) => {
1451      defn
1452        .get_parameters()
1453        .is_none_or(|p| p.get_parameters().is_empty())
1454        && match defn.get_expansion() {
1455          // (an empty body is none)
1456          None => true,
1457          Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(value)) => value.unlist_ref().iter().all(|b| {
1458            matches!(
1459              b.get_catcode(),
1460              Catcode::SPACE | Catcode::BEGIN | Catcode::END
1461            ) || cs_in(b, &["\\relax", "\\@empty", "\\empty"])
1462          }),
1463          _ => false,
1464        }
1465    },
1466    meaning => meaning == lookup_meaning(&TOKEN_RELAX),
1467  }
1468}
1469
1470/// Is the occurrence of a store at `body[k]` glued to the class's printed text — the character token right before or
1471/// after it a letter or a digit, or a `.`, `/` or `-` inside a word (with a letter, a digit or another of them beyond
1472/// it), with no space, glue or line break between? The class's text and the store's then print as one word, which is
1473/// one value the frontmatter does not carry, and the line stays the class's: piparticle-final.cls:272 `{DOI:
1474/// 10.4279/PIP.\@doi}`, handed on, left "020001" as the paper's DOI (1002.3829, 1012.4507, 1503.03445, 1503.07722,
1475/// 1510.06950); ar-1col.cls:335 `https://doi.org/\@doi\endgraf` (1903.12215, 2004.12182, 2603.20393). Not glued: a
1476/// sentence's full stop after the store (`\enspace\@ACMCCS.}`, CSML.cls's shape, perfect_kernel_batch75a), a label's
1477/// `:` or a bracket (`doi:\@doi`, lms; `(\@pubyear`, mnras), an operand of `\if`, `\ifx` or `\ifcat` (`\if!\@subtitle`,
1478/// lipics-v2021, llncs), which TeX compares and does not print, and the first argument of `\href` (`open`, the
1479/// innermost group's brace: `\href{mailto:\@email}`, modernposter), a link's target.
1480fn glued_to_class_text(body: &[Token], k: usize, open: Option<usize>) -> bool {
1481  const CONDITIONALS: &[&str] = &["\\if", "\\ifx", "\\ifcat"];
1482  // (`Some(true)`: a letter or a digit; `Some(false)`: a `.`, `/` or `-`)
1483  let word_char = |t: Option<&Token>| -> Option<bool> {
1484    let t = t?;
1485    match t.get_catcode() {
1486      Catcode::LETTER => Some(true),
1487      Catcode::OTHER => t.with_str(|s| {
1488        let mut chars = s.chars();
1489        match (chars.next(), chars.next()) {
1490          (Some(c), None) if c.is_ascii_digit() => Some(true),
1491          (Some('.' | '/' | '-'), None) => Some(false),
1492          _ => None,
1493        }
1494      }),
1495      _ => None,
1496    }
1497  };
1498  let before = |j: usize| j.checked_sub(1).map(|i| &body[i]);
1499  let glued_before = match word_char(before(k)) {
1500    Some(true) => true,
1501    Some(false) => word_char(before(k - 1)).is_some(),
1502    None => false,
1503  } && !in_a_dimension(body, k);
1504  let glued_after = match word_char(body.get(k + 1)) {
1505    Some(true) => true,
1506    Some(false) => word_char(body.get(k + 2)).is_some(),
1507    None => false,
1508  };
1509  let operand = (k >= 1 && cs_in(&body[k - 1], CONDITIONALS))
1510    || (k >= 2 && cs_in(&body[k - 2], CONDITIONALS) && body[k - 1].get_catcode() != Catcode::SPACE);
1511  let link_target = open.is_some_and(|start| {
1512    body[..start]
1513      .iter()
1514      .rev()
1515      .find(|b| b.get_catcode() != Catcode::SPACE)
1516      .is_some_and(|b| *b == T_CS!("\\href"))
1517  });
1518  !operand && !link_target && (glued_before || glued_after)
1519}
1520
1521/// Is `body[k]` in or right after the dimension a skip, kern, penalty or register assignment reads (`\hskip.5em\@kword`,
1522/// jps-cp.cls; `\vskip 4ex\@subtitle`, jurabook.cls; `\hangindent 3.8pc\@keywords`, w-art.cls)? Its characters are no
1523/// printed text ([`glue_end`]).
1524fn in_a_dimension(body: &[Token], k: usize) -> bool {
1525  const READS_A_NUMBER: &[&str] = &[
1526    "\\penalty",
1527    "\\raise",
1528    "\\lower",
1529    "\\moveleft",
1530    "\\moveright",
1531  ];
1532  let Some(j) = body[..k]
1533    .iter()
1534    .rposition(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS)
1535  else {
1536    return false;
1537  };
1538  let t = &body[j];
1539  (cs_in(t, GLUE_SETTERS)
1540    || cs_in(t, READS_A_NUMBER)
1541    || matches!(lookup_meaning(t), Some(Stored::Register(_))))
1542    && glue_end(body, j + 1).is_some_and(|end| end >= k)
1543}
1544
1545/// Does `t` end the line TeX prints: `\par` (a blank line in the code is one; `\endgraf` is plain's `\par`), `\\`,
1546/// `\newline`, `\break` and `\linebreak`, an alignment's `\cr`, and the primitive `\vskip`, which in horizontal mode
1547/// ends the paragraph?
1548fn ends_a_line(t: &Token) -> bool {
1549  cs_in(t, &[
1550    "\\par",
1551    "\\endgraf",
1552    "\\\\",
1553    "\\newline",
1554    "\\break",
1555    "\\linebreak",
1556    "\\cr",
1557    "\\crcr",
1558    "\\vskip",
1559  ])
1560}
1561
1562/// How many macros deep [`prints_text`] reads a value's expansion; past it, a macro counts as printing.
1563const PRINTS_TEXT_DEPTH: usize = 6;
1564
1565/// Spacing, rules and breaks of TeX's and LaTeX's that print no text, taking no argument (besides [`layout`]).
1566const SPACING: &[&str] = &[
1567  "\\hfil",
1568  "\\hfill",
1569  "\\hss",
1570  "\\vfil",
1571  "\\vfill",
1572  "\\vss",
1573  "\\quad",
1574  "\\qquad",
1575  "\\enskip",
1576  "\\enspace",
1577  "\\thinspace",
1578  "\\,",
1579  "\\ ",
1580  "\\/",
1581  "\\strut",
1582  "\\null",
1583  "\\break",
1584  "\\endgraf",
1585  "\\cr",
1586  "\\crcr",
1587  "\\protect",
1588];
1589
1590/// Does `code` print text — a character, math, or a command not known to print nothing? What prints nothing is a
1591/// space, a group's braces, a font, size or shape switch (a command whose definition carries a font: LaTeX's
1592/// `\itshape`, `\large`, and [`layout`]), spacing ([`SPACING`], [`LAYOUT_ARGS`] with their arguments, [`GLUE_SETTERS`]
1593/// and a register with their glue), a rule (`\hrule`/`\vrule` with their `height`/`width`/`depth`, LaTeX's
1594/// `\rule[<raise>]{<width>}{<height>}`), a line break with its `*` and `[<skip>]`, and a parameterless macro whose
1595/// expansion prints nothing, read `depth` macros deep. Only printed text is a value of a class's line (user ruling
1596/// 2026-10-22; [`ClassReaders::store_lines`]).
1597fn prints_text(code: &[Token], depth: usize) -> bool {
1598  let mut k = 0;
1599  while k < code.len() {
1600    let t = code[k];
1601    k += 1;
1602    match t.get_catcode() {
1603      Catcode::SPACE | Catcode::BEGIN | Catcode::END => continue,
1604      Catcode::ACTIVE if t == T_ACTIVE!('~') => continue,
1605      Catcode::CS | Catcode::ACTIVE => {},
1606      _ => return true,
1607    }
1608    if cs_in(&t, &["\\\\", "\\newline", "\\linebreak"]) {
1609      // `\\*[<skip>]`, `\linebreak[<number>]`
1610      k = skip_spaces(code, k);
1611      if code.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("*")) {
1612        k = skip_spaces(code, k + 1);
1613      }
1614      if code.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("[")) {
1615        match code[k..].iter().position(|b| *b == T_OTHER!("]")) {
1616          Some(close) => k += close + 1,
1617          None => return true,
1618        }
1619      }
1620      continue;
1621    }
1622    if layout(&t) || cs_in(&t, SPACING) {
1623      continue;
1624    }
1625    if let Some((_, nargs)) = LAYOUT_ARGS.iter().find(|(cs, _)| cs_in(&t, &[cs])) {
1626      k = skip_spaces(code, k);
1627      if code.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("*")) {
1628        k += 1;
1629      }
1630      for _ in 0..*nargs {
1631        match tokens_group_end(code, skip_spaces(code, k)) {
1632          Some(past) => k = past,
1633          None => return true,
1634        }
1635      }
1636      continue;
1637    }
1638    if t == T_CS!("\\rule") {
1639      k = skip_spaces(code, k);
1640      if code.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("[")) {
1641        match code[k..].iter().position(|b| *b == T_OTHER!("]")) {
1642          Some(close) => k += close + 1,
1643          None => return true,
1644        }
1645      }
1646      for _ in 0..2 {
1647        match tokens_group_end(code, skip_spaces(code, k)) {
1648          Some(past) => k = past,
1649          None => return true,
1650        }
1651      }
1652      continue;
1653    }
1654    if cs_in(&t, &["\\hrule", "\\vrule"]) {
1655      // `\hrule height 0.4pt depth 0pt width \textwidth`
1656      loop {
1657        let j = skip_spaces(code, k);
1658        let keyword = ["height", "width", "depth"].into_iter().find(|keyword| {
1659          keyword.chars().enumerate().all(|(n, c)| {
1660            code.get(j + n).is_some_and(|b| {
1661              b.get_catcode() == Catcode::LETTER && b.with_str(|s| s.len() == 1 && s.starts_with(c))
1662            })
1663          })
1664        });
1665        let Some(keyword) = keyword else {
1666          break;
1667        };
1668        match glue_end(code, j + keyword.len()) {
1669          Some(past) => k = past,
1670          None => return true,
1671        }
1672      }
1673      continue;
1674    }
1675    if cs_in(&t, GLUE_SETTERS) || matches!(lookup_meaning(&t), Some(Stored::Register(_))) {
1676      // (a skip or a register's assignment prints nothing: `\vskip 12\p@`, `\parindent=0pt`; a register read some
1677      // other way — a `\chardef`'d character, a count set to a number — counts as printing)
1678      match glue_end(code, k) {
1679        Some(past) => k = past,
1680        None => return true,
1681      }
1682      continue;
1683    }
1684    if let Ok(Some(defn)) = lookup_definition(&t)
1685      && defn.declared_font().is_some()
1686      && defn
1687        .get_parameters()
1688        .is_none_or(|p| p.get_parameters().is_empty())
1689    {
1690      continue;
1691    }
1692    match parameterless_body(&t) {
1693      Some(expansion) if depth > 0 && !prints_text(expansion.unlist_ref(), depth - 1) => continue,
1694      _ => return true,
1695    }
1696  }
1697  false
1698}
1699
1700/// The class's setter `\name`, if it stores its argument for the title page ([`setter_stores`]): in its own
1701/// `\@name` (ptptex.cls:616 `\inst` → `\@inst`) — a raw package's, read by code which never runs here or by no
1702/// macro ([`ClassReaders::read_where_nothing_runs`]) —, or — rule (a) — in one other `\@X` that only the class's
1703/// title-page code reads ([`ClassReaders`]): apa6.cls:760 `\long\def\affiliation#1{\long\def\@affil{#1}}` and :779
1704/// `\authornote` → `\@acks`, read only by its `\maketitle` (:1126-1234, :1367-1466, :1571-1707) and the
1705/// `\check@author` it calls (:786-795). A store some other class macro reads stays raw: letter.cls:155 `\address`
1706/// fills `\fromaddress` for `\opening` (:221-237), afthesis.cls:535 `\addr@ss` its vita (:744-749) (sweep 93:
1707/// rerouting those left the readers without their value). A kernel-owned store (`\@date`) is not a class's.
1708/// Returns the body, the stored argument's number and the store.
1709fn store_setter_body(
1710  name: &str,
1711  package: bool,
1712  readers: &mut Option<ClassReaders>,
1713) -> Result<Option<(Tokens, usize, Token)>> {
1714  let Some((body, arg, stores)) = table_setter_stores(&T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}")))? else {
1715    return Ok(None);
1716  };
1717  let own = T_CS!(&s!("\\@{name}"));
1718  if stores.contains(&own) {
1719    // (a raw package's own `\@<name>` only when code that never runs here reads it, or no macro does: rerouted,
1720    // rlj.sty:73's `\@keywords`, read only by the `\makeCover` 2605.31044 comments out, was invented, and one a macro
1721    // the document calls prints was printed twice, review rev95d B1. A raw class's — and a package's its read loads —
1722    // as before: the class's title page reaches its stores along code the survey does not follow, bfhlayout.sty
1723    // :743-752's `\@titlefooterleft`, printed by a scrlayer layer of bfhthesis.cls's title page, reads `\@department` and
1724    // `\@institute` (DEMO-BFHThesis), and the test lost them; requiring every reader to be title-page code also lost
1725    // dmtcs.cls's keywords, printed by its `\@dokeywords` from the refused `\endabstract` and its `\noabstract`, 0905.1102)
1726    if package
1727      && !readers
1728        .get_or_insert_with(ClassReaders::survey)
1729        .read_where_nothing_runs(&own)
1730    {
1731      return Ok(None);
1732    }
1733    return Ok(Some((body, arg, own)));
1734  }
1735  let [store] = stores
1736    .iter()
1737    .filter(|t| t.with_str(|s| s.starts_with("\\@")))
1738    .copied()
1739    .collect::<Vec<_>>()[..]
1740  else {
1741    return Ok(None);
1742  };
1743  let class_owned = match lookup_meaning(&store) {
1744    None => true,
1745    Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) => defn.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File,
1746    Some(_) => false,
1747  };
1748  if !class_owned {
1749    return Ok(None);
1750  }
1751  let readers = readers.get_or_insert_with(ClassReaders::survey);
1752  Ok(
1753    readers
1754      .read_only_by_the_title_page(&store)
1755      .then_some((body, arg, store)),
1756  )
1757}
1758
1759/// A kernel field setter the raw class redefined — tufte-common.def:608-614 `\renewcommand{\date}[1]{\gdef\@date{#1}…
1760/// \protected@xdef\thedate{#1}…}` (2603.01440; 25 tufte papers) — is still that field's setter (K11): when it stores
1761/// its argument in the field's store among side definitions only ([`pure_stores`]), the class's body stays and the
1762/// store is handed to the frontmatter as the kernel's `\date` hands it (sect05.rs), so the side definitions are kept.
1763/// The kernel's `\title` and `\author` are locked; `\date` is not, and is not locked for this.
1764fn reroute_kernel_field_setters(cls: &str) -> Result<()> {
1765  const FIELDS: &[(&str, &str)] = &[(
1766    "date",
1767    r"\expandafter\lx@add@date@halved\expandafter{\@date}\expandafter\lx@date@after\expandafter{\@date}",
1768  )];
1769  for (name, handing) in FIELDS {
1770    let cs = T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"));
1771    let Some(defn) = lookup_definition(&cs)? else {
1772      continue;
1773    };
1774    if defn.get_origin() != DefinitionOrigin::File {
1775      continue;
1776    }
1777    let Some((body, 1, stores)) = setter_stores(&cs)? else {
1778      continue;
1779    };
1780    if !stores.contains(&T_CS!(&s!("\\@{name}"))) {
1781      continue;
1782    }
1783    let mut body = body.unlist();
1784    body.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(*handing).unlist());
1785    DefMacro!(cs, convert_latex_args(1, None)?, Tokens::new(body));
1786    Info!(
1787      "frontmatter",
1788      cls,
1789      s!("The raw class's \\{name} keeps its definitions and sets the frontmatter's {name}")
1790    );
1791  }
1792  Ok(())
1793}

1795/// The Frontmatter API's contact setters (OmniBus.cls.ltxml:77-94, the generic class Perl gives a paper whose class has
1796/// no binding) and the API call each is when a raw file's own definition of it sets its argument as a title-page line
1797/// ([`in_place_contact_setter`]). An address or affiliation printed where it is called stands under the names printed
1798/// above it: the authors new since the last such entry (`annotate=run`, `frontmatter_api.rs`), or those its marks
1799/// request; an e-mail line goes where the API's evidence puts each of its addresses (the author it spells, …), a
1800/// list written out one address each, as a class's address list is (`\lx@split@addresses`, OD #805): 1303.3004's
1801/// `\email{rnarayan@…, jmcclintock@…, atchekho@…}` is one address of each of its three authors. `ARG` the line; an
1802/// `[optional]{mandatory}` setter's optional argument is read by [`optional_contact`].
1803const IN_PLACE_CONTACT_SETTERS: &[(&str, &str)] = &[
1804  ("address", "\\lx@add@address[annotate=run]{ARG}"),
1805  ("affil", "\\lx@add@affiliation[annotate=run]{ARG}"),
1806  ("affiliation", "\\lx@add@affiliation[annotate=run]{ARG}"),
1807  ("institute", "\\lx@add@affiliation[annotate=run]{ARG}"),
1808  ("email", "\\lx@split@addresses{\\lx@add@email}{ARG}"),
1809];
1810
1811/// What ends a line or a paragraph, or starts a list item (`\begin{indented}\item[]`): a setter whose executed code has
1812/// one around its argument sets the argument as a line of its own. (Not an environment's `\begin`/`\end` as such: a
1813/// font environment, aptmod.cls:137 `\begin{footnotesize}Email address: #1\end{footnotesize}`, sets no line.)
1814const LINE_BREAKS: &[&str] = &[
1815  "\\par",
1816  "\\endgraf",
1817  "\\\\",
1818  "\\newline",
1819  "\\linebreak",
1820  "\\break",
1821  "\\vskip",
1822  "\\vspace",
1823  "\\smallskip",
1824  "\\medskip",
1825  "\\bigskip",
1826  "\\noindent",
1827  "\\item",
1828  "\\centerline",
1829  "\\leftline",
1830  "\\rightline",
1831  "\\cr",
1832  "\\crcr",
1833];
1834
1835/// The control sequences `body` writes — the next control sequence after a definer ([`WRITERS`]), past the
1836/// `\expandafter`s of an append (`\expandafter\def\expandafter\@aabuffer\expandafter{\@aabuffer …}`) — each with
1837/// whether the text it writes holds argument `#<arg>` (the definer's next group, or `\g@addto@macro`'s second
1838/// argument).
1839fn written_stores(body: &[Token], arg: Option<usize>) -> Vec<(Token, bool)> {
1840  let holds_arg = |text: &[Token]| {
1841    arg.is_some_and(|arg| {
1842      text
1843        .iter()
1844        .any(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::ARG && t.with_str(|s| s == arg.to_string()))
1845    })
1846  };
1847  let mut out = Vec::new();
1848  for (i, t) in body.iter().enumerate() {
1849    if !cs_in(t, WRITERS) || *t == T_CS!("\\let") {
1850      continue;
1851    }
1852    let mut k = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
1853    while body.get(k) == Some(&T_CS!("\\expandafter")) {
1854      k = skip_spaces(body, k + 1);
1855    }
1856    // (`\newcommand{\x}`: the braced name)
1857    if body
1858      .get(k)
1859      .is_some_and(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN)
1860    {
1861      k = skip_spaces(body, k + 1);
1862    }
1863    let Some(target) = body.get(k).filter(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS) else {
1864      continue;
1865    };
1866    let mut open = k + 1;
1867    while open < body.len() && body[open].get_catcode() != Catcode::BEGIN {
1868      open += 1;
1869    }
1870    let written = tokens_group_end(body, open).map_or(&[][..], |past| &body[open..past]);
1871    out.push((*target, holds_arg(written)));
1872  }
1873  out
1874}
1875
1876/// The stores the class's own `\author` — the definition the kernel's locked `\author` refused, kept as
1877/// `\lx@dropped@author` (state.rs `install_definition`, sect08.rs's `\newcommand` bail) — writes, itself or through the
1878/// macros a raw file defined that it calls (ae100prg.cls:170 `\@ifnextchar[{\@sauthor}{\@fauthor}`).
1879fn class_author_stores() -> Vec<Token> {
1880  let dropped = T_CS!("\\lx@dropped@author");
1881  let mut stores = Vec::new();
1882  for name in live_read_order(&[dropped], &[], false) {
1883    let cs = with(name, |n| T_CS!(n));
1884    let body = match lookup_meaning(&cs) {
1885      Some(Stored::Expandable(defn))
1886        if cs == dropped || defn.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File =>
1887      {
1888        match defn.get_expansion() {
1889          Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) => body.clone(),
1890          _ => continue,
1891        }
1892      },
1893      _ => continue,
1894    };
1895    stores.extend(
1896      written_stores(body.unlist_ref(), None)
1897        .into_iter()
1898        .map(|(store, _)| store),
1899    );
1900  }
1901  stores
1902}

1904/// The commands whose braced argument is printed where they stand — LaTeX's text faces and boxes, an e-mail link
1905/// (ae100prg.cls:185 `\address{E-mail: \mailto{#1}}`), a colour (acl2023.sty:186 `\textcolor{black}{\@corremail}`,
1906/// 2506.10896) — with the number of arguments before it they do not print (a link's target, a parbox's width, a colour's
1907/// name).
1908const PRINTED_ARGUMENTS: &[(&str, usize)] = &[
1909  ("\\textbf", 0),
1910  ("\\textit", 0),
1911  ("\\textsl", 0),
1912  ("\\textsc", 0),
1913  ("\\textup", 0),
1914  ("\\textmd", 0),
1915  ("\\textrm", 0),
1916  ("\\textsf", 0),
1917  ("\\texttt", 0),
1918  ("\\textnormal", 0),
1919  ("\\emph", 0),
1920  ("\\underline", 0),
1921  ("\\mbox", 0),
1922  ("\\fbox", 0),
1923  ("\\centerline", 0),
1924  ("\\leftline", 0),
1925  ("\\rightline", 0),
1926  ("\\mailto", 0),
1927  ("\\url", 0),
1928  ("\\href", 1),
1929  ("\\parbox", 1),
1930  ("\\textcolor", 1),
1931];
1932
1933/// The open brace of the group whose close brace is `body[close]`.
1934fn group_open(body: &[Token], close: usize) -> Option<usize> {
1935  let mut depth = 0usize;
1936  for j in (0..=close).rev() {
1937    match body[j].get_catcode() {
1938      Catcode::END => depth += 1,
1939      Catcode::BEGIN => {
1940        depth = depth.checked_sub(1)?;
1941        if depth == 0 {
1942          return Some(j);
1943        }
1944      },
1945      _ => {},
1946    }
1947  }
1948  None
1949}
1950
1951/// The command a braced group opening at `body[open]` is an argument of ([`PRINTED_ARGUMENTS`],
1952/// [`UNPRINTED_ARGUMENTS`]), and whether the command prints it: `(the command's index, printed)` — the place of the group
1953/// among the braced arguments right before it (spaces between, an optional `[…]` before the first passed over). `None`
1954/// when no such command reads it.
1955fn argument_of(body: &[Token], open: usize) -> Option<(usize, bool)> {
1956  let mut k = skip_spaces_back(body, open);
1957  let mut place = 0usize;
1958  while k > 0 && body[k - 1].get_catcode() == Catcode::END {
1959    k = skip_spaces_back(body, group_open(body, k - 1)?);
1960    place += 1;
1961  }
1962  if k > 0 && body[k - 1] == T_OTHER!("]") {
1963    k = skip_spaces_back(
1964      body,
1965      body[..k - 1].iter().rposition(|t| *t == T_OTHER!("["))?,
1966    );
1967  }
1968  let command = body[..k]
1969    .last()
1970    .filter(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS)?;
1971  if let Some((_, unprinted)) = PRINTED_ARGUMENTS
1972    .iter()
1973    .find(|(cs, _)| command.with_str(|s| s == *cs))
1974  {
1975    return (place <= *unprinted).then_some((k - 1, place == *unprinted));
1976  }
1977  (place == 0 && cs_in(command, UNPRINTED_ARGUMENTS)).then_some((k - 1, false))
1978}
1979
1980/// Is `body[at]` inside an argument a command does not print ([`argument_of`]) — the target of `\href{mailto:\@x}{\@x}`
1981/// (acl2023.sty:186, 2506.10896), a `\write`'s text —, at any depth: TeX does not typeset it, so it has no label before
1982/// it to read (brief 97b).
1983fn in_unprinted_argument(body: &[Token], at: usize) -> bool {
1984  let mut depth = 0usize;
1985  for j in (0..at).rev() {
1986    match body[j].get_catcode() {
1987      Catcode::END => depth += 1,
1988      Catcode::BEGIN if depth > 0 => depth -= 1,
1989      Catcode::BEGIN if argument_of(body, j).is_some_and(|(_, printed)| !printed) => return true,
1990      _ => {},
1991    }
1992  }
1993  false
1994}
1995
1996/// The primitives that read a braced text without printing it: the log, a write, a mark, a special.
1997const UNPRINTED_ARGUMENTS: &[&str] = &[
1998  "\\message",
1999  "\\errmessage",
2000  "\\write",
2001  "\\mark",
2002  "\\marks",
2003  "\\special",
2004  "\\showtokens",
2005  "\\detokenize",
2006  "\\scantokens",
2007];
2008
2009/// The arguments the command `t` reads where it stands, each whether it is optional (`[…]`, read only when one
2010/// follows) and whether the command prints it: a face, a box or a link the argument it prints ([`PRINTED_ARGUMENTS`]); a
2011/// macro a raw file defined each argument its own code prints ([`prints_argument`], a few levels down: ae100prg.cls:185
2012/// `\email` passing `#1` to its `\address`); any other command's arguments, by its parameters (an undefined command one
2013/// braced argument), unprinted — it may store them (spr-sola-addons.sty:317 `\add@xtok\adresses@list{…#2…}`,
2014/// 2005.09586), make them a footnote (`\thanks{#1}`) or write them to the log ([`UNPRINTED_ARGUMENTS`]). `None` when it
2015/// takes none (a switch, a register, a conditional's `\fi`), and `Some` of nothing when its arguments are delimited,
2016/// which the token walk cannot follow.
2017fn command_arguments(t: &Token, depth: usize) -> Option<Vec<(bool, bool)>> {
2018  if let Some((_, unprinted)) = PRINTED_ARGUMENTS
2019    .iter()
2020    .find(|(cs, _)| t.with_str(|s| s == *cs))
2021  {
2022    let mut args = vec![(false, false); *unprinted];
2023    args.push((false, true));
2024    return Some(args);
2025  }
2026  if cs_in(t, UNPRINTED_ARGUMENTS) {
2027    return Some(vec![(false, false)]);
2028  }
2029  let meaning = lookup_meaning(t);
2030  let params = match &meaning {
2031    Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) => defn.get_parameters(),
2032    Some(Stored::Primitive(defn)) => defn.get_parameters(),
2033    Some(Stored::Constructor(defn)) => defn.get_parameters(),
2034    None => return Some(vec![(false, false)]),
2035    _ => return None,
2036  }
2037  .map(|p| p.get_parameters().to_vec())
2038  .unwrap_or_default();
2039  if params.is_empty() {
2040    return None;
2041  }
2042  let raw_body = match &meaning {
2043    Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) if defn.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File && depth < 4 => {
2044      if params.iter().any(|p| !undelimited(p)) {
2045        return Some(Vec::new());
2046      }
2047      match defn.get_expansion() {
2048        Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) => Some(executed_code(body.unlist_ref())),
2049        _ => None,
2050      }
2051    },
2052    _ => None,
2053  };
2054  Some(
2055    params
2056      .iter()
2057      .enumerate()
2058      .map(|(k, p)| {
2059        let printed = raw_body
2060          .as_deref()
2061          .is_some_and(|code| prints_argument(code, k + 1, depth + 1));
2062        (p.optional, printed)
2063      })
2064      .collect(),
2065  )
2066}
2067
2068/// Whether `code` (a body's executed code, [`executed_code`]) prints its argument `#<arg>` where it is called: the
2069/// argument stands at its top level or in a plain group (`{\reset@font\affilfont#1\par}`, ar-1col.cls:341; one after a
2070/// switch, `{\eaddfnt{\par #1}}`), or in an argument a command prints ([`command_arguments`]).
2071fn prints_argument(code: &[Token], arg: usize, depth: usize) -> bool {
2072  let is_arg = |t: &Token| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::ARG && t.with_str(|s| s == arg.to_string());
2073  // the index past one argument at `k` (a group or one token), spaces before it skipped
2074  let past_argument = |k: usize| -> usize {
2075    let k = skip_spaces(code, k);
2076    tokens_group_end(code, k).unwrap_or((k + 1).min(code.len()))
2077  };
2078  let mut i = 0;
2079  while i < code.len() {
2080    let t = code[i];
2081    if is_arg(&t) {
2082      return true;
2083    }
2084    if t.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN {
2085      let end = tokens_group_end(code, i).unwrap_or(code.len());
2086      if prints_argument(&code[i + 1..end.saturating_sub(1).max(i + 1)], arg, depth) {
2087        return true;
2088      }
2089      i = end;
2090      continue;
2091    }
2092    if t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS
2093      && let Some(arguments) = command_arguments(&t, depth)
2094    {
2095      if arguments.is_empty() {
2096        // (delimited arguments: what follows is the command's to read)
2097        return false;
2098      }
2099      let mut k = i + 1;
2100      for (optional, printed) in arguments {
2101        let at = skip_spaces(code, k);
2102        let end = if optional {
2103          if code.get(at) != Some(&T_OTHER!("[")) {
2104            continue;
2105          }
2106          code[at..]
2107            .iter()
2108            .position(|b| *b == T_OTHER!("]"))
2109            .map_or(code.len(), |p| at + p + 1)
2110        } else {
2111          past_argument(k)
2112        };
2113        if printed && prints_argument(&code[at.min(end)..end], arg, depth) {
2114          return true;
2115        }
2116        k = end;
2117      }
2118      i = k.max(i + 1);
2119      continue;
2120    }
2121    i += 1;
2122  }
2123  false
2124}
2125
2126/// Whether the raw file's own `\<name>` is a contact setter of the title page, by its code: a macro of one argument
2127/// (or of an optional one and one, setting the second) that sets the argument as a line of its own where it is called
2128/// — its executed code ([`executed_code`]) prints the argument there ([`prints_argument`]) beside a line break
2129/// ([`LINE_BREAKS`]): ae100prg.cls:188
2130/// `\begin{indented}\item[]\normalsize #1\end{indented}`, :185 `\vspace*{5pt}\address{E-mail: …#1…}` (1303.3004),
2131/// ar-1col.cls:341 `\par…{\reset@font\affilfont#1\par}` (1602.05576, 2405.08158) — or that appends it to a store the
2132/// class's own `\author` writes too ([`class_author_stores`]), the author block its title page prints and the kernel's
2133/// `\author` carries: 11lomcon.sty:15-18 `\@aabuffer` (hep-ph0312288), jetpl.cls:286-303 `\@authoraddress`
2134/// (cond-mat0611585). Its argument number when it is one, and whether it prints the line where it is called (else it
2135/// stores it for the title page). Anything else stays the class's: a mark
2136/// (agujournal2019.cls `\def\affil#1{$^{#1}$\ignorespaces}`), an inline face of an address used inside a note or a line
2137/// (`\newcommand{\email}[1]{\texttt{#1}}`, `\href{mailto:#1}{#1}`: a run-in setter, [`run_in_contact_setter`], the API's
2138/// only where its call stands as a line of its own), a footnote (ecai.cls `\institute` =
2139/// `\thanks{#1}`), a store only its own title page reads (K11's; interspeech.cls's `\gdef\@address{#1\\}`;
2140/// spr-sola-addons.sty:317's `\add@xtok\adresses@list{…}`, 2005.09586), a `\message` that prints nothing
2141/// (dmtcs-episciences.cls), a switch or a dispatcher without the argument (jmlr2e.sty `\def\email{\hfill\small\sc}`,
2142/// imsart.sty `\def\address{\@ifnextchar[…}`).
2143fn in_place_contact_setter(name: &str) -> Result<Option<(usize, bool)>> {
2144  let cs = T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"));
2145  let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(&cs) else {
2146    return Ok(None);
2147  };
2148  let arg = defn
2149    .get_parameters()
2150    .and_then(|p| match p.get_parameters().as_slice() {
2151      [only] if !only.optional && undelimited(only) => Some(1),
2152      [first, second] if first.optional && !second.optional && undelimited(second) => Some(2),
2153      _ => None,
2154    });
2155  let (Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)), Some(arg)) = (defn.get_expansion(), arg) else {
2156    return Ok(None);
2157  };
2158  let body = body.unlist_ref();
2159  let executed = executed_code(body);
2160  let sets_a_line =
2161    prints_argument(&executed, arg, 0) && executed.iter().any(|t| cs_in(t, LINE_BREAKS));
2162  if sets_a_line {
2163    return Ok(Some((arg, true)));
2164  }
2165  // (not the kernel's own `\@author`: the rows a class appends to it are its authors' already, each the rows after
2166  // its own `\author` — `\lx@author@flush`, 59d: cjs-rcs-article.cls:361 `\newcommand\affil[1]{\g@addto@macro
2167  // \@author{\\#1}}`)
2168  let appended: Vec<Token> = written_stores(body, Some(arg))
2169    .into_iter()
2170    .filter_map(|(store, holds)| holds.then_some(store))
2171    .filter(|store| *store != T_CS!("\\@author"))
2172    .collect();
2173  if appended.is_empty() {
2174    return Ok(None);
2175  }
2176  let author_stores = class_author_stores();
2177  Ok(
2178    appended
2179      .iter()
2180      .any(|s| author_stores.contains(s))
2181      .then_some((arg, false)),
2182  )
2183}

2185/// What sets an argument as a mark or a note rather than a line: a superscript or subscript, a note, a raised box, math.
2186const MARK_OR_NOTE: &[&str] = &[
2187  "\\textsuperscript",
2188  "\\textsubscript",
2189  "\\footnote",
2190  "\\footnotetext",
2191  "\\thanks",
2192  "\\marginpar",
2193  "\\raisebox",
2194  "\\ensuremath",
2195  "\\sp",
2196  "\\sb",
2197];
