2199/// Whether the raw file's own `\<name>` — one of [`IN_PLACE_CONTACT_SETTERS`], of one argument, which
2200/// [`in_place_contact_setter`] did not take — prints its argument where it is called as text, with no line break of its
2201/// own: iopjournal.cls:108 `\newcommand{\affil}[1]{{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont \raggedright \if@anonymous \phantom{#1}
2202/// \else #1 \fi}}`, a paragraph of its own by the blank lines the document puts around each call (2608.28486,
2203/// 2512.15449, 2602.03759, 2606.14342, 2609.32125). Not a mark — math, a superscript, a note or a raised box anywhere in
2204/// its executed code ([`MARK_OR_NOTE`]: agujournal2019.cls's `\def\affil#1{$^{#1}$\ignorespaces}`) — and not a setter
2205/// whose code reads past the call (`tail_reads_ahead`). Its call is the API's only as a line of its own in the title
2206/// block ([`class_contact_run_in`]).
2207fn run_in_contact_setter(name: &str) -> bool {
2208  let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"))) else {
2209    return false;
2210  };
2211  if !defn.get_parameters().is_some_and(
2212    |p| matches!(p.get_parameters().as_slice(), [only] if !only.optional && undelimited(only)),
2213  ) {
2214    return false;
2215  }
2216  let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion() else {
2217    return false;
2218  };
2219  let body = body.unlist_ref();
2220  let executed = executed_code(body);
2221  prints_argument(&executed, 1, 0)
2222    && !executed.iter().any(|t| {
2223      matches!(
2224        t.get_catcode(),
2225        Catcode::MATH | Catcode::SUPER | Catcode::SUB
2226      ) || cs_in(t, MARK_OR_NOTE)
2227    })
2228    && !crate::latex_constructs::tail_reads_ahead(body)
2229}

2231/// A rerouted run-in setter's call ([`run_in_contact_setter`]): `api` — its line placed by its leading label,
2232/// [`by_its_label`] — where it stands as a line of its own in the title block — between paragraphs at the body's own
2233/// level, the authors set and not yet placed ([`title_block_line`]), its line led by no mark no author requests and
2234/// holding at most one the names request (`relocate::requested_marks`) —, else `class`; never inside a creator's
2235/// own content, where a call printing no line of its own is part of the name's text, nor in the title page's deposit
2236/// ([`in_the_title_page_deposit`]).
2237fn class_contact_run_in(api: Tokens, class: Tokens) -> Result<Tokens> {
2238  if in_the_title_page_deposit() || kernel_and_list(last_group(&class))? {
2239    return Ok(class);
2240  }
2241  let line = last_group(&class);
2242  // (a line holding several marks the names request — `\affil{$^1$Univ A, $^2$Univ B}` run in — is no one entry the
2243  // run's authors share: the class's line, as before. The API's Cut would cut it (`contact_line_call`), but routing a
2244  // run-in line holding several marks is a change of its own, measured on its own population; SYNC row "99b" (e))
2245  if !(title_block_line(true)
2246    && !leads_with_unrequested_mark(line)?
2247    && requested_marks(&Tokens::new(line.to_vec()), "class_contact_run_in")?.held <= 1)
2248  {
2249    return Ok(class);
2250  }
2251  by_its_label(api, line)
2252}
2253
2254/// A rerouted setter's call ([`reroute_in_place_contact_setters`]): `api` where the line it sets is a contact of the
2255/// author creators, else `class`, the file's own definition (`\lx@class@contact@<name>`). The API's inside a creator's
2256/// own content while it digests (`creator_entry_in_progress`: ar-1col's `\affil{$^1$…}` inside `\author{}`,
2257/// 1602.05576, 2405.08158; every one of ci2018's `\affil`s in one `\author`, 1805.12376), or while the author creators
2258/// it annotates are being set — one stands (`has_author_creators`), and neither has a `\maketitle` placed them
2259/// (`author_creators_placed`) nor has the document body sealed them (`frontmatter_entry_sealed`: the paper's authors
2260/// came before its first section) — for a setter that prints its line where it is called (`in_place`) only a call that
2261/// is a line of its own in the title block: between paragraphs at the document body's own level ([`at_the_body_level`]:
2262/// in a group of the body, as an author group's environment is — imsart's `{aug}`, 1301.1466 —, never inside a box, a
2263/// note, a list item, a theorem, a minipage, a float, a `\marginpar`, a `\caption` or an alignment's cell, each of
2264/// which binds a mode of its own: `\footnote{\email{…}}`, `\begin{thm}\address{…} holds.\end{thm}`, `\item \email{…}`,
2265/// `\parbox{5cm}{\address{…}}`, `\begin{tabular}{c}Cy Cole\\ \affil{Univ C}\end{tabular}` — review rev94b r2 B3),
2266/// outside any other frontmatter entry digesting (an abstract). Content is never moved out of its sentence, box or
2267/// note: such a setter's call inside a paragraph, a box or an abstract (before `\maketitle`, or in a class without one:
2268/// "…from the curator, `\email{data@archive.org}`, until 2030.", review rev94b B3; commat.cls's `\address` after the
2269/// "Affiliation:" label of its title page's footnote, 2204.02666) is the class's line where it stands, and so is every
2270/// rerouted setter the class's own definition calls (`class_bodies_call_class_copies`: ae100prg.cls's `\email` prints
2271/// through its `\address` after a `\vspace*`, 1303.3004). So a contact line of a later unit is its text, not the
2272/// paper's authors' contact; a file whose names never become creators — osameet2.sty:49 `\let\author\MEETauthor`,
2273/// saj.cls's `\authors` (1710.05603, 2110.09949, 2212.09079) — keeps its lines beside the names it prints, not on a
2274/// nameless creator; and a line set after `\maketitle` placed the names stays where it prints, as the late frontmatter
2275/// path reads such a list right only under a binding that declares the class's mark conventions (cpc-hepnp.cls's
2276/// `\address` after `\maketitle`: its `,n)` e-mail marks and typed-number lists, OD #912, task 91c; just.cls's binding
2277/// routes the same list).
2278///
2279/// A line in doubt — a line of its own that leads with a mark no author requests at the call
2280/// ([`leads_with_unrequested_mark`]), an in-place setter's optional key no author gave, an optional the API cannot read
2281/// ([`optional_contact`]) — is no creator's contact: the class's line, printed where it is called and digested once, no
2282/// API call queued for it (review rev94b r3 B2: `\affil{\inst{3}Lab C}` under `Ann\inst{1}` and `Bob\inst{2}` was a
2283/// nameless creator's). A call kept in doubt beside its printed line digested the line twice — a counter stepped twice,
2284/// a label made twice (review rev94b r6 B1: "Univ B2", "Body 2." where pdflatex prints 1) —, so none is kept: a mark
2285/// only a later `\author` requests leaves its line where the class prints it, its author with no contact (the residual
2286/// `raw_class_optional_mark_a_later_author_requests`, OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #936). A stored setter's call keeps
2287/// the API's. The API's line is placed by its leading label ([`by_its_label`]); a call in the title page's deposit is the
2288/// class's ([`in_the_title_page_deposit`]).
2289fn class_contact(api: Tokens, class: Tokens, in_place: bool) -> Result<Tokens> {
2290  if in_the_title_page_deposit() || kernel_and_list(last_group(&class))? {
2291    return Ok(class);
2292  }
2293  if creator_entry_in_progress()
2294    || (title_block_line(in_place)
2295      && !(in_place && leads_with_unrequested_mark(last_group(&class))?))
2296  {
2297    by_its_label(api, last_group(&class))
2298  } else {
2299    Ok(class)
2300  }
2301}

2303/// A rerouted setter's API call `api` as [`contact_line_call`] places its line: the template's own call of an address
2304/// or an affiliation (`\lx@add@affiliation[annotate=run]{<line>}`, [`IN_PLACE_CONTACT_SETTERS`]) by the line's leading
2305/// label — the in-place, stored and run-in routes alike (review rev99b B1: a line under `annotate=run` was every author's
2306/// of the run when the frontmatter observer declined) —; any other call (an e-mail list, an optional argument's
2307/// `label=` or caption, [`optional_contact`]) as it is.
2308fn by_its_label(api: Tokens, line: &[Token]) -> Result<Tokens> {
2309  let tokens = api.unlist_ref();
2310  let Some(head) = tokens
2311    .first()
2312    .filter(|head| **head == T_CS!("\\lx@add@affiliation") || **head == T_CS!("\\lx@add@address"))
2313  else {
2314    return Ok(api);
2315  };
2316  let mut template =
2317    mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!("{head}[annotate=run]"))).unlist();
2318  template.push(T_BEGIN!());
2319  template.extend_from_slice(line);
2320  template.push(T_END!());
2321  if tokens != template.as_slice() {
2322    return Ok(api);
2323  }
2324  // (one entry only: a call whose lines are each led by a label of their own — jetpl.cls's stored `\address{$^{\#}$ … \\
2325  // $^*$ … \\ $^+$ …}`, 0807.3896, which the label of its first line alone gave to the first author — goes as it is,
2326  // under its run, review rev94b r3 B1: the frontmatter API's Cut cuts it at the marks the names request
2327  // (`cut_marked_contact`, OD #954), each entry its requesters' and one no name requests the block's; a call the Cut
2328  // declines is the frontmatter observer's, the font open before each entry kept (`\lx@fm@entry@font`), or whole)
2329  if displaced_entries(&Tokens::new(line.to_vec()), None)?.len() > 1 {
2330    return Ok(api);
2331  }
2332  contact_line_call(&head.to_string(), &Tokens::new(line.to_vec()))
2333}

2335/// Whether the class's title page is being deposited (sect05.rs `\lx@deposit@maketitle`, its `\@maketitle` or its
2336/// own `\maketitle` replayed): a rerouted setter's call there is one the class's store kept as the document wrote it,
2337/// in a call the displaced route left the class's (`displaced_argument_in_doubt`: 2607.25849's `\affiliation{email:
2338/// \email{…}\\ …}`), so it is the class's too, printed where its title page prints the store (review rev99b B3).
2339fn in_the_title_page_deposit() -> bool { lookup_bool("lx_depositing_frontmatter_fields") }
2340
2341/// Whether a rerouted setter's call is a line of the authors being set ([`class_contact`]): an author creator stands to
2342/// carry it, none placed nor sealed yet, and — for a setter printing its line where it is called — the call stands
2343/// between paragraphs at the body's own level, outside any other frontmatter entry digesting.
2344fn title_block_line(in_place: bool) -> bool {
2345  (!in_place || (at_the_body_level() && !frontmatter_entry_in_progress()))
2346    && has_author_creators()
2347    && !author_creators_placed()
2348    && !frontmatter_entry_sealed("ltx:creator", "author")
2349}
2350
2351/// The content of the last braced group of `tokens`: a rerouted setter's class call's mandatory argument.
2352fn last_group(tokens: &Tokens) -> &[Token] {
2353  let tokens = tokens.unlist_ref();
2354  let Some(close) = tokens.iter().rposition(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::END) else {
2355    return &[];
2356  };
2357  let mut depth = 0usize;
2358  for at in (0..=close).rev() {
2359    match tokens[at].get_catcode() {
2360      Catcode::END => depth += 1,
2361      Catcode::BEGIN => {
2362        depth -= 1;
2363        if depth == 0 {
2364          return &tokens[at + 1..close];
2365        }
2366      },
2367      _ => {},
2368    }
2369  }
2370  &[]
2371}
2372
2373/// The operand of the printed mark `line` leads with — `\inst{3}`, `\textsuperscript{3}`, `\@textsuperscript{3}`,
2374/// `$^{3}$`, `$^3$` —, if it does.
2375fn leading_mark_operand(line: &[Token]) -> Option<Vec<Token>> {
2376  leading_mark(line).map(|(operand, _)| operand)
2377}
2378
2379/// The printed mark `line` leads with ([`leading_mark_operand`]): its operand, and the index past the mark when it is
2380/// whole in `line` (a math mark's closing `$` included), else `None` there.
2381fn leading_mark(line: &[Token]) -> Option<(Vec<Token>, Option<usize>)> {
2382  let mut at = line
2383    .iter()
2384    .position(|t| t.get_catcode() != Catcode::SPACE)?;
2385  // (a braced argument's content, or the one token, and the index past it)
2386  let operand = |at: usize| -> Option<(Vec<Token>, usize)> {
2387    let first = line.get(at)?;
2388    if first.get_catcode() != Catcode::BEGIN {
2389      return Some((vec![*first], at + 1));
2390    }
2391    let mut depth = 0usize;
2392    for (k, t) in line.iter().enumerate().skip(at) {
2393      match t.get_catcode() {
2394        Catcode::BEGIN => depth += 1,
2395        Catcode::END => {
2396          depth -= 1;
2397          if depth == 0 {
2398            return Some((line[at + 1..k].to_vec(), k + 1));
2399          }
2400        },
2401        _ => {},
2402      }
2403    }
2404    None
2405  };
2406  let first = line[at];
2407  if first.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS
2408    && first.with_cs_name(|n| matches!(n, "\\inst" | "\\textsuperscript" | "\\@textsuperscript"))
2409  {
2410    return operand(at + 1).map(|(operand, past)| (operand, Some(past)));
2411  }
2412  if first.get_catcode() != Catcode::MATH {
2413    return None;
2414  }
2415  at += 1;
2416  // (an empty group a script hangs on, `${}^3$`)
2417  if line
2418    .get(at)
2419    .is_some_and(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN)
2420    && line
2421      .get(at + 1)
2422      .is_some_and(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::END)
2423  {
2424    at += 2;
2425  }
2426  if line.get(at)?.get_catcode() != Catcode::SUPER {
2427    return None;
2428  }
2429  let (operand, past) = operand(at + 1)?;
2430  let close = skip_spaces(line, past);
2431  let whole = line
2432    .get(close)
2433    .is_some_and(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::MATH)
2434    .then_some(close + 1);
2435  Some((operand, whole))
2436}
2437
2438/// Whether a leading mark's `operand` labels its line: what it prints, read by its printed text as the line's label is
2439/// (`queue_digest::prints_a_mark`, the reading of `line_mark_label`): a letter or a digit (`$^{a}$`, `\inst{3}`), any
2440/// other character (`$^*$`, `$^{+}$`), or whatever symbol a command prints (`$^\dagger$`, `$^{\ddagger}$`, `$^{\#}$`,
2441/// `$^\bigstar$`, `$^\clubsuit$`, `\textsuperscript{\textdagger}`) — not spacing alone, nor a command printing nothing
2442/// (review rev99b2 F2 and rev100a S1: symbol commands were read as no mark, so `\affil{$^\dagger$Deceased}` or
2443/// `\affil{$^\bigstar$Deceased}`, which no name requests, went to every author of the run).
2444fn is_a_mark(operand: &[Token]) -> Result<bool> { prints_a_mark(operand) }
2445
2446/// Whether `line` leads with a printed mark ([`leading_mark_operand`]) whose label no author's name requests at the
2447/// call, queued or digested in the flush (`queued_authors_requesting`, `digested_authors_requesting`), the names showing
2448/// marks of their own (`authors_show_marks`): a contact line no creator asks for, which the API would give to a creator
2449/// of no name (review rev94b r3 B2: orph3's `\address[\inst{3}]{Lab C, Place}`, orph6's `\affil{\inst{3}Lab C, Place}`
2450/// under `Ann Able\inst{1} and Bob Baker\inst{2}`), or every author of the run (review rev99b2 F2: `\affil{$^\dagger$
2451/// Deceased}` under `Ann Able$^{1}$, Bob Baker$^{2}$`, a control-sequence symbol, [`is_a_mark`]).
2452fn leads_with_unrequested_mark(line: &[Token]) -> Result<bool> {
2453  let Some(operand) = leading_mark_operand(line) else {
2454    return Ok(false);
2455  };
2456  Ok(
2457    authors_show_marks()?
2458      && queued_authors_requesting(&operand)?.is_empty()
2459      && !digested_authors_requesting(&Tokens::new(operand.clone()).to_string())
2460      && is_a_mark(&operand)?
2461      && !requested_as_printed(&operand)?,
2462  )
2463}
2464
2465/// Whether a name requests the label a leading mark's `operand` prints — read by its printed text, as
2466/// [`contact_line_call`] places the line (`line_mark_label`, `names_request_label`), through the one key both sides are
2467/// keyed by (`queue_digest::clean_frontmatter_labels`, an asterisk one way however written: `one_asterisk`) —, where the
2468/// readers by the operand as written miss a mark spelled otherwise on the name (`\affil{\textsuperscript{\dag}Deceased}`
2469/// under `Ann Able$^{1,\dagger}$`; `$^\ast$` under `$^{1,*}$` and `$^*$` under `$^{1,\ast}$`, review rev100a B1): such a
2470/// line is its carriers', not in doubt ([`is_a_mark`], review rev99b2 F2).
2471fn requested_as_printed(operand: &[Token]) -> Result<bool> {
2472  match line_mark_label(&Tokens::new(operand.to_vec()))? {
2473    Some(label) => names_request_label(&label),
2474    None => Ok(false),
2475  }
2476}
2477
2478/// A rerouted `[optional]{mandatory}` setter's call (`\lx@class@contact@optional`): the API call its optional argument
2479/// `opt` makes of the line `arg`, by the API's convention — Perl's own bindings pass it as the entry's label
2480/// (jheppub.sty.ltxml:35-37 `\affiliation[a]{…}` → `label={a}`, `\author[a]` → `annotations={a}`) or as its caption
2481/// (ams_support.sty.ltxml:115 `\address[]{}` → `\lx@add@address[name={#1}]{#2}`) —, then given to [`class_contact`]
2482/// as the call without one is. Empty, it is dropped, as OmniBus drops it (OmniBus.cls.ltxml:92). A printed mark (a
2483/// superscript, `\textsuperscript`, `\inst`: w-art.cls's `\address[\inst{1}]{…}`, 0910.5837, 1105.1753,
2484/// quant-ph0606237) leads the line, read by its marks as any marked line is. A key an `\author[key]` gave its authors
2485/// (jheppub-like `\author[a,b]{…}` with `\affiliation[b]{…}`, 2303.16316; stacs.cls's `\address[uniroma]{…}`,
2486/// 0902.1737; `lx_author_keys`, `\lx@author@keys`) labels the line for each of those authors (`author:N`); a key the
2487/// names' marks print (`\address[a]{…}` under `Ann$^a$`) labels it by that mark. Words no creator requests are its
2488/// caption (amsart copies' `\address[Current address]{…}`: "(Current address) Lab C, Place" — smfart, nyjm, birkmult,
2489/// birkart, ECACRM). Anything else is in doubt: an in-place setter's call stays the class's own, a stored one's drops
2490/// it (review rev94b r1-r2 B2: a caption written for every optional made w-art's marks and jheppub's keys names).
2491fn optional_contact(name: &str, in_place: bool, opt: Tokens, arg: Tokens) -> Result<Tokens> {
2492  let Some((_, template)) = IN_PLACE_CONTACT_SETTERS.iter().find(|(n, _)| *n == name) else {
2493    return Ok(arg);
2494  };
2495  let opt_tokens: Vec<Token> = opt.unlist();
2496  let trimmed: Vec<&Token> = opt_tokens
2497    .iter()
2498    .filter(|t| t.get_catcode() != Catcode::SPACE)
2499    .collect();
2500  // (the class's own call, the optional argument braced when it holds a `]`)
2501  let class = {
2502    let mut tokens = vec![T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@class@contact@{name}")), T_OTHER!("[")];
2503    let bracket = opt_tokens.iter().any(|t| *t == T_OTHER!("]"));
2504    if bracket {
2505      tokens.push(T_BEGIN!());
2506    }
2507    tokens.extend(opt_tokens.iter().cloned());
2508    if bracket {
2509      tokens.push(T_END!());
2510    }
2511    tokens.extend([T_OTHER!("]"), T_BEGIN!()]);
2512    tokens.extend(arg.unlist_ref().iter().cloned());
2513    tokens.push(T_END!());
2514    Tokens::new(tokens)
2515  };
2516  // (the API call with `options` added to its own, its line `line`)
2517  let call = |options: &str, line: Tokens| -> Tokens {
2518    let template = if options.is_empty() {
2519      template.to_string()
2520    } else if template.contains("[annotate=run]") {
2521      template.replace("[annotate=run]", &s!("[{options}]"))
2522    } else {
2523      template.replace("\\lx@add@email}", &s!("\\lx@add@email[{options}]}}"))
2524    };
2525    let placeholder = T_CS!("\\lx@class@contact@line");
2526    let caption = T_CS!("\\lx@class@contact@caption");
2527    let mut out = Vec::new();
2528    for t in mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(
2529      template.replace("ARG", "\\lx@class@contact@line"),
2530    ))
2531    .unlist()
2532    {
2533      if t == placeholder {
2534        out.extend(line.unlist_ref().iter().cloned());
2535      } else if t == caption {
2536        out.extend(opt_tokens.iter().cloned());
2537      } else {
2538        out.push(t);
2539      }
2540    }
2541    Tokens::new(out)
2542  };
2543  let decide = |api: Tokens| class_contact(api, class.clone(), in_place);
2544  if trimmed.is_empty() {
2545    return decide(call("", arg));
2546  }
2547  let mark = trimmed.iter().any(|t| {
2548    t.get_catcode() == Catcode::SUPER
2549      || (t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS
2550        && t.with_cs_name(|n| matches!(n, "\\inst" | "\\textsuperscript" | "\\@textsuperscript")))
2551  });
2552  if mark {
2553    // (a mark no author requests at the call is in doubt — review rev94b r3 B2: the address it labels a nameless
2554    // creator's; an in-place setter's line the class's, printed once, review rev94b r6 B1: [`class_contact`])
2555    if leads_with_unrequested_mark(&opt_tokens)? {
2556      return if in_place {
2557        Ok(class)
2558      } else {
2559        decide(call("", arg))
2560      };
2561    }
2562    // (an address's line read for its marks as an affiliation's is, `\lx@affiliation@withinst`: the mark labels it and
2563    // prints nothing of its own in it)
2564    let mut line = opt_tokens.clone();
2565    line.extend(arg.unlist_ref().iter().cloned());
2566    if template.starts_with("\\lx@add@address") {
2567      let mut wrapped = vec![T_CS!("\\lx@affiliation@withinst"), T_BEGIN!()];
2568      wrapped.extend(line);
2569      wrapped.push(T_END!());
2570      line = wrapped;
2571    }
2572    return decide(call("", Tokens::new(line)));
2573  }
2574  let plain = trimmed
2575    .iter()
2576    .all(|t| matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER) && **t != T_OTHER!("]"));
2577  if plain {
2578    let text: String = opt_tokens.iter().map(ToString::to_string).collect();
2579    let keys: Vec<&str> = text
2580      .split(',')
2581      .map(str::trim)
2582      .filter(|k| !k.is_empty())
2583      .collect();
2584    // (the authors an `\author[key]` gave each key)
2585    let records = lookup_string(AUTHOR_KEYS);
2586    let mut authors: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
2587    let mut every_key = !keys.is_empty();
2588    for key in &keys {
2589      let mut found = false;
2590      for record in records.split('\u{1}').filter(|r| !r.is_empty()) {
2591        let mut parts = record.split('\u{2}');
2592        let (Some(record_keys), Some(from), Some(to)) = (parts.next(), parts.next(), parts.next())
2593        else {
2594          continue;
2595        };
2596        if record_keys.split(',').map(str::trim).any(|k| k == *key)
2597          && let (Ok(from), Ok(to)) = (from.parse::<usize>(), to.parse::<usize>())
2598        {
2599          found = true;
2600          authors.extend(from + 1..=to);
2601        }
2602      }
2603      every_key &= found;
2604    }
2605    if every_key && !authors.is_empty() {
2606      authors.sort_unstable();
2607      authors.dedup();
2608      let mut tokens = Vec::new();
2609      for n in authors {
2610        tokens.extend(call(&s!("label={{author:{n}}}"), arg.clone()).unlist());
2611      }
2612      return decide(Tokens::new(tokens));
2613    }
2614    let mut all_requested = !keys.is_empty();
2615    for key in &keys {
2616      if !queued_authors_requesting_key(key)? && !digested_authors_requesting(key) {
2617        all_requested = false;
2618        break;
2619      }
2620    }
2621    if all_requested {
2622      return decide(call(&s!("label={{{}}}", keys.join(",")), arg));
2623    }
2624    // (a list of single words, `c, d`, is a key list no creator requests — in doubt, no caption: review rev94b r3 B2b,
2625    // `\address[c, d]{…}` was captioned "c, d")
2626    let key_list = text.contains(',')
2627      && text
2628        .split(',')
2629        .all(|piece| piece.split_whitespace().count() <= 1);
2630    if !key_list
2631      && text
2632        .split_whitespace()
2633        .filter(|w| w.chars().any(char::is_alphabetic))
2634        .count()
2635        >= 2
2636    {
2637      // (an address or an affiliation keeps its run, an e-mail the API's own placement)
2638      let options = if template.contains("[annotate=run]") {
2639        "annotate=run,name={\\lx@class@contact@caption}"
2640      } else {
2641        "name={\\lx@class@contact@caption}"
2642      };
2643      return decide(call(options, arg));
2644    }
2645  }
2646  // (in doubt: an in-place setter's line is the class's, printed where it is called and digested once — a key no author
2647  // gave, an optional the API cannot read: [`class_contact`] —, a stored setter's call the API's without the optional)
2648  if in_place {
2649    Ok(class)
2650  } else {
2651    decide(call("", arg))
2652  }
2653}
2654
2655/// The records of the kernel's `\author[key]{…}` calls (`\lx@author@keys`): each its keys (`a,b`), the number of the
2656/// author creators standing before it and after it, `\u{2}`-parted, the records `\u{1}`-parted.
2657const AUTHOR_KEYS: &str = "lx_author_keys";
2658
2659/// Whether the digestion stands between paragraphs at the document body's own level: in vertical mode, and no frame
2660/// binds a mode of its own (`BOUND_MODE`: the body's is `\begin{document}`'s, bound in no frame of its own,
2661/// sect02.rs) — a group of the body binds none (`{\small …}`, an environment that is a group, imsart's `{aug}`); a box,
2662/// a note, a list, a theorem, a minipage, a float or an alignment's cell does (`begin_mode`) — or in the box the
2663/// authors were set in, still open, no box opened since (`\lx@author@box`): the title block's own box, imsart.sty's
2664/// `\open@fm` `\global\setbox\fm@box=\vbox\bgroup` around `{frontmatter}` (1301.1466's `\affiliation` in its
2665/// `{aug}`).
2666fn at_the_body_level() -> bool {
2667  let boxes = mode_frames();
2668  matches!(
2669    lookup_string("MODE").as_str(),
2670    "vertical" | "internal_vertical"
2671  ) && (boxes == 0 || lookup_string(AUTHOR_BOX) == author_box())
2672}
2673
2674/// How many frames bind a mode of their own ([`at_the_body_level`]).
2675fn mode_frames() -> usize {
2676  (0..get_frame_depth())
2677    .filter(|&n| is_value_bound("BOUND_MODE", Some(n)))
2678    .count()
2679}
2680
2681/// The box the digestion stands in: the serial (`lx@frame@id`, `push_stack_frame`) of the innermost frame binding a
2682/// mode of its own ([`mode_frames`]) — the same while that box is open and no box opened inside it, whatever groups
2683/// opened or closed in it; another box, even at the same depth (a second minipage), another.
2684fn author_box() -> String {
2685  (0..get_frame_depth())
2686    .find(|&n| is_value_bound("BOUND_MODE", Some(n)))
2687    .and_then(|n| value_in_frame("lx@frame@id", n))
2688    .map(|id| match id {
2689      Stored::Number(n) => n.0.to_string(),
2690      other => format!("{other:?}"),
2691    })
2692    .unwrap_or_default()
2693}
2694
2695/// The box the kernel's last `\author` call stood in ([`author_box`]), recorded globally (`\lx@author@box`): a group
2696/// it was called in — imsart's `{aug}`, the kernel's own call — closes before the authors' box does (review rev94b r3:
2697/// imsart's `\affiliation` after `\end{aug}` in its `\open@fm` box went to the class); recorded anew at each
2698/// `\author`, read only while the authors are being set (`class_contact`).
2699const AUTHOR_BOX: &str = "lx_author_box";
2700
2701/// Each kept class definition (`\lx@class@contact@<name>`) of the rerouted setters `names`, rewritten so that its calls
2702/// to another rerouted setter — or to itself — call that setter's class definition while the setter still means the
2703/// reroute, else its meaning then (`\lx@class@contact@dispatch@<name>`, one token at the call site, which expands to
2704/// that one token: a document's
2705/// `\renewcommand{\address}[1]{…Addr: #1…}` is what ae100prg's `\email` prints through — review rev94b r3 S3): a setter run as the class's
2706/// prints all of its line where the class prints it, never part of it through the API (ae100prg.cls:185 `\def\email#1
2707/// {\vspace*{5pt}\address{E-mail: #1}}`, 1303.3004: its `\address` the class's `\lx@class@contact@address`). Static, at
2708/// the reroute: nothing is left in the call's input for the class's own look-ahead to see (a class's
2709/// `\def\email#1{\\{\ttfamily #1}\ignorespaces}` skips the spaces after the call, `\@ifnextchar`/`\@ifstar` read the
2710/// token after it — review rev94b r2 S3).
2711fn class_bodies_call_class_copies(names: &[&str]) {
2712  let rerouted: Vec<(Token, Token)> = names
2713    .iter()
2714    .map(|name| {
2715      (
2716        T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}")),
2717        T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@class@contact@{name}")),
2718      )
2719    })
2720    .collect();
2721  for (_, copy) in &rerouted {
2722    let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(copy) else {
2723      continue;
2724    };
2725    let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion() else {
2726      continue;
2727    };
2728    if !body
2729      .unlist_ref()
2730      .iter()
2731      .any(|t| rerouted.iter().any(|(cs, _)| cs == t))
2732    {
2733      continue;
2734    }
2735    let tokens = body.unlist_ref();
2736    let mut calls: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
2737    for (k, t) in tokens.iter().enumerate() {
2738      match rerouted.iter().position(|(cs, _)| cs == t) {
2739        // (an `\ifx` operand compares the class's own definition, as the file's test did: `\ifx\address\relax`)
2740        Some(at)
2741          if [1, 2]
2742            .iter()
2743            .any(|back| k >= *back && tokens[k - back] == T_CS!("\\ifx")) =>
2744        {
2745          calls.push(rerouted[at].1);
2746        },
2747        // (one token, whatever reads it: `\expandafter\address\expandafter{…}`, `\let\x\address`, `\@ifnextchar[\address`
2748        // — review rev94b r4 S2, cexp.cls's `\email`: a braced name after one dispatcher left the class copy's `#1` to
2749        // the `\expandafter`)
2750        Some(at) => calls.push(T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@class@contact@dispatch@{}", names[at]))),
2751        None => calls.push(*t),
2752      }
2753    }
2754    let mut rewritten = (*defn).clone();
2755    rewritten.expansion = Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(Tokens::new(calls)));
2756    rewritten.plan = std::cell::OnceCell::new();
2757    assign_meaning(
2758      copy,
2759      Stored::Expandable(Rc::new(rewritten)),
2760      Some(Scope::Global),
2761    );
2762  }
2763}
2764
2765/// After a raw file is read (a class, `\lx@class@loaded@raw`; a package, `\lx@package@loaded@raw`): each Frontmatter
2766/// API contact setter it defined (`IN_PLACE_CONTACT_SETTERS`; still its own definition, not a binding's, nor one K11
2767/// rerouted as a store) that sets its argument as a title-page line (`in_place_contact_setter`) is the API's, as the
2768/// kernel's locked `\author` stays the kernel's against the file's own: the line becomes a contact of the creators,
2769/// keyed by its printed marks, and the file's in-place typesetting of it is not kept — in pdflatex it prints under the
2770/// names its `\author` printed, which the kernel's `\author` made creators. Beyond Perl, which reads no shipped file and
2771/// gives the paper OmniBus's setters. OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #936.
2772pub fn reroute_in_place_contact_setters(file: &str) -> Result<()> {
2773  // (each decided on the file's own definitions before any is rerouted: ae100prg.cls's `\email` prints through its
2774  // `\address`)
2775  let mut setters = Vec::new();
2776  for (name, api) in IN_PLACE_CONTACT_SETTERS {
2777    if !defined_by_a_raw_file(name)? {
2778      continue;
2779    }
2780    if let Some((arg, in_place)) = in_place_contact_setter(name)? {
2781      setters.push((*name, *api, arg, in_place, false));
2782    } else if run_in_contact_setter(name) {
2783      setters.push((*name, *api, 1, true, true));
2784    }
2785  }
2786  let mut rerouted = Vec::new();
2787  for (name, api, arg, in_place, run_in) in setters {
2788    let (params, api, class_args) = if arg == 2 {
2789      (
2790        convert_latex_args(2, Some(Tokens::new(Vec::new())))?,
2791        s!(
2792          "\\lx@class@contact@optional{{{name}}}{{{}}}{{#1}}{{#2}}",
2793          u8::from(in_place)
2794        ),
2795        "[#1]{#2}",
2796      )
2797    } else {
2798      (
2799        convert_latex_args(1, None)?,
2800        api.replace("ARG", "#1"),
2801        "{#1}",
2802      )
2803    };
2804    // (the file's own definition kept, for the calls `\lx@class@contact` gives it; both global, as the file's own
2805    // definition was the document's for the rest of the run)
2806    let cs = T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"));
2807    Let!(T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@class@contact@{name}")), cs, Scope::Global);
2808    // (a setter that prints its line where it is called decides by where the call stands, `\lx@class@contact`; one
2809    // that stores it for the title page sets a title-page line wherever it is called, `\lx@class@contact@stored`)
2810    let decider = if run_in {
2811      "\\lx@class@contact@runin"
2812    } else if in_place {
2813      "\\lx@class@contact"
2814    } else {
2815      "\\lx@class@contact@stored"
2816    };
2817    let body = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!(
2818      "{decider}{{{api}}}{{\\lx@class@contact@{name}{class_args}}}"
2819    )));
2820    // (protected, so that it decides where it is digested: a call inside a class's `\protected@xdef` store stays in
2821    // the store as written, never its decision at the store's expansion baked into it — iau.cls:1516's numbered
2822    // `\affiliation` store around 2607.25849's inline `\email`, whose API call fired at the deposit and gave the
2823    // address to the editors' creator, review rev99b B3)
2824    DefMacro!(cs, params, body, scope => Some(Scope::Global), protected => true);
2825    // (the reroute's own meaning, which a class body's call is dispatched by: `\lx@class@contact@dispatch@<name>`, one
2826    // token where the body called the setter)
2827    Let!(
2828      T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@class@contact@rerouted@{name}")),
2829      cs,
2830      Scope::Global
2831    );
2832    let dispatch = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!(
2833      "\\lx@class@contact@dispatch{{{name}}}"
2834    )));
2835    DefMacro!(
2836      T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@class@contact@dispatch@{name}")),
2837      None,
2838      dispatch,
2839      scope => Some(Scope::Global)
2840    );
2841    rerouted.push(name);
2842  }
2843  class_bodies_call_class_copies(&rerouted);
2844  if !rerouted.is_empty() {
2845    Info!(
2846      "frontmatter",
2847      file,
2848      s!(
2849        "The raw file's contact setters that print a title-page line set the frontmatter's contacts: \\{}",
2850        rerouted.join(", \\")
2851      )
2852    );
2853  }
2854  Ok(())
2855}

2857/// The in-place API a table setter's call reaches when its store is an affiliation or an address of the creators that
2858/// the class's title page prints into the body ([`reroute_displaced_setters`]): its table API's kind
2859/// (`STORE_SETTERS`: `\lx@add@store@affiliations`, `\lx@add@address`), each line of it placed as
2860/// [`contact_line_call`] places a line.
2861fn displaced_api(name: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
2862  let (_, api) = STORE_SETTERS.iter().find(|(n, _)| *n == name)?;
2863  if api.starts_with("\\lx@add@store@affiliations") {
2864    Some("\\lx@add@affiliation")
2865  } else if api.starts_with("\\lx@add@address") {
2866    Some("\\lx@add@address")
2867  } else {
2868    None
2869  }
2870}
2871
2872/// The API call (`head`: `\lx@add@affiliation`, `\lx@add@address`) of one contact line of a displaced or run-in setter,
2873/// or of an in-place or stored setter's call of one entry (`hand_displaced_stores`, [`by_its_label`]). A line led by a printed label (`leading_line_label`, the shared
2874/// reader: a superscript, `\textsuperscript`, a hand-printed number) that a name requests (`names_request_label`) is that
2875/// label's — `label={affiliation:<key>}`, the mark keyed by its printed text as the names' requests are
2876/// (`line_mark_label`) —, which the kernel pairs with the names requesting it: under `annotate=run` every author of the
2877/// run held every line whenever the frontmatter observer declined to re-part them (review rev99b B1: 2512.20289's
2878/// `\affil{$^a$…}` and `\affil{$^b$…}` under `$^{1,a}$`, `$^{2,b}$`, `$^{3,a}$`, the observer declining over the ORCID
2879/// links; 2609.15125, 2607.25849, 1603.07668, 1704.03333). Marks win (FIDELITY_CHARTER "One placement order", R1). Any
2880/// other line — led by no label, by a hand-printed number no name requests (user ruling R2), or by a mark no name
2881/// requests by its label (a glued symbol run, `$^{*+}$`, requests `*` and `+` only by the frontmatter observer's parting,
2882/// 0807.3896) — goes under the names of its run (`annotate=run`, as [`IN_PLACE_CONTACT_SETTERS`]' lines do), capped at
2883/// its own run where a labelled line stands in the kernel's (`run_cap_past_labels`: a labelled contact ends no run,
2884/// 2608.19687's `\affil{$^10$…}` reached every author since line 2). A line holding several entries led by marks the
2885/// names request (`holds_requested_marked_entries`, the API's Cut decision, its one reader: `\institute{$^{1}$Univ A,
2886/// $^{2}$Univ B}` run in, 1812.07638's 64 institutes under 200 names) is the call bare, no label and no run, which the
2887/// frontmatter API's Cut (`cut_marked_contact`, OD #954) cuts once into one entry per mark, each its requesting names',
2888/// one no name requests the block's (SYNC row "99b" (e)). A line holding several requested marks that the Cut cannot cut
2889/// — set in a box or an environment, or not led by one — lands once at the block for the frontmatter observer to read by
2890/// its marks (`_unsplit`, the REVTeX binding's fail-safe). The line keeps its printed mark, as the class prints it.
2891fn contact_line_call(head: &str, line: &Tokens) -> Result<Tokens> {
2892  let mut call = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!("{head}"))).unlist();
2893  if holds_requested_marked_entries(line)? {
2894    call.push(T_BEGIN!());
2895    call.extend(line.unlist_ref().iter().copied());
2896    call.push(T_END!());
2897    return Ok(Tokens::new(call));
2898  }
2899  call.push(T_OTHER!("["));
2900  if requested_marks(line, "contact_line_call")?.held > 1 {
2901    call.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal("label={addresses:block},_unsplit=true").unlist());
2902  } else {
2903    let (mut legend, mut glyph) = (None, None);
2904    let label = match leading_line_label(line) {
2905      Some((key, typed, _)) => {
2906        let operand = (!typed)
2907          .then(|| leading_mark_operand(line.unlist_ref()))
2908          .flatten();
2909        let (label, printed_glyph) = match &operand {
2910          Some(operand) => match line_mark(&Tokens::new(operand.clone()))? {
2911            Some((label, glyph)) => (Some(label), Some(glyph)),
2912            None => (None, None),
2913          },
2914          None => (Some(s!("affiliation:{key}")), None),
2915        };
2916        legend = operand
2917          .as_deref()
2918          .and_then(|operand| legend_note_kind(operand, printed_glyph.as_deref(), line));
2919        glyph = printed_glyph;
2920        match label {
2921          Some(label) if names_request_label(&label)? => Some(label),
2922          _ => None,
2923        }
2924      },
2925      None => None,
2926    };
2927    // (a legend its symbol labels — `$^*$Author to whom any correspondence should be addressed.` — is a note of the names
2928    // carrying the symbol, its mark the symbol as the line prints it: `legend_note_kind`; `line_mark`, `$^\ast$`'s `∗`,
2929    // which the label keys `*`)
2930    if let (Some(label), Some(kind)) = (&label, legend) {
2931      let glyph = glyph
2932        .as_deref()
2933        .unwrap_or_else(|| label.strip_prefix("affiliation:").unwrap_or(label));
2934      let mut note = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!(
2935        "\\lx@annotate@frontmatter{{ltx:creator}}{{ltx:note}}[role=thanks,class={{ltx_note_frontmatter ltx_thanks_{kind}}},label={{"
2936      )))
2937      .unlist();
2938      note.extend(ExplodeText!(label));
2939      note.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal("},mark={").unlist());
2940      note.extend(ExplodeText!(glyph));
2941      note.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal("}]").unlist());
2942      note.push(T_BEGIN!());
2943      note.extend(line.unlist_ref().iter().copied());
2944      note.push(T_END!());
2945      return Ok(Tokens::new(note));
2946    }
2947    match label {
2948      // (the label as text, each of its characters as it is: a symbol mark's `#` is no parameter token, a `%` no
2949      // comment — `\address{$^{\#}$ Institute …}` under `Klinkhamer $^{\#}$`, 0807.3896)
2950      Some(label) => {
2951        call.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal("label={").unlist());
2952        call.extend(ExplodeText!(&label));
2953        call.push(T_END!());
2954      },
2955      // (capped at the line's own run where a labelled line stands in the kernel's, `run_cap_past_labels`)
2956      None => {
2957        let role = head.trim_start_matches("\\lx@add@");
2958        let run = match run_cap_past_labels(role) {
2959          Some(cap) => s!("annotate=run:{cap}"),
2960          None => "annotate=run".to_string(),
2961        };
2962        call.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(run)).unlist());
2963      },
2964    }
2965  }
2966  call.push(T_OTHER!("]"));
2967  call.push(T_BEGIN!());
2968  call.extend(line.unlist_ref().iter().copied());
2969  call.push(T_END!());
2970  Ok(Tokens::new(call))
2971}
2972
2973/// Is the raw file's own `\<name>` a setter that stores its argument for the title page without printing it ([`
2974/// reroute_displaced_setters`]): a macro of one argument, still the file's own definition (neither K11 nor the in-place
2975/// reroute took it), whose executed code ([`executed_code`]) does not print the argument where it is called
2976/// ([`prints_argument`]), whose body writes it into a definition ([`written_stores`]: a `\gdef`, `\xdef`, `\g@addto@macro`,
2977/// a `\csname`-named one) and whose last command reads nothing after the call (`tail_reads_ahead`)?
2978fn displaced_setter(name: &str) -> bool {
2979  let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"))) else {
2980    return false;
2981  };
2982  if defn.get_origin() != DefinitionOrigin::File
2983    || !defn.get_parameters().is_some_and(
2984      |p| matches!(p.get_parameters().as_slice(), [only] if !only.optional && undelimited(only)),
2985    )
2986  {
2987    return false;
2988  }
2989  let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion() else {
2990    return false;
2991  };
2992  let body = body.unlist_ref();
2993  !prints_argument(&executed_code(body), 1, 0)
2994    && written_stores(body, Some(1))
2995      .iter()
2996      .any(|(_, holds)| *holds)
2997    && !crate::latex_constructs::tail_reads_ahead(body)
2998}
2999
3000/// After a raw class, or a raw package read outside a raw class's read, loaded, and K11 and the in-place reroute had
3001/// their pick (apa6.cls's `\affiliation`, a pure store K11 takes at the class's end, after ftnright.sty its read loads;
3002/// 1710.02566): each table setter of an
3003/// affiliation or an address (`displaced_api`) the file defined that stores its argument for the title page without
3004/// printing it (`displaced_setter`) — rstransa.cls:1154 `\gdef\@address{#1\ifaffbreak\relax\end{splitaddress}\fi}`
3005/// (2607.20672), alta.cls:58 `\gdef\alta@affiliation{#1}` (2609.39929), eph.cls:325
3006/// `\def\epl@institute{\epl@institute@makeup{#1}}` (nlin0310001), iau.cls:1515's numbered `\protected@xdef\csname
3007/// affil\the\affilcount\endcsname{#1}` (2510.16758, 2607.25849): a store K11's pure-store test (`pure_stores`, a `\@name`
3008/// store) does not take, which the kernel's `\maketitle` deposit (sect05.rs `\lx@deposit@maketitle`) printed into the
3009/// body under the names, every author with no contact. The setter runs as the file's own (`\lx@class@displaced@<name>`),
3010/// under a meaning recorder (`\lx@displaced@begin`, `\lx@displaced@end`; state.rs `begin_meaning_recorder`, which
3011/// nests, unlike an assignment journal: review rev99b B4) that records, of each macro it changed, the
3012/// part that holds the call's argument (sect05.rs `assigned_parts_holding`): the class's own record of the value, its
3013/// provenance. At `\maketitle` the title page's speculative run decides which of them it prints
3014/// (`route_and_hand_displaced`); those are the creators' contacts through the API, and the deposit takes them out of
3015/// the class's macros (`\lx@replay@records`), never by their text. OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #955.
3016pub fn reroute_displaced_setters(file: &str) -> Result<()> {
3017  let mut rerouted: Vec<&str> = Vec::new();
3018  // (not a setter K11 or the in-place reroute took: theirs, from this file's load or an earlier one's)
3019  let k11 = lookup_string("lx_rerouted_stores");
3020  for (name, _) in STORE_SETTERS {
3021    if displaced_api(name).is_none()
3022      || rerouted.contains(name)
3023      || k11.split(',').any(|n| n == *name)
3024      || lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@class@contact@rerouted@{name}"))).is_some()
3025      || defined_by_a_binding(name)?
3026    {
3027      continue;
3028    }
3029    if !displaced_setter(name) {
3030      continue;
3031    }
3032    let cs = T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"));
3033    Let!(T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@class@displaced@{name}")), cs, Scope::Global);
3034    let body = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!(
3035      "\\lx@displaced@begin\\lx@class@displaced@{name}{{#1}}\\lx@displaced@end{{{name}}}{{#1}}"
3036    )));
3037    // (protected, as the rerouted contact setters are: [`reroute_in_place_contact_setters`])
3038    DefMacro!(cs, convert_latex_args(1, None)?, body, scope => Some(Scope::Global), protected => true);
3039    rerouted.push(name);
3040  }
3041  if !rerouted.is_empty() {
3042    Info!(
3043      "frontmatter",
3044      file,
3045      s!(
3046        "The raw file's setters that store an affiliation or address for its title page record what they store: \\{}",
3047        rerouted.join(", \\")
3048      )
3049    );
3050  }
3051  Ok(())
3052}
3053
3054/// The calls of displaced setters open now (`\lx@displaced@begin` … `\lx@displaced@end`), innermost last: `1` for one
3055/// whose meanings a recorder records (`begin_meaning_recorder`), `0` for one in a replay or a deposit, which records
3056/// nothing. A state value, so a document that stops inside a call leaves none open to the next one a worker converts
3057/// (review rev99b S1).
3058const DISPLACED_OPEN: &str = "lx_displaced_open";
3059
3060/// The state holding the displaced setters' records (`\lx@displaced@end`): each the setter's name, the macro it
3061/// changed, the part of it that holds the call's argument, and the argument.
3062const DISPLACED_RECORDS: &str = "lx_displaced_records";
3063
3064/// A displaced setter's record ([`DISPLACED_RECORDS`]).
3065struct DisplacedRecord {
3066  setter: String,
3067  store:  Token,
3068  part:   Vec<Token>,
3069  arg:    Tokens,
3070}
3071
3072/// The records of the displaced setters' calls whose part still stands in its macro ([`DISPLACED_RECORDS`]): a call
3073/// whose value a later call replaced (`\gdef\@address{…}` twice) is no longer printed by anything.
3074fn displaced_records() -> Vec<DisplacedRecord> {
3075  let Some(Stored::VecDequeStored(records)) = lookup_value(DISPLACED_RECORDS) else {
3076    return Vec::new();
3077  };
3078  let mut out = Vec::new();
3079  for record in &records {
3080    let Stored::VecDequeStored(fields) = record else {
3081      continue;
3082    };
3083    let (
3084      Some(Stored::String(setter)),
3085      Some(Stored::String(store)),
3086      Some(Stored::Tokens(part)),
3087      Some(Stored::Tokens(arg)),
3088    ) = (fields.front(), fields.get(1), fields.get(2), fields.get(3))
3089    else {
3090      continue;
3091    };
3092    let store = T_CS!(&with(*store, ToString::to_string));
3093    let part = part.unlist_ref().to_vec();
3094    let stands = parameterless_body(&store).is_some_and(|body| {
3095      let body = body.unlist_ref();
3096      !part.is_empty()
3097        && body.len() >= part.len()
3098        && body.windows(part.len()).any(|w| w == part.as_slice())
3099    });
3100    if stands {
3101      out.push(DisplacedRecord {
3102        setter: with(*setter, ToString::to_string),
3103        store,
3104        part,
3105        arg: arg.clone(),
3106      });
3107    }
3108  }
3109  out
3110}
3111
3112/// `tokens` without its `\label{…}`s: a label in a title-page store has nothing of ours to name (the replay of a class
3113/// setter gobbles them, sect05.rs `\lx@replay@inert`), and inside a frontmatter entry it asks for an annotation no
3114/// entry answers ("Orphaned frontmatter annotation", review rev98c S3).
3115fn without_labels(tokens: &Tokens) -> Tokens {
3116  let toks = tokens.unlist_ref();
3117  let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(toks.len());
3118  let mut i = 0;
3119  while i < toks.len() {
3120    if toks[i] == T_CS!("\\label") {
3121      let k = skip_spaces(toks, i + 1);
3122      if let Some(past) = tokens_group_end(toks, k) {
3123        i = past;
3124        continue;
3125      }
3126    }
3127    out.push(toks[i]);
3128    i += 1;
3129  }
3130  Tokens::new(out)
3131}
3132
3133/// The entries of a displaced setter's argument: its lines (`\\`, `\par`, `relocate::split_top_level_lines`), an entry
3134/// starting at each line led by a printed label (`relocate::leading_line_label`, the shared reader: `$^{1}$`,
3135/// `\textsuperscript{1}`, `${}^{1}$`, a hand-printed `1)`), an unlabelled line the entry's before it, the unlabelled
3136/// lines before the first label one entry — rstransa's `\address{$^{1}$… \\ $^{2}$…}`, each line an author's;
3137/// 2607.25849's `\affiliation{email: \email{…}\\ $^1$Argelander-Institut …}` —; the whole argument one entry when no
3138/// line leads with a label (one address over two lines). Marks inside one line are the API's to cut.
3139fn displaced_entries(arg: &Tokens, store: Option<&str>) -> Result<Vec<Tokens>> {
3140  use crate::base_utilities::{leading_line_label, split_top_level_lines};
3141  // (an `\and` list the class's title page numbers: entry n labelled n, `class_numbered_institutes` — caosp.sty:519-527's
3142  // `\institutename`, astro-ph9805069; review rev99b B2; its `\and`s by meaning, P4d)
3143  if let Some(numbered) = class_numbered_institutes(arg, store)? {
3144    return Ok(
3145      split_tokens(numbered, vec![T_CS!("\\and").into()])
3146        .into_iter()
3147        .filter(|entry| entry.unlist_ref().iter().any(|t| *t != T_SPACE!()))
3148        .collect(),
3149    );
3150  }
3151  let toks = arg.unlist_ref();
3152  let lines: Vec<&[Token]> = split_top_level_lines(toks)
3153    .into_iter()
3154    .filter(|line| line.iter().any(|t| *t != T_SPACE!()))
3155    .collect();
3156  let labelled = |line: &[Token]| leading_line_label(&Tokens::new(line.to_vec())).is_some();
3157  if lines.len() < 2 || !lines.iter().any(|line| labelled(line)) {
3158    return Ok(vec![arg.clone()]);
3159  }
3160  let mut entries: Vec<Vec<Token>> = Vec::new();
3161  for line in lines {
3162    match entries.last_mut() {
3163      Some(entry) if !labelled(line) => {
3164        entry.push(T_CS!("\\\\"));
3165        entry.extend_from_slice(line);
3166      },
3167      _ => entries.push(line.to_vec()),
3168    }
3169  }
3170  // (each without the spaces around it: the space after a `\\` is no part of the next entry)
3171  Ok(
3172    entries
3173      .into_iter()
3174      .map(|entry| {
3175        let start = entry
3176          .iter()
3177          .position(|t| *t != T_SPACE!())
3178          .unwrap_or(entry.len());
3179        let end = entry
3180          .iter()
3181          .rposition(|t| *t != T_SPACE!())
3182          .map_or(start, |e| e + 1);
3183        Tokens::new(entry[start..end.max(start)].to_vec())
3184      })
3185      .collect(),
3186  )
3187}

3189/// Are the entries of a displaced setter's call in doubt: one leads with a mark ([`is_a_mark`], a control-sequence symbol
3190/// too: `$^\dagger$Deceased`, review rev99b2 F2) no author requests — queued, or placed by the `\maketitle` that reads
3191/// them (`placed_authors_requesting`) — under names showing marks, as an in-place setter's line in doubt stays the
3192/// class's ([`leads_with_unrequested_mark`])?
3193fn displaced_in_doubt(entries: &[Tokens]) -> Result<bool> {
3194  for entry in entries {
3195    if let Some(operand) = leading_mark_operand(entry.unlist_ref())
3196      && authors_show_marks()?
3197      && queued_authors_requesting(&operand)?.is_empty()
3198      && !placed_authors_requesting(&Tokens::new(operand.clone()).to_string())
3199      && is_a_mark(&operand)?
3200      && !requested_as_printed(&operand)?
3201    {
3202      return Ok(true);
3203    }
3204  }
3205  Ok(false)
3206}

3208/// The `\and`-family separators a store's line or argument holds at its own level, by meaning — the binding-callable
3209/// query (`argument_separators`, OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #995, Option C P2-6): a written `\and`, one a macro of the
3210/// document's or the class's holds, a `\let` copy, a `\csname and\endcsname`, never one a box, a table or a note of the
3211/// line holds — each with what it means where the gate asks. `None` where no rehearsal could read them (a call inside one,
3212/// over the queries' allowance, a group left open): the gate then reads the line's spelling, as before P2-6.
3213fn and_separators(line: &[Token]) -> Result<Option<Vec<FmArgSeparator>>> {
3214  argument_separators(&Tokens::new(line.to_vec()))
3215}
3216
3217/// Does a line hold an `\and`-family separator at its own level ([`and_separators`]), whatever it means where the gate
3218/// asks: what it prints is the class's title page's to decide (caosp's numbers, cauinst's `\par`, sifrev's own
3219/// `\split@rg`), so a gate reading one keeps the class's text or tries the class's numbering — no name is parted by it.
3220/// Unread, a literal `\and` anywhere in the line, the spelling the gates read before P2-6.
3221fn holds_and_separators(line: &[Token]) -> Result<bool> {
3222  Ok(match and_separators(line)? {
3223    Some(separators) => !separators.is_empty(),
3224    None => line.contains(&T_CS!("\\and")),
3225  })
3226}

3228/// Does a contact line hold an `\and` list the class's title page does not number (`class_numbered_institutes` reads one
3229/// that it does): the class's own entry markup, which the kernel's `\and` prints " and " — a call in doubt, the class's
3230/// line (`displaced_argument_in_doubt`; [`kernel_and_list`]; review rev99b B2: astro-ph9805069's
3231/// four institutes as one contact with "and" written in; sifrev.cls's `\institute`, its own `\split@rg` over `\and`,
3232/// 1704.03333). The separators by meaning ([`holds_and_separators`]: held in a macro, a `\let` copy, P2-6).
3233fn unnumbered_and_list(line: &[Token], store: Option<&str>) -> Result<bool> {
3234  Ok(
3235    holds_and_separators(line)?
3236      && class_numbered_institutes(&Tokens::new(line.to_vec()), store)?.is_none(),
3237  )
3238}
3239
3240/// [`unnumbered_and_list`], for a line a rerouted in-place, stored or run-in setter's call hands the API where it is
3241/// called ([`class_contact`], [`class_contact_run_in`]): only where a separator of the line is the kernel's own `\and`
3242/// (sect05.rs's constructor, " and ", which invents the word) by its meaning where the setter is called
3243/// ([`and_separators`]: `FmSepMeaning::Kernel` — a `\let` copy of it and one a macro holds too, trap T8 of the P2 plan:
3244/// the rehearsal reads every `\and` as a separator, so the gate asks what it means) — a class or a binding defining
3245/// `\and` for its lines has the API print that (smfart.cls's `\address{Nazi Boni University, … \and Université …}`,
3246/// "&", 2508.07946). Unread ([`and_separators`]'s `None`), the spelling and the meaning of `\and` itself, as before P2-6:
3247/// a literal `\and` while `\and` is any pool constructor.
3248fn kernel_and_list(line: &[Token]) -> Result<bool> {
3249  let kernel = match and_separators(line)? {
3250    Some(separators) => separators
3251      .iter()
3252      .any(|separator| separator.meaning == FmSepMeaning::Kernel),
3253    None => {
3254      line.contains(&T_CS!("\\and"))
3255        && matches!(lookup_meaning(&T_CS!("\\and")), Some(Stored::Constructor(defn))
3256          if matches!(defn.origin, DefinitionOrigin::Pool | DefinitionOrigin::Unknown))
3257    },
3258  };
3259  Ok(kernel && class_numbered_institutes(&Tokens::new(line.to_vec()), None)?.is_none())
3260}
3261
3262/// Does a displaced setter's argument hold the class's own structure, which the API cannot read as an entry's text: a
3263/// frontmatter setter of its own (a table name, [`IN_PLACE_CONTACT_SETTERS`]' `\email`, a `\thanks` or `\footnote`:
3264/// 2607.25849's `\affiliation{email: \email{…}\\ $^1$…}`; a `\footnotemark[n]` the lines are led by, which pairs them with
3265/// the names' own `\footnotemark[n]` — simauth.cls:190's `\address`, 1603.07668, whose two lines went to all three
3266/// authors, review rev99b B1), or a macro of the raw files' with parameters other than a
3267/// mark the readers read ([`leading_mark`]'s `\inst`, `\textsuperscript`) — cta-author.cls's `\address{\add{1}{…}
3268/// \add{2}{…}\email{…}}`, whose entries and marks only its `\add` makes (2307.11112: routed whole, the three authors were
3269/// read as one)? Then the call is in doubt and stays the class's text.
3270fn displaced_argument_in_doubt(
3271  arg: &Tokens,
3272  store: Option<&str>,
3273  redefined: &[Token],
3274) -> Result<bool> {
3275  // (the class's own entry separators: an `\and` list its title page does not number — `class_numbered_institutes`
3276  // reads one that it does —, whose `\and` the kernel prints " and " (astro-ph9805069's four institutes as one
3277  // contact of every author, "and" written three times; sifrev.cls's own `\split@rg`, 1704.03333; review rev99b B2), and
3278  // a `\\` or `\par` the title page defines anew where it prints the store (`separators_redefined`))
3279  let toks = arg.unlist_ref();
3280  if unnumbered_and_list(toks, store)? || redefined.iter().any(|separator| toks.contains(separator))
3281  {
3282    return Ok(true);
3283  }
3284  Ok(toks.iter().any(|t| {
3285    if t.get_catcode() != Catcode::CS
3286      || t.with_cs_name(|n| matches!(n, "\\inst" | "\\textsuperscript" | "\\@textsuperscript"))
3287    {
3288      return false;
3289    }
3290    let setter = t.with_cs_name(|n| {
3291      let bare = n.trim_start_matches('\\');
3292      STORE_SETTERS.iter().any(|(name, _)| *name == bare)
3293        || IN_PLACE_CONTACT_SETTERS
3294          .iter()
3295          .any(|(name, _)| *name == bare)
3296        || matches!(
3297          n,
3298          "\\thanks" | "\\footnote" | "\\footnotetext" | "\\footnotemark"
3299        )
3300    });
3301    setter
3302      || matches!(lookup_meaning(t), Some(Stored::Expandable(d))
3303        if d.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File
3304          && d.get_parameters().is_some_and(|p| !p.get_parameters().is_empty()))
3305  }))
3306}
3307
3308/// At `\maketitle` (`\lx@store@defaults`, before K11's stores are handed on — an editor store makes its creator after
3309/// the authors' contacts, `harvest_stores` — and before the title-page deposit): the displaced setters' records
3310/// ([`displaced_records`]) whose macro the class's whole title page prints, by its speculative sentinel runs
3311/// (`sentinel_runs` of `title_page_print_tokens`, the two-sentinel rule's confirming run for a plain value,
3312/// [`confirmed_printed`]), are routed ([`route_displaced`]) and handed on ([`hand_displaced_stores`]). Without a run made,
3313/// nothing is: the class's text stays where its title page prints it. Once.
3314fn route_and_hand_displaced() -> Result<Vec<Digested>> {
3315  if lookup_bool("lx_displaced_decided")
3316    || lookup_bool("lx_depositing_class_maketitle")
3317    || lookup_bool("lx_replaying_class_setter")
3318  {
3319    return Ok(Vec::new());
3320  }
3321  let records = displaced_records();
3322  if records.is_empty() || !has_author_creators() {
3323    return Ok(Vec::new());
3324  }
3325  assign_value("lx_displaced_decided", true, Some(Scope::Global));
3326  let mut stores: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
3327  for record in &records {
3328    if !stores.contains(&record.store) {
3329      stores.push(record.store);
3330    }
3331  }
3332  use crate::latex_constructs::title_page_print_tokens;
3333  let Some((printed, letters)) = sentinel_runs(&stores, &title_page_print_tokens, true, false)?
3334  else {
3335    return Ok(Vec::new());
3336  };
3337  let printed = if printed.split(',').any(|name| {
3338    stores
3339      .iter()
3340      .find(|t| t.with_str(|cs| cs == name))
3341      .and_then(parameterless_body)
3342      .as_ref()
3343      .and_then(plain_value_text)
3344      .is_some()
3345  }) {
3346    // (the title-page reading's text, its run with the document's own values)
3347    let reading = crate::latex_constructs::title_page_reading::title_page_reading()?;
3348    match reading.outcome {
3349      crate::latex_constructs::title_page_reading::RunOutcome::Printed => {
3350        confirmed_printed(&stores, &printed, &letters, &reading.text)
3351      },
3352      _ => printed,
3353    }
3354  } else {
3355    printed
3356  };
3357  DebugFeature!(
3358    "frontmatter",
3359    "FRONT displaced stores printed by the title page: [{printed}] of {}",
3360    stores.len()
3361  );
3362  route_displaced(records, &printed)?;
3363  hand_displaced_stores()
3364}
3365
3366/// Does a displaced setter's store stay the class's by the rules K11 applies to its own stores ([`store_setter_body`],
3367/// [`reroute_raw_class_stores`]) — the pure-store test of its body aside, which is what the displaced route loosens: a
3368/// macro of the raw files' that is no title-page code reads it ([`ClassReaders::read_by_other_code`]), or the title page
3369/// prints it glued to the class's text or in one line with another value the frontmatter does not carry
3370/// ([`ClassReaders::store_lines`], judged with the document's values: uiucthesis.cls:177 `for the degree of \@degree\ in
3371/// \@dept\\`, zzfmd's `{\@jinfo, \@address\par}`)? A store named by computation (`\csname affil\the\affilcount
3372/// \endcsname`) has no static reader, and none of these holds for it.
3373fn displaced_store_stays_the_classs(
3374  store: &Token,
3375  readers: &ClassReaders,
3376  carried: &[Token],
3377) -> bool {
3378  if readers.read_by_other_code(store) {
3379    return true;
3380  }
3381  let lines = readers.store_lines(store, &|t: &Token| carried.contains(t));
3382  lines.glued || lines.others.iter().any(prints_as_a_value)
3383}
3384
3385/// The displaced setters' records whose macro the title page prints (`printed`, the names of the printed stores) are
3386/// routed ([`route_and_hand_displaced`]) — the deposit and the replay take each part out of its macro
3387/// (`lx_replay_records`, sect05.rs `\lx@replay@records`), and [`hand_displaced_stores`] hands each call's argument to
3388/// the API — while K11's reader and line rules leave the store to them ([`displaced_store_stays_the_classs`]) and no
3389/// entry is in doubt ([`displaced_in_doubt`], [`displaced_argument_in_doubt`]); the rest stay the class's text.
3390fn route_displaced(records: Vec<DisplacedRecord>, printed: &str) -> Result<()> {
3391  let printed: Vec<&str> = printed.split(',').filter(|n| !n.is_empty()).collect();
3392  let mut replay = match lookup_value("lx_replay_records") {
3393    Some(Stored::VecDequeStored(records)) => records,
3394    _ => VecDeque::new(),
3395  };
3396  let mut routed = VecDeque::new();
3397  let readers = ClassReaders::survey();
3398  let mut carried = frontmatter_owned_stores();
3399  carried.extend(records.iter().map(|r| r.store));
3400  for record in records {
3401    if !record.store.with_str(|cs| printed.contains(&cs)) {
3402      continue;
3403    }
3404    if displaced_store_stays_the_classs(&record.store, &readers, &carried) {
3405      Info!(
3406        "frontmatter",
3407        s!("\\{}", record.setter),
3408        s!(
3409          "\\{} stays the class's: other code of the class reads its store, or its title page prints it in one line with another value",
3410          record.setter
3411        )
3412      );
3413      continue;
3414    }
3415    let store = record.store.with_str(ToString::to_string);
3416    // (an `\and`-family separator by meaning, `holds_and_separators`: whether the class's title page numbers the entries)
3417    if holds_and_separators(record.arg.unlist_ref())? {
3418      note_and_numbering(&record.store)?;
3419    }
3420    let entries = displaced_entries(&record.arg, Some(&store))?;
3421    let redefined = readers.separators_redefined(&record.store, &[T_CS!("\\\\"), T_CS!("\\par")]);
3422    if displaced_in_doubt(&entries)?
3423      || displaced_argument_in_doubt(&record.arg, Some(&store), &redefined)?
3424    {
3425      Info!(
3426        "frontmatter",
3427        s!("\\{}", record.setter),
3428        s!(
3429          "\\{} leads with a mark no author requests, or holds the class's own entry markup: its line stays where the class's title page prints it",
3430          record.setter
3431        )
3432      );
3433      continue;
3434    }
3435    replay.push_back(Stored::VecDequeStored(VecDeque::from([
3436      Stored::String(pin(record.store.with_str(ToString::to_string))),
3437      Stored::Tokens(Tokens::new(record.part)),
3438    ])));
3439    routed.push_back(Stored::VecDequeStored(VecDeque::from([
3440      Stored::String(pin(record.setter)),
3441      Stored::Tokens(record.arg),
3442      Stored::String(pin(store)),
3443    ])));
3444  }
3445  if routed.is_empty() {
3446    return Ok(());
3447  }
3448  assign_value(
3449    "lx_replay_records",
3450    Stored::VecDequeStored(replay),
3451    Some(Scope::Global),
3452  );
3453  assign_value(
3454    "lx_displaced_routed",
3455    Stored::VecDequeStored(routed),
3456    Some(Scope::Global),
3457  );
3458  Ok(())
3459}
3460
3461/// Each routed displaced call ([`route_displaced`]) as the API's contacts of the creators, in the order the document
3462/// made them — an entry each ([`displaced_entries`]), through the in-place API of its kind ([`displaced_api`]:
3463/// `\lx@add@affiliation`, `\lx@add@address`), each placed by its leading label ([`contact_line_call`]); `\label`s
3464/// gobbled ([`without_labels`]). Once.
3465fn hand_displaced_stores() -> Result<Vec<Digested>> {
3466  let Some(Stored::VecDequeStored(routed)) = lookup_value("lx_displaced_routed") else {
3467    return Ok(Vec::new());
3468  };
3469  assign_value("lx_displaced_routed", Stored::None, Some(Scope::Global));
3470  let mut calls: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
3471  for record in &routed {
3472    let Stored::VecDequeStored(fields) = record else {
3473      continue;
3474    };
3475    let (Some(Stored::String(setter)), Some(Stored::Tokens(arg))) = (fields.front(), fields.get(1))
3476    else {
3477      continue;
3478    };
3479    let setter = with(*setter, ToString::to_string);
3480    let Some(api) = displaced_api(&setter) else {
3481      continue;
3482    };
3483    let store = match fields.get(2) {
3484      Some(Stored::String(store)) => Some(with(*store, ToString::to_string)),
3485      _ => None,
3486    };
3487    for entry in displaced_entries(&without_labels(arg), store.as_deref())? {
3488      calls.extend(contact_line_call(api, &entry)?.unlist());
3489    }
3490    Info!(
3491      "frontmatter",
3492      s!("\\{setter}"),
3493      s!(
3494        "The class's title page prints what \\{setter} stored: the creators' contacts, taken out of the title page's deposit"
3495      )
3496    );
3497  }
3498  if calls.is_empty() {
3499    return Ok(Vec::new());
3500  }
3501  Ok(vec![digest_store_calls(calls)?])
3502}
3503
3504/// Reroute every table setter the raw class `cls` defined as a store; a binding's own setter
3505/// (acmart.cls's `\copyrightyear`, ported as the class's store) is not the raw class's.
3506pub fn reroute_raw_class_stores(cls: &str) -> Result<()> { reroute_raw_file_stores(cls, false) }
3507
3508/// After a raw package `sty` was read outside a raw class's read (`\lx@package@loaded@raw`; one a raw class loads is
3509/// that class's, rerouted at its end with its title page known): the table setters it defined itself
3510/// (`defined_by_a_raw_file`), as a raw class's are — medima.sty:300-312 redefines elsarticle's bound `\received`,
3511/// `\accepted` (and `\finalform`, `\availableonline`, `\communicated`) as stores only its own refused `\maketitle`
3512/// reads, so the paper's "Article history" was lost (2307.16262; jcomp, prletters, ycviu, yjvci, cag, jasr ship the
3513/// same shape under elsarticle). The class's other K11 passes (its keywords environments, kernel field setters and
3514/// off-table stores) stay the class's.
3515pub fn reroute_raw_package_stores(sty: &str) -> Result<()> { reroute_raw_file_stores(sty, true) }
3516
3517/// [`reroute_raw_class_stores`], or [`reroute_raw_package_stores`] when `package`.
3518fn reroute_raw_file_stores(cls: &str, package: bool) -> Result<()> {
3519  let mut rerouted: Vec<&str> = Vec::new();
3520  let mut readers: Option<ClassReaders> = None;
3521  let mut setters = Vec::new();
3522  let mut toks: Vec<(&str, Vec<Token>)> = Vec::new();
3523  for (name, api) in STORE_SETTERS {
3524    if defined_by_a_binding(name)? || (package && !defined_by_a_raw_file(name)?) {
3525      continue;
3526    }
3527    if let Some(label) = toks_store(name, &mut readers)? {
3528      toks.push((*name, label));
3529      continue;
3530    }
3531    if let Some(setter) = store_setter_body(name, package, &mut readers)? {
3532      setters.push((*name, *api, setter));
3533    }
3534  }
3535  if !toks.is_empty() {
3536    let mut known = lookup_string("lx_toks_stores");
3537    for (name, label) in &toks {
3538      assign_value(
3539        &s!("lx_toks_label_{name}"),
3540        Stored::Tokens(Tokens::new(label.clone())),
3541        Some(Scope::Global),
3542      );
3543      if !known.split(',').any(|n| n == *name) {
3544        if !known.is_empty() {
3545          known.push(',');
3546        }
3547        known.push_str(name);
3548      }
3549    }
3550    assign_value(
3551      "lx_toks_stores",
3552      Stored::String(pin(known)),
3553      Some(Scope::Global),
3554    );
3555    Info!(
3556      "frontmatter",
3557      cls,
3558      s!(
3559        "The raw file's token registers only its printed code prints are the frontmatter's at \\maketitle: \\{}",
3560        toks
3561          .iter()
3562          .map(|(n, _)| *n)
3563          .collect::<Vec<_>>()
3564          .join(", \\")
3565      )
3566    );
3567  }
3568  // What the frontmatter carries: these setters' stores and the kernel's fields, which the deposit nulls.
3569  let carried_stores: Vec<Token> = setters
3570    .iter()
3571    .map(|(_, _, (_, _, store_cs))| *store_cs)
3572    .chain(["\\@title", "\\@author", "\\@date", "\\@thanks"].map(|cs| T_CS!(cs)))
3573    .collect();
3574  let carried = |t: &Token| carried_stores.contains(t);
3575  for (name, api, (store, arg, store_cs)) in setters {
3576    let readers = readers.get_or_insert_with(ClassReaders::survey);
3577    let lines = readers.store_lines(&store_cs, &carried);
3578    if lines.glued || lines.others.iter().any(prints_as_a_value) {
3579      Info!(
3580        "frontmatter",
3581        cls,
3582        s!(
3583          "The raw class's \\{name} stays the class's: its title page prints it in one line with another value"
3584        )
3585      );
3586      continue;
3587    }
3588    // The other values of its lines print nothing as the class leaves them; `\maketitle` judges them again with the
3589    // document's (`harvest_stores`), so a store handed on as it is set waits for it (`lx_store_deferred_<name>`) —
3590    // when one of them is a placeholder the document can fill there ([`may_become_a_value`]), not the line's code
3591    // (`\@setfontsize`, `\@journalfont`; review rev79a2 finding 5).
3592    let others: Vec<Token> = lines
3593      .others
3594      .into_iter()
3595      .filter(may_become_a_value)
3596      .collect();
3597    let line_values = !others.is_empty();
3598    assign_value(
3599      &s!("lx_store_line_values_{name}"),
3600      Stored::Tokens(Tokens::new(others)),
3601      Some(Scope::Global),
3602    );
3603    if line_values && !read_at_maketitle(name) {
3604      assign_value(&s!("lx_store_deferred_{name}"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
3605    }
3606    // A store the document never set is handed on at `\maketitle` only when the title page prints it.
3607    assign_value(
3608      &s!("lx_store_title_page_reads_{name}"),
3609      readers.read_by_the_title_page(&store_cs),
3610      Some(Scope::Global),
3611    );
3612    assign_value(
3613      &s!("lx_store_cs_{name}"),
3614      Stored::String(pin(
3615        store_cs.with_str(|cs| cs.trim_start_matches('\\').to_string()),
3616      )),
3617      Some(Scope::Global),
3618    );
3619    // The setter stores as the class does, so the class's other readers of `\@<name>` (gaceta
3620    // checks `\@editor` for its section editor line) keep seeing the value; only `\@maketitle` is
3621    // discarded. A store that annotates a creator (an affiliation, address, e-mail, ORCID) or makes
3622    // one (an editor, a speaker) is then only recorded: it is read once, at `\maketitle`, where the author list it annotates exists
3623    // (`harvest_stores`; set later, it is handed on at once, `\lx@store@late`). Any other store is
3624    // handed to its frontmatter API as it is set — keywords set after `\maketitle` for an
3625    // abstract that prints them (pittetd.cls:503-527), an abstract at its place in the body —, save
3626    // an abstract or keywords the title page names by its label, which `\maketitle` reads too
3627    // (`labelled_api`, silent-loss #6a).
3628    let (params, arg_ref) = if arg == 2 {
3629      (convert_latex_args(2, Some(Tokens::new(Vec::new())))?, "#2")
3630    } else {
3631      (convert_latex_args(1, None)?, "#1")
3632    };
3633    // The label the class's title page prints before the store ("Resumen: \@resumen") names the entry, and the deposit
3634    // takes it out of the title page with the store (`labelled_api`).
3635    let api = labelled_api(name, api, &store_cs, readers);
3636    let api = api.as_str();
3637    let mut body = store.unlist();
3638    let tail = if read_at_maketitle(name) {
3639      s!("\\lx@store@set{{{name}}}\\lx@store@late{{{name}}}{{{arg_ref}}}")
3640    } else {
3641      s!(
3642        "\\lx@store@set{{{name}}}\\lx@store@hand{{{name}}}{{{}}}",
3643        api.replace("#1", arg_ref)
3644      )
3645    };
3646    body.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(tail)).unlist());
3647    DefMacro!(T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}")), params, Tokens::new(body));
3648    rerouted.push(name);
3649  }
3650  if !package {
3651    reroute_keywords_environments(cls, &mut readers)?;
3652    reroute_kernel_field_setters(cls)?;
3653    survey_off_table_stores(cls, &mut readers)?;
3654  }
3655  if !rerouted.is_empty() {
3656    // A raw class that `\LoadClass`es another raw class reroutes twice: the
3657    // list accumulates, so the base class's stores stay captured too.
3658    let prior = lookup_string("lx_rerouted_stores");
3659    let mut captured: Vec<&str> = prior.split(',').filter(|n| !n.is_empty()).collect();
3660    for name in &rerouted {
3661      if !captured.contains(name) {
3662        captured.push(name);
3663      }
3664    }
3665    // Inside the deposit group a store handed to the frontmatter reads as empty, so a kept
3666    // `\@maketitle` never typesets it twice; one not handed on (an affiliation with no author to
3667    // carry it: courseoutline.cls:150 `\@department` in a document with no `\author`) stays the
3668    // class's to print. One the document never set and the class gives no value before its own
3669    // `\maketitle` — an.cls:532 `\ch@ckobl{institute}{Address(es) of author(s) should be given}`
3670    // runs in the class's `\maketitle`, which the kernel's replaces — is defined: its undefined
3671    // store raised an error that dropped the whole deposit, the paper's `\keywords` line with it
3672    // (0 errors; pdflatex prints the line beside the class's placeholder). As `\relax` or `\@empty`,
3673    // whichever the title page prints fewer characters under in TeX's runs (`decide_unset_nulls`): `\relax`
3674    // where a class tests for the unset store, ptptex.cls:88-104's journal line and "(Received …)"
3675    // printed unless `\@ifundefined{@pubinfo}`/`{@recdate}` (hep-th9803240, 1309.6905); `\@empty`
3676    // where it tests `\ifx\@institute\@empty` after its own `\maketitle` defaulted it so.
3677    DefMacro!("\\lx@captured@stores", sub[_args] {
3678      let names = lookup_string("lx_rerouted_stores");
3679      let mut nulls: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
3680      for name in names.split(',').filter(|n| !n.is_empty()) {
3681        let store = store_of(name);
3682        let null = if lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_handed_{name}")) {
3683          s!("\\let\\{store}\\@empty")
3684        } else if lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_unset_empty_{name}")) {
3685          s!("\\lx@ifdefined\\{store}\\else\\let\\{store}\\@empty\\fi")
3686        } else {
3687          s!("\\lx@ifdefined\\{store}\\else\\let\\{store}\\relax\\fi")
3688        };
3689        nulls.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(null)).unlist());
3690      }
3691      Ok(Tokens::new(nulls))
3692    });
3693    // A store the document never set keeps the class's default (lion-msc.cls:
3694    // 196-203 `\gdef\@affiliation{Huygens-Kamerlingh Onnes Laboratory, …}`),
3695    // which `\@maketitle` typesets; nulled in the deposit, it reached neither
3696    // the frontmatter nor the body. The kernel `\maketitle` hands it to the
3697    // frontmatter (`\lx@store@defaults` in `\lx@maketitle@body`, sect05.rs),
3698    // as pdflatex prints it only there — when the title page prints it
3699    // (`lx_store_title_page_reads_<name>`, set above; 1209.2617's tlp placeholders are its running head's).
3700    assign_value(
3701      "lx_rerouted_stores",
3702      Stored::String(pin(captured.join(","))),
3703      Some(Scope::Global),
3704    );
3705    // The class's `\@maketitle` is NOT discarded here: `\lx@deposit@maketitle`
3706    // (sect05.rs) digests it with the captured stores nulled and keeps the
3707    // result only if it typeset anything — ptptex's (every field captured)
3708    // yields nothing and is dropped; bfhthesis's degree/advisor block beside
3709    // its captured `\@institution` is kept (sweep 93 lost half of two bfh-ci
3710    // manuals' recall to an unconditional discard).
3711    Info!(
3712      "frontmatter",
3713      cls,
3714      s!(
3715        "Rerouted the raw {}'s title-page stores to the frontmatter API: \\{}",
3716        if package { "package" } else { "class" },
3717        rerouted.join(", \\")
3718      )
3719    );
3720  }
3721  Ok(())
3722}
3723
3724/// The text a class prints right before `body[at]` in its line: the letters, other characters, spaces and ties back to
3725/// the line's start — a group's open brace, the body's start, a line break (`\\`, `\par`, `\newline`), a branch's
