3726/// `\else`/`\fi`/`\or`, a `\relax`, a font or size switch, or a parameterless macro of the class's (its `\footerfont`)
3727/// —, without the spaces it starts with; empty when it holds no letter or character, or when what ends it is anything
3728/// else (a test's operand, `\ifdim\wd0>0pt *\the\X`).
3729fn printed_text_before(body: &[Token], at: usize) -> Vec<Token> {
3730  let mut start = at;
3731  while start > 0 {
3732    let t = body[start - 1];
3733    if matches!(
3734      t.get_catcode(),
3735      Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER | Catcode::SPACE
3736    ) || t == T_ACTIVE!('~')
3737    {
3738      start -= 1;
3739      continue;
3740    }
3741    let line_start = t.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN
3742      || cs_in(&t, &["\\\\", "\\newline", "\\else", "\\fi", "\\or"])
3743      || layout(&t)
3744      || (t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS
3745        && parameterless_body(&t).is_some()
3746        && matches!(lookup_meaning(&t), Some(Stored::Expandable(ref d)) if d.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File));
3747    if !line_start {
3748      return Vec::new();
3749    }
3750    break;
3751  }
3752  let text: Vec<Token> = body[start..at]
3753    .iter()
3754    .skip_while(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE)
3755    .map(|t| if *t == T_ACTIVE!('~') { T_SPACE!() } else { *t })
3756    .collect();
3757  if text
3758    .iter()
3759    .any(|t| matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER))
3760  {
3761    text
3762  } else {
3763    Vec::new()
3764  }
3765}
3766
3767/// Is the table name `\<name>` a token register a raw file made (`\newtoks\correspondingauthor`, googlecloud.cls:80,
3768/// cmu.cls:113, deepseek.cls:112, nv.cls:180 and 20 more classes of the census: googledeepmind, nvidiatechreport,
3769/// berkeley, instadeep, hku, ds, dm, dmml, …), which the document fills by assignment (`\correspondingauthor{…}`) and
3770/// which only printed code reads ([`ClassReaders::printed_register`])? Its value is then the frontmatter's at
3771/// `\maketitle` (`harvest_stores`), the table's call of it led by the label the class prints before it: the class prints
3772/// it only in the foot of the page style its `\maketitle` selects (`\fancypagestyle{firststyle}`, now `\ps@firststyle`,
3773/// fancyhdr_sty.rs), page furniture LaTeXML does not set, and it was lost with 0 errors (2607.06973, 2608.26053,
3774/// 2604.13010). Beyond Perl, which gives the register no meaning a raw class's copy does not (an undefined
3775/// `\correspondingauthor` Error, its value a body paragraph). Returns the label's tokens.
3776fn toks_store(name: &str, readers: &mut Option<ClassReaders>) -> Result<Option<Vec<Token>>> {
3777  let cs = T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"));
3778  let toks = matches!(lookup_meaning(&cs), Some(Stored::Register(ref r)) if matches!(r.register_type, RegisterType::Tokens));
3779  if !toks || !defined_by_a_raw_file(name)? {
3780    return Ok(None);
3781  }
3782  Ok(
3783    readers
3784      .get_or_insert_with(ClassReaders::survey)
3785      .printed_register(&cs),
3786  )
3787}
3788
3789/// The value of the token register `\<name>` ([`toks_store`]).
3790fn toks_value(name: &str) -> Option<Tokens> {
3791  match lookup_register_quiet(&s!("\\{name}")) {
3792    Some(RegisterValue::Tokens(value)) => Some(value),
3793    _ => None,
3794  }
3795}
3796
3797/// The table's call `api` of a date or a classification named `label` (`name={…}`, joining its options:
3798/// `\lx@add@classification[scheme=…]{#1}`); an empty `label` gives it no name, not its role's default.
3799fn named_call(api: &str, label: &str) -> String {
3800  match api.split_once('[') {
3801    Some((command, rest)) => s!("{command}[name={{{label}}},{rest}"),
3802    None => api.replacen("{#1}", &s!("[name={{{label}}}]{{#1}}"), 1),
3803  }
3804}
3805
3806/// The frontmatter call of the store `\<name>` (the table's `api`), named by the label the class's title page prints
3807/// right before the store ([`ClassReaders::title_page_label`]) when the entry is one shown under a name: an abstract,
3808/// keywords, a classification or a date ("Received~\@received"). rcresumen's `\def\@maketitle{… Resumen: \@resumen}`: the class's abstract is named
3809/// "Resumen:", as astlb.cls:200's in-abstract `Key words:` names its keywords (1202.2889). An abstract or keywords store
3810/// the title page reads, labelled or not, and a labelled classification, is the class's store, which LaTeX expands
3811/// where the class's `\maketitle` prints it: its setter only stores (`lx_store_deferred_<name>`, `\lx@store@hand`) and
3812/// `\maketitle` queues the entry from the store (`harvest_stores`), with no clearing of other entries, digested there
3813/// with the rest — so a value using a macro or package defined later in the preamble reads as pdflatex reads it (review
3814/// probe `early`: "Uses bar and red"), and the document's own `{abstract}`, which clears what is queued, does not remove
3815/// it: the class's store and the document's abstract are two elements (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #154), as pdflatex
3816/// prints both — "Resumen:" and "Abstract", the document's first when its `{abstract}` comes before `\maketitle`; an
3817/// unlabelled `\begin{quote}\itshape\@resumen\end{quote}` too (review probe `d_nolab`; silent-loss #6a). The label's
3818/// tokens are recorded for the title-page deposit (`lx_store_label_part_<name>`), which takes them out with the store
3819/// when it is handed on (`\lx@store@handed`): the label is the entry's name, not a line of its own before the body —
3820/// once `\maketitle`'s run of the title page shows it printed right before the store (`verify_title_page_labels`;
3821/// else the entry is unnamed, `lx_store_api_unnamed_<name>`). A date or a classification with no label read has no name
3822/// — never its role's default the class does not print. The api of any other store is the table's. Returns the api,
3823/// stored for the later calls (`store_api`).
3824fn labelled_api(name: &str, api: &str, store: &Token, readers: &ClassReaders) -> String {
3825  // (`_store`: the class's store's entry, which only another of the same store replaces — the document's own abstract
3826  // under the same name does not, `frontmatter_clear_same_name`, while the title page prints the store,
3827  // `release_unprinted_store_entries`. The store's name, not the setter's: two setters of one store, a class's `\let\keyword
3828  // \keywords` or `\let\resumen\abst`, make one entry — review probes `al1`, `al2`)
3829  let tag = store.with_str(|cs| cs.trim_start_matches('\\').to_string());
3830  let (named, unnamed) = if api.starts_with("\\lx@add@abstract") {
3831    (
3832      Some(s!(
3833        "\\lx@frontmatter@mark{{ltx:abstract}}\\lx@add@frontmatter{{ltx:abstract}}[_store={tag},name={{"
3834      )),
3835      s!(
3836        "\\lx@frontmatter@mark{{ltx:abstract}}\\lx@add@frontmatter{{ltx:abstract}}[_store={tag}]{{#1}}"
3837      ),
3838    )
3839  } else if api.starts_with("\\lx@add@keywords") {
3840    (
3841      Some(s!(
3842        "\\lx@add@frontmatter{{ltx:keywords}}[_store={tag},name={{"
3843      )),
3844      s!("\\lx@add@frontmatter{{ltx:keywords}}[_store={tag}]{{#1}}"),
3845    )
3846  } else if api.starts_with("\\lx@add@classification") || api.starts_with("\\lx@add@date") {
3847    // (a date's label names it, as a classification's does: `Received~\@received\par`, the deposit takes "Received"
3848    // out with the store — it stayed a body paragraph of its own, "Received" over nothing: a raw package's dates under
3849    // article, 2307.16262's medima.sty shape, and a raw class's. Unlabelled, it has no name, not the role's default:
3850    // medima.sty's final-form date printed as "Received in final form" was named "Revised " (FIDELITY_CHARTER
3851    // 2026-10-30, never a role name the class does not print; review rev95d B2) — the defaults stay the bindings'
3852    // own calls', revtex4_support's and aipproc's)
3853    (None, named_call(api, ""))
3854  } else if api.starts_with("\\lx@add@frontmatter{ltx:note}") {
3855    // (a note's printed label leads it, as the class prints it (FIDELITY_CHARTER §4) — in its text, as a token
3856    // register's note's does (`toks_store`): the schema's `ltx:note` has no `name` (LaTeXML-meta.rnc:27-35).
3857    // acl2023.sty:184-187's `\ifx\@corremail\empty\else … Correspondence: \href{mailto:\@corremail}{…\@corremail}\fi`,
3858    // whose test leaves the title page with the store, took "Correspondence:" with it, printed nowhere (2506.10896,
3859    // brief 97b); unlabelled, the table's call)
3860    (None, api.to_string())
3861  } else {
3862    return api.to_string();
3863  };
3864  let deferred = |call: &str| {
3865    assign_value(
3866      &s!("lx_store_api_{name}"),
3867      Stored::String(pin(call)),
3868      Some(Scope::Global),
3869    );
3870    assign_value(&s!("lx_store_deferred_{name}"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
3871  };
3872  // (a date's or a classification's label is read in any code the title page reaches, grown into it or not: jasr.sty's
3873  // `\receivedhead~\@received` is in `\elsarthistorybox`, which its `\MaketitleBox` prints, a macro its uncalled
3874  // `\pprintMaketitle` calls too, 2311.02753; review rev95d-r2)
3875  let label = readers.title_page_label(store).or_else(|| {
3876    named
3877      .is_none()
3878      .then(|| readers.label_in(store, &readers.printed))
3879      .flatten()
3880  });
3881  let Some(StoreLabel {
3882    reader,
3883    part,
3884    text,
3885    in_argument,
3886  }) = label
3887  else {
3888    if named.is_some() && readers.read_by_the_title_page(store) {
3889      deferred(&unnamed);
3890      return unnamed;
3891    }
3892    if named.is_some() {
3893      return api.to_string();
3894    }
3895    // (no label read: the role's default stands where its word is the text the class prints right before the store —
3896    // wiley2sp.cls:296-302's `\receivedname\ignorespaces\@received`, `\def\receivedname{{\normalfont Received:}\ }`,
3897    // which the line reader takes for a value (1211.4450, 1306.2559) —, else the date has no name)
3898    if readers.prints_default_name_before(store, api) {
3899      return api.to_string();
3900    }
3901    // (the unnamed call, for `\maketitle`'s reads of the store too, `store_api`)
3902    assign_value(
3903      &s!("lx_store_api_{name}"),
3904      Stored::String(pin(unnamed.as_str())),
3905      Some(Scope::Global),
3906    );
3907    return unnamed;
3908  };
3909  // (the label as printed, without the space before the store)
3910  let label = text.to_string().trim_end().to_string();
3911  // A date printed inside a parenthesis the label opens — jpsj2.cls's and ptptex.cls's `(Received \@recdate)` — is no
3912  // labelled date: "(Received" names nothing, and the deposit would keep the ")" alone. It keeps the role's name.
3913  let opens_a_bracket = |label: &str| {
3914    let (mut round, mut square) = (0i32, 0i32);
3915    for c in label.chars() {
3916      match c {
3917        '(' => round += 1,
3918        ')' => round -= 1,
3919        '[' => square += 1,
3920        ']' => square -= 1,
3921        _ => {},
3922      }
3923    }
3924    round > 0 || square > 0
3925  };
3926  if api.starts_with("\\lx@add@date") && opens_a_bracket(&label) {
3927    return api.to_string();
3928  }
3929  let call = match named {
3930    Some(head) => s!("{head}{label}}}]{{#1}}"),
3931    None if api.starts_with("\\lx@add@frontmatter{ltx:note}") => {
3932      // (`\space`: the call's text is tokenized up to `#1`, where a trailing source space is no token)
3933      api.replacen("{#1}", &s!("{{{label}\\space #1}}"), 1)
3934    },
3935    None => named_call(api, &label),
3936  };
3937  assign_value(
3938    &s!("lx_store_label_reader_{name}"),
3939    Stored::String(pin(reader)),
3940    Some(Scope::Global),
3941  );
3942  assign_value(
3943    &s!("lx_store_label_part_{name}"),
3944    Stored::Tokens(part),
3945    Some(Scope::Global),
3946  );
3947  assign_value(
3948    &s!("lx_store_label_{name}"),
3949    Stored::Tokens(text),
3950    Some(Scope::Global),
3951  );
3952  assign_value(
3953    &s!("lx_store_label_in_argument_{name}"),
3954    in_argument,
3955    Some(Scope::Global),
3956  );
3957  assign_value(
3958    &s!("lx_store_api_unnamed_{name}"),
3959    Stored::String(pin(unnamed.as_str())),
3960    Some(Scope::Global),
3961  );
3962  deferred(&call);
3963  call
3964}
3965
3966/// The store `\<name>` is handed to the frontmatter: the title-page deposit reads it as empty
3967/// (`\lx@captured@stores`), and the label its title page prints before it ([`labelled_api`]), now the entry's name,
3968/// is taken out with it, once (`note_title_page_part`, read by `\lx@replay@records`).
3969fn note_store_handed(name: &str) {
3970  assign_value(&s!("lx_store_handed_{name}"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
3971  if let Some(Stored::Tokens(part)) = lookup_value(&s!("lx_store_label_part_{name}"))
3972    && !lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_label_noted_{name}"))
3973  {
3974    assign_value(
3975      &s!("lx_store_label_noted_{name}"),
3976      true,
3977      Some(Scope::Global),
3978    );
3979    crate::latex_constructs::note_title_page_part(
3980      &lookup_string(&s!("lx_store_label_reader_{name}")),
3981      part,
3982    );
3983  }
3984}
3985
3986/// The store `\<name>` set after `\maketitle` read the stores was handed on as it was set (`\lx@store@late`,
3987/// `\lx@store@hand`): a harvest re-armed for a superseding `\maketitle` does not hand it again (`lx_stores_late`).
3988fn note_store_late(name: &str) {
3989  let late = lookup_string("lx_stores_late");
3990  if !late.split(',').any(|n| n == name) {
3991    let late = if late.is_empty() {
3992      name.to_string()
3993    } else {
3994      s!("{late},{name}")
3995    };
3996    assign_value(
3997      "lx_stores_late",
3998      Stored::String(pin(late)),
3999      Some(Scope::Global),
4000    );
4001  }
4002}
4003
4004/// The frontmatter call of the store `\<name>`: the class's label's ([`labelled_api`]) or the table's.
4005fn store_api(name: &str) -> Option<String> {
4006  let named = lookup_string(&s!("lx_store_api_{name}"));
4007  if !named.is_empty() {
4008    return Some(named);
4009  }
4010  STORE_SETTERS
4011    .iter()
4012    .find(|(n, _)| *n == name)
4013    .map(|(_, api)| api.to_string())
4014}
4015
4016/// The kernel's title fields, which LaTeX's `\@maketitle` prints (article.cls:209-229) and the title-page deposit and
4017/// replay read as empty (`\lx@deposit@maketitle`, sect05.rs).
4018const KERNEL_FIELDS: [&str; 4] = ["\\@title", "\\@author", "\\@date", "\\@thanks"];
4019
4020/// Silent-loss #5 (a), the generic rule for the class's off-table stores (75a row (a)): a one-argument setter the raw
4021/// class defined, not in the table (`STORE_SETTERS`) and not one of its commands with an `@`, whose body is a pure store
4022/// of its argument ([`setter_stores`]) in one control sequence of the class's that only its title-page code reads
4023/// (rule (a)'s [`ClassReaders::read_only_by_the_title_page`], the class's own `\@title`/`\@author`/`\@date` counted
4024/// as title-page code, LaTeX's `\@maketitle` printing them): jfpc.cls:121-132 `\titre`, `\auteurs`,
4025/// `\institutions`, `\mels`, read only by its `\def\@title`/`\def\@author` (:176-191; 1503.05530), which the
4026/// deposit reads as empty, so the authors were lost; risethesis's cover fields (2208.03566), vmsta's `\fnm`. Each is
4027/// recorded with its class default (`lx_offtable_stores`); at `\maketitle` `\lx@store@untypeset` hands on the value
4028/// the document gave one whose title-page code did not typeset it.
4029fn survey_off_table_stores(cls: &str, readers: &mut Option<ClassReaders>) -> Result<()> {
4030  let from_a_file = |stored: &Stored| match stored {
4031    Stored::Expandable(defn) => defn.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File,
4032    _ => false,
4033  };
4034  let mut found: Vec<(String, Token)> = Vec::new();
4035  let mut names: Vec<String> = current_meanings_where(from_a_file)
4036    .into_iter()
4037    .map(|(key, _)| with(key, ToString::to_string))
4038    .filter(|name| {
4039      name.len() > 1 && name.starts_with('\\') && name[1..].chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphabetic())
4040    })
4041    .collect();
4042  names.sort();
4043  let known = lookup_string("lx_offtable_stores");
4044  for cs_name in names {
4045    let name = &cs_name[1..];
4046    if STORE_SETTERS.iter().any(|(n, _)| *n == name)
4047      || ["title", "author", "date", "thanks", "maketitle", "and"].contains(&name)
4048      || known.split(',').any(|n| n == name)
4049    {
4050      continue;
4051    }
4052    let Some((_, _, stores)) = setter_stores(&T_CS!(&cs_name))? else {
4053      continue;
4054    };
4055    let [store] = stores[..] else {
4056      continue;
4057    };
4058    let class_owned = match lookup_meaning(&store) {
4059      None => true,
4060      Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) => defn.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File,
4061      Some(_) => false,
4062    };
4063    if !class_owned || store.with_str(|s| KERNEL_FIELDS.contains(&s)) {
4064      continue;
4065    }
4066    let readers = readers.get_or_insert_with(ClassReaders::survey);
4067    if readers.read_only_by_the_title_page_or_fields(&store) {
4068      found.push((name.to_string(), store));
4069    }
4070  }
4071  if found.is_empty() {
4072    return Ok(());
4073  }
4074  let mut list: Vec<String> = known
4075    .split(',')
4076    .filter(|n| !n.is_empty())
4077    .map(String::from)
4078    .collect();
4079  for (name, store) in &found {
4080    let default = match lookup_meaning(store) {
4081      Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) => match defn.get_expansion() {
4082        Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) => Stored::Tokens(body.clone()),
4083        _ => Stored::Tokens(Tokens::default()),
4084      },
4085      _ => Stored::Bool(false),
4086    };
4087    assign_value(
4088      &s!("lx_offtable_default_{name}"),
4089      default,
4090      Some(Scope::Global),
4091    );
4092    assign_value(
4093      &s!("lx_offtable_store_{name}"),
4094      Stored::Token(*store),
4095      Some(Scope::Global),
4096    );
4097    if let Some(StoreLabel {
4098      part, text: label, in_argument, ..
4099    }) = readers
4100      .as_ref()
4101      .and_then(|r| r.label_in(store, &r.title_page_fields))
4102    {
4103      assign_value(
4104        &s!("lx_offtable_label_in_argument_{name}"),
4105        in_argument,
4106        Some(Scope::Global),
4107      );
4108      // (with the space the title page prints between the label and the store, a tie in the note's name, as
4109      // acmart_cls.rs:178's `name={Price:~}`: jfpc.cls:177 "Actes JFPC \@lannee", "Actes JFPC 2015")
4110      let mut label = label.unlist();
4111      let separated = part.unlist_ref().windows(2).any(|pair| {
4112        pair[1] == *store
4113          && (pair[0].get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE
4114            || pair[0].get_catcode() == Catcode::ACTIVE
4115            || pair[0] == T_CS!("\\ "))
4116      });
4117      if separated
4118        && label
4119          .last()
4120          .is_some_and(|t| t.get_catcode() != Catcode::SPACE)
4121      {
4122        label.push(T_ACTIVE!('~'));
4123      }
4124      assign_value(
4125        &s!("lx_offtable_label_{name}"),
4126        Stored::Tokens(Tokens::new(label)),
4127        Some(Scope::Global),
4128      );
4129    }
4130    list.push(name.clone());
4131  }
4132  // (the stores the class's own `\@author` reads, in its order, for `hand_untypeset_stores`)
4133  let author_reads: Vec<String> = file_body(&T_CS!("\\@author"))
4134    .map(|body| {
4135      let mut reads: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
4136      for t in body.unlist_ref() {
4137        if found.iter().any(|(_, store)| store == t) {
4138          let cs = t.with_str(ToString::to_string);
4139          if !reads.contains(&cs) {
4140            reads.push(cs);
4141          }
4142        }
4143      }
4144      reads
4145    })
4146    .unwrap_or_default();
4147  assign_value(
4148    "lx_offtable_author_reads",
4149    Stored::String(pin(author_reads.join(","))),
4150    Some(Scope::Global),
4151  );
4152  assign_value(
4153    "lx_offtable_stores",
4154    Stored::String(pin(list.join(","))),
4155    Some(Scope::Global),
4156  );
4157  Info!(
4158    "frontmatter",
4159    cls,
4160    s!(
4161      "The raw class's title-page stores outside the table, handed on at \\maketitle when its title page does not typeset them: \\{}",
4162      found
4163        .iter()
4164        .map(|(n, _)| n.as_str())
4165        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
4166        .join(", \\")
4167    )
4168  );
4169  Ok(())
4170}
4171
4172/// `body` without what it stores and does not run (tex.web §473): the parameter and replacement texts of the definitions
4173/// it makes (`\def`-family, LaTeX's `\newcommand` family, `\g@addto@macro`), and the page furniture it sets
4174/// (`\markboth`, `\markright`, `\@mkboth`; the running heads LaTeXML drops), and what it puts in a box or measures
4175/// (`\setbox`, `\sbox`, `\settowidth`, …), which prints only where the box is used: degruyter-journal-a.cls:574-586
4176/// `\def\@headlineauthor{\xprint{\@nameshortone}…}` and acmlarge.cls:1021 `\def\@firstfoot{…\@acmVolume…}` (1704.03582,
4177/// 1508.03599) print their stores in a running head, not on the title page.
4178fn executed_code(body: &[Token]) -> Vec<Token> {
4179  const DEFS: &[&str] = &[
4180    "\\def",
4181    "\\gdef",
4182    "\\edef",
4183    "\\xdef",
4184    "\\protected@edef",
4185    "\\protected@xdef",
4186  ];
4187  const COMMANDS: &[&str] = &[
4188    "\\newcommand",
4189    "\\renewcommand",
4190    "\\providecommand",
4191    "\\DeclareRobustCommand",
4192  ];
4193  // (with the boxes it fills and the material it measures, which print only if used: degruyter-journal-a.cls:620
4194  // `\global\setbox\shorttitrun=\hbox{…\@headlinetitle…}`, measured for its running head)
4195  const FURNITURE: &[(&str, usize)] = &[
4196    ("\\markboth", 2),
4197    ("\\markright", 1),
4198    ("\\@mkboth", 2),
4199    ("\\g@addto@macro", 2),
4200    ("\\sbox", 2),
4201    ("\\savebox", 2),
4202    ("\\settowidth", 2),
4203    ("\\settoheight", 2),
4204    ("\\settodepth", 2),
4205    ("\\settototalheight", 2),
4206  ];
4207  // the index past one argument at `k`: a group or a single token
4208  let argument = |k: usize| -> usize {
4209    let k = skip_spaces(body, k);
4210    tokens_group_end(body, k).unwrap_or((k + 1).min(body.len()))
4211  };
4212  let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(body.len());
4213  let mut i = 0;
4214  while i < body.len() {
4215    let t = body[i];
4216    if cs_in(&t, DEFS) {
4217      // the definer and its name stay (the name is written, not read); the parameter text and the replacement text go
4218      out.push(t);
4219      if let Some(name) = body.get(skip_spaces(body, i + 1)) {
4220        out.push(*name);
4221      }
4222      let mut k = i + 1;
4223      while k < body.len() && body[k].get_catcode() != Catcode::BEGIN {
4224        k += 1;
4225      }
4226      i = tokens_group_end(body, k).unwrap_or(body.len());
4227      continue;
4228    }
4229    if cs_in(&t, COMMANDS) {
4230      let mut k = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
4231      if body.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("*")) {
4232        k += 1;
4233      }
4234      k = argument(k);
4235      for _ in 0..2 {
4236        let j = skip_spaces(body, k);
4237        if body.get(j) == Some(&T_OTHER!("[")) {
4238          k = body[j..]
4239            .iter()
4240            .position(|b| *b == T_OTHER!("]"))
4241            .map_or(body.len(), |p| j + p + 1);
4242        }
4243      }
4244      i = argument(k);
4245      continue;
4246    }
4247    if let Some((_, n)) = FURNITURE.iter().find(|(cs, _)| cs_in(&t, &[cs])) {
4248      let mut k = i + 1;
4249      for _ in 0..*n {
4250        k = argument(k);
4251        // (`\savebox{\x}[<width>][<pos>]{…}`)
4252        while body.get(skip_spaces(body, k)) == Some(&T_OTHER!("[")) {
4253          let j = skip_spaces(body, k);
4254          k = body[j..]
4255            .iter()
4256            .position(|b| *b == T_OTHER!("]"))
4257            .map_or(body.len(), |p| j + p + 1);
4258        }
4259      }
4260      i = k;
4261      continue;
4262    }
4263    if t == T_CS!("\\setbox") {
4264      // `\setbox<register>=\hbox{…}`: up to the box's group, and the group
4265      let mut k = i + 1;
4266      while k < body.len() && body[k].get_catcode() != Catcode::BEGIN {
4267        k += 1;
4268      }
4269      i = tokens_group_end(body, k).unwrap_or(body.len());
4270      continue;
4271    }
4272    out.push(t);
4273    i += 1;
4274  }
4275  out
4276}
4277
4278/// What the live code (`reads_of_branches`, the switches as they are now) reached from `roots` reads, in the order it
4279/// reads it (depth first, each control sequence once), following the macros a raw file defined and never entering
4280/// `blocked` (the fields a title-page deposit reads as empty); with `executed`, only the code that runs
4281/// ([`executed_code`]).
4282fn live_read_order(roots: &[Token], blocked: &[Token], executed: bool) -> Vec<SymStr> {
4283  struct Walk<'a> {
4284    blocked:  &'a [Token],
4285    executed: bool,
4286    seen:     FxHashSet<SymStr>,
4287    order:    Vec<SymStr>,
4288  }
4289  impl Walk<'_> {
4290    fn visit(&mut self, body: &Tokens, depth: usize) {
4291      let code = if self.executed {
4292        executed_code(body.unlist_ref())
4293      } else {
4294        body.unlist_ref().to_vec()
4295      };
4296      for cs in reads_of_branches(&code, true) {
4297        if self.blocked.contains(&cs) || !self.seen.insert(cs.get_cs_name()) {
4298          continue;
4299        }
4300        self.order.push(cs.get_cs_name());
4301        if depth < 64
4302          && let Some(callee) = file_body(&cs)
4303        {
4304          self.visit(&callee, depth + 1);
4305        }
4306      }
4307    }
4308  }
4309  let mut walk = Walk {
4310    blocked,
4311    executed,
4312    seen: FxHashSet::default(),
4313    order: Vec::new(),
4314  };
4315  for root in roots {
4316    if blocked.contains(root) || !walk.seen.insert(root.get_cs_name()) {
4317      continue;
4318    }
4319    walk.order.push(root.get_cs_name());
4320    let body = if *root == T_CS!(CLASS_AUTHOR_ROOT) {
4321      crate::latex_constructs::class_author_reader_body()
4322    } else {
4323      file_body(root)
4324    };
4325    if let Some(body) = body {
4326      walk.visit(&body, 0);
4327    }
4328  }
4329  walk.order
4330}
4331
4332/// The token body of `cs` when a raw file defined it.
4333fn file_body(cs: &Token) -> Option<Tokens> { body_from(cs, &[DefinitionOrigin::File]) }
4334
4335/// The token body of `cs` when a definition of one of the `origins` made it.
4336fn body_from(cs: &Token, origins: &[DefinitionOrigin]) -> Option<Tokens> {
4337  match lookup_meaning(cs) {
4338    Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) if origins.contains(&defn.get_origin()) => {
4339      match defn.get_expansion() {
4340        Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) => Some(body.clone()),
4341        _ => None,
4342      }
4343    },
4344    _ => None,
4345  }
4346}
4347
4348/// A sentinel value of the speculative title-page runs ([`before_title_page_runs`]), one per store and kind. `Letters`:
4349/// `LxSentinel`, its index spelled in the letters `a`–`p`, and `zZ` — in mixed case, so that a class's `\MakeUppercase`
4350/// and its `\MakeLowercase` both show (`confirmed_printed`) —, matched lower-cased ([`sentinel_found`]);
4351/// `Digits`: its index in the digits `1`–`8` between `9090` and `0909`. No sentinel is part of another of its kind. A
4352/// store is printed only when both its sentinels reach the text (`sentinels_printed`): a numeric context consumes the
4353/// digits, while TeX's "Missing number" recovery typesets the letters — `\setcounter{page}{\@fp}` (gammas.cls:305,
4354/// kluwer.cls:1049; review probe `num`) prints neither value. A test of the value takes its branch with the sentinel's
4355/// value, not the document's: `\ifnum\@x<100 No.~\@x\fi` with 7 takes the other branch with the digits (the store
4356/// counts as not printed; pdflatex prints "No. 7"), and `\romannumeral\@va` prints neither sentinel (pdflatex: "iii") —
4357/// residuals, OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #767; a store tested against a word the class prints instead,
4358/// `\ifx\@vc\@open OPEN\else\@vc\fi`, is caught by the confirming run with the document's values
4359/// ([`confirmed_printed`]).
4360#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
4361enum Sentinel {
4362  Letters,
4363  Digits,
4364}
4365
4366fn sentinel(i: usize, kind: Sentinel) -> String {
4367  let (base, first) = match kind {
4368    Sentinel::Letters => (16, b'a'),
4369    Sentinel::Digits => (8, b'1'),
4370  };
4371  let mut digits = Vec::new();
4372  let mut n = i;
4373  loop {
4374    digits.push(first + (n % base) as u8);
4375    n /= base;
4376    if n == 0 {
4377      break;
4378    }
4379  }
4380  digits.reverse();
4381  let index = String::from_utf8_lossy(&digits);
4382  match kind {
4383    Sentinel::Letters => s!("LxSentinel{index}zZ"),
4384    Sentinel::Digits => s!("9090{index}0909"),
4385  }
4386}
4387
4388/// The sentinel `i` of `kind` as it is looked for in a run's lower-cased text.
4389fn sentinel_found(i: usize, kind: Sentinel) -> String { sentinel(i, kind).to_lowercase() }
4390
4391/// `\def<store>{<sentinel i>}` for each store, the `i`-th its index; `<store>={<sentinel i>}` for a token register (the
4392/// unclaimed-text net's: sig-alternate.cls:1374-1398 `\tntoks`).
4393fn sentinel_definitions(stores: &[Token], kind: Sentinel) -> Vec<Token> {
4394  let mut defs = Vec::new();
4395  for (i, store) in stores.iter().enumerate() {
4396    if is_token_register(store) {
4397      defs.push(*store);
4398      defs.push(T_OTHER!("="));
4399    } else {
4400      defs.push(T_CS!("\\def"));
4401      defs.push(*store);
4402    }
4403    defs.push(T_BEGIN!());
4404    defs.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(sentinel(i, kind))).unlist());
4405    defs.push(T_END!());
4406  }
4407  defs
4408}
4409
4410/// The stores both of whose sentinels ([`sentinel_definitions`], same order) the two speculative runs of the same
4411/// title-page code printed — `letters` and `digits`, their texts —, in the order the first printed them, as a
4412/// comma-separated list of their names.
4413fn sentinels_printed(stores: &[Token], letters: &str, digits: &str) -> String {
4414  let mut found: Vec<(usize, String)> = stores
4415    .iter()
4416    .enumerate()
4417    .filter(|(i, _)| digits.contains(&sentinel(*i, Sentinel::Digits)))
4418    .filter_map(|(i, store)| {
4419      letters
4420        .find(&sentinel_found(i, Sentinel::Letters))
4421        .map(|at| (at, store.with_str(ToString::to_string)))
4422    })
4423    .collect();
4424  found.sort();
4425  found
4426    .into_iter()
4427    .map(|(_, name)| name)
4428    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
4429    .join(",")
4430}
4431
4432/// The stores the title-page code `build` makes (given the sentinel definitions to make before it runs) prints, each
4433/// given its sentinel of each kind in two speculative runs ([`sentinels_printed`]; the second only when the first
4434/// printed a letters sentinel), with the first run's text in its own case. `None` when a run cannot be made. A run that
4435/// ended in a Fatal of its own ([`SpeculativeRun::Aborted`]) printed nothing when `aborted_printed_nothing` — the run of
4436/// the whole title page: a store is not handed on on the word of a run that died —, else it is no run either: the
4437/// deposit's and the replay's (`None`, so the code walk decides, `walked_title_page_reads`) — a Fatal the sentinel value
4438/// alone caused does not stop the real deposit printing the store, which would then be handed on as well.
4439fn sentinel_runs(
4440  stores: &[Token],
4441  build: &dyn Fn(&[Token]) -> Tokens,
4442  aborted_printed_nothing: bool,
4443  isolated: bool,
4444) -> Result<Option<(String, String)>> {
4445  use crate::latex_constructs::{SpeculativeRun, title_page_probe_run};
4446  let letters = match title_page_probe_run(
4447    build(&sentinel_definitions(stores, Sentinel::Letters)),
4448    isolated,
4449  )? {
4450    SpeculativeRun::Printed(text) => text,
4451    SpeculativeRun::Aborted if aborted_printed_nothing => {
4452      return Ok(Some((String::new(), String::new())));
4453    },
4454    SpeculativeRun::Aborted | SpeculativeRun::Unavailable | SpeculativeRun::OverBudget(_) => {
4455      return Ok(None);
4456    },
4457  };
4458  let lowered = letters.to_lowercase();
4459  if !lowered.contains("lxsentinel") {
4460    return Ok(Some((String::new(), letters)));
4461  }
4462  let digits = match title_page_probe_run(
4463    build(&sentinel_definitions(stores, Sentinel::Digits)),
4464    isolated,
4465  )? {
4466    SpeculativeRun::Printed(text) => text,
4467    SpeculativeRun::Aborted if aborted_printed_nothing => {
4468      return Ok(Some((String::new(), letters)));
4469    },
4470    SpeculativeRun::Aborted | SpeculativeRun::Unavailable | SpeculativeRun::OverBudget(_) => {
4471      return Ok(None);
4472    },
4473  };
4474  Ok(Some((
4475    sentinels_printed(stores, &lowered, &digits),
4476    letters,
4477  )))
4478}
4479
4480/// How many `\and` entries [`note_and_numbering`] gives the store it tries: three, so the second and third entries' numbers
4481/// stand between two sentinels, where no other text of the title page is.
4482const AND_NUMBERING_ENTRIES: usize = 3;
4483
4484/// Whether the class's own title page numbers the `\and` entries of `store` (`\@institute`), recorded once per store as
4485/// `lx_and_numbered_<store>` for `affiliations::class_numbered_institutes` — the class's own output as the provenance of
4486/// the numbering, as the sentinel runs are of what it prints ([`sentinel_runs`]): one speculative run of the whole title
4487/// page ([`title_page_print_tokens`]) with the store set to an `\and` list of letter sentinels ([`sentinel`]), which
4488/// numbers them when it printed each entry right after its number in `\and` order ([`printed_after_their_numbers`]) —
4489/// sab.cls:404-426's `\institutename`, `\def\and{\par\refstepcounter{inst}…$^{\theinst}$\enspace}` (2308.05831,
4490/// 2308.05830; edpsci.cls:327-345, 2501.08874; baaa.cls:590-614, 2206.02830), comsis2.cls:831-836's
4491/// `\stepcounter{@inst}` (2207.09249). Only when the queued authors' marks request numbers
4492/// (`affiliations::numbered_marks_requested`); nothing is recorded when no run could be made or its text does not hold the entries (the class's
4493/// `\institutename` is then read as before).
4494pub(crate) fn note_and_numbering(store: &Token) -> Result<()> {
4495  use crate::latex_constructs::{SpeculativeRun, title_page_print_tokens, title_page_probe_run};
4496  let key = s!("lx_and_numbered_{}", store.with_str(ToString::to_string));
4497  if lookup_value(&key).is_some() || !numbered_marks_requested()? {
4498    return Ok(());
4499  }
4500  let mut defs = vec![T_CS!("\\def"), *store, T_BEGIN!()];
4501  for i in 0..AND_NUMBERING_ENTRIES {
4502    if i > 0 {
4503      defs.extend([T_SPACE!(), T_CS!("\\and"), T_SPACE!()]);
4504    }
4505    defs.extend(
4506      mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(sentinel(i, Sentinel::Letters))).unlist(),
4507    );
4508  }
4509  defs.push(T_END!());
4510  let SpeculativeRun::Printed(text) = title_page_probe_run(title_page_print_tokens(&defs), false)?
4511  else {
4512    return Ok(());
4513  };
4514  let numbered = printed_after_their_numbers(&text.to_lowercase(), AND_NUMBERING_ENTRIES);
4515  DebugFeature!(
4516    "frontmatter",
4517    "FRONT {key}: the title page numbers the store's \\and entries: {numbered:?}"
4518  );
4519  if let Some(numbered) = numbered {
4520    assign_value(&key, Stored::Bool(numbered), Some(Scope::Global));
4521  }
4522  Ok(())
4523}
4524
4525/// Did a run's lower-cased text print the letter sentinels `0..entries` ([`sentinel_found`]) each right after its number
4526/// (`1`, `2`, …, any non-alphanumeric — a space, a `.`, a `)` — between them): the digits ending the text between the
4527/// previous sentinel and this one are its number, for the first one the digits before it ending in `1` (the authors'
4528/// marks may print right before it, "Cole1,2" then "1")? `None` when the text does not hold them all in order: the run
4529/// does not show the store (raa.cls sets its title page in a header box the run's text does not reach, 2003.13998,
4530/// 2511.10805), so it says nothing of its numbering.
4531fn printed_after_their_numbers(text: &str, entries: usize) -> Option<bool> {
4532  let mut from = 0;
4533  let mut numbered = true;
4534  for i in 0..entries {
4535    let found = sentinel_found(i, Sentinel::Letters);
4536    let at = text[from..].find(&found).map(|k| from + k)?;
4537    let before = text[from..at].trim_end_matches(|c: char| !c.is_alphanumeric());
4538    let digits = &before[before.trim_end_matches(|c: char| c.is_ascii_digit()).len()..];
4539    let number = (i + 1).to_string();
4540    numbered &= digits == number || (i == 0 && digits.ends_with(&number));
4541    from = at + found.len();
4542  }
4543  Some(numbered)
4544}
4545
4546/// A store's value as plain text — characters and spaces only, at least one character —, without its spaces; `None`
4547/// for any other value (a macro, an accent, a group), whose printed form is not its tokens.
4548fn plain_value_text(value: &Tokens) -> Option<String> {
4549  let mut text = String::new();
4550  for t in value.unlist_ref() {
4551    match t.get_catcode() {
4552      Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER => text.push_str(&t.to_string()),
4553      Catcode::SPACE => {},
4554      _ => return None,
4555    }
4556  }
4557  (!text.is_empty()).then_some(text)
4558}
4559
4560/// The letters and digits of a text, as the comparisons of source and typeset text read it ([`confirmed_printed`],
4561/// [`printed_right_before`]): what TeX typesets of `'`, ``` `` ``` and `''` is ’, “ and ”, of `--` a dash, and the spaces
4562/// are the layout's — only the alphanumerics are the same on both sides (`O'Brien`, ``` ``Quoted'' words```; review
4563/// probes `q3`, `q4`, `ap`).
4564fn alphanumerics(text: &str) -> String { text.chars().filter(|c| c.is_alphanumeric()).collect() }
4565
4566/// The confirming check of the two-sentinel rule ([`before_title_page_runs`]): of the stores the sentinel runs found
4567/// printed (`printed`, names; `letters`, the letters run's text in its own case), those whose value is plain text
4568/// ([`plain_value_text`]) stay printed only when its letters and digits ([`alphanumerics`]) are in the title page printed
4569/// with the document's own values (`real`) — in their case, unless the run printed the store's mixed-case sentinel in
4570/// another case (a class's `\MakeUppercase` or `\MakeLowercase`). A class that tests the value and prints its own word
4571/// instead, `\ifx\@vc\@open OPEN\else\@vc\fi` with "open" (review probe `w`), printed the letters sentinel, which is no
4572/// "open", but not the value. A value with no letter or digit is not checked.
4573fn confirmed_printed(stores: &[Token], printed: &str, letters: &str, real: &str) -> String {
4574  let real = alphanumerics(real);
4575  let real_lowered = real.to_lowercase();
4576  printed
4577    .split(',')
4578    .filter(|n| !n.is_empty())
4579    .filter(|name| {
4580      let Some(i) = stores.iter().position(|t| t.with_str(|cs| cs == *name)) else {
4581        return true;
4582      };
4583      let Some(value) = parameterless_body(&stores[i])
4584        .as_ref()
4585        .and_then(plain_value_text)
4586        .map(|text| alphanumerics(&text))
4587        .filter(|text| !text.is_empty())
4588      else {
4589        return true;
4590      };
4591      if letters.contains(&sentinel(i, Sentinel::Letters)) {
4592        real.contains(&value)
4593      } else {
4594        real_lowered.contains(&value.to_lowercase())
4595      }
4596    })
4597    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
4598    .join(",")
4599}
4600
4601/// A label's text ([`LabelText`]) as the title page prints it, lower-cased, its letters and digits only
4602/// ([`alphanumerics`]): its characters, and a class macro that is text read as its characters.
4603fn label_plain_text(label: &Tokens) -> String {
4604  let mut out = String::new();
4605  for t in label.unlist_ref() {
4606    match t.get_catcode() {
4607      Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER => out.push_str(&t.to_string()),
4608      Catcode::CS => {
4609        if let Some(body) = parameterless_body(t) {
4610          for e in body.unlist_ref() {
4611            if matches!(e.get_catcode(), Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER) {
4612              out.push_str(&e.to_string());
4613            }
4614          }
4615        }
4616      },
4617      _ => {},
4618    }
4619  }
4620  alphanumerics(&out).to_lowercase()
4621}
4622
4623/// Does `text` (lower-cased) print `label` ([`label_plain_text`]) right before an occurrence of `sentinel`, its letters
4624/// and digits compared ([`alphanumerics`]: the typeset ’ of the class's `Author's keywords:`)?
4625fn printed_right_before(text: &str, sentinel: &str, label: &str) -> bool {
4626  if label.is_empty() {
4627    return false;
4628  }
4629  let mut from = 0;
4630  while let Some(found) = text[from..].find(sentinel) {
4631    let at = from + found;
4632    if alphanumerics(&text[..at]).ends_with(label) {
4633      return true;
4634    }
4635    from = at + sentinel.len();
4636  }
4637  false
4638}
4639
4640/// At `\maketitle`, before its stores are handed on (`\lx@store@defaults`): the labels read off the class's code
4641/// ([`ClassReaders::title_page_label`] — `lx_store_label_<name>` of a rerouted store, `lx_offtable_label_<name>` of an
4642/// off-table one) stay the entries' names only when TeX's output says so: one speculative run of the class's whole
4643/// title page (`title_page_print_tokens`), each labelled store given its letters sentinel, must print the label's text
4644/// right before the store's sentinel ([`printed_right_before`]). A label the run does not print there is none: the URL
4645/// argument's "mailto:" of `\href{mailto:\@vg}{\@vg}`, whose page shows only the address (review probe `ctx3`). Without
4646/// a run (none can be made, or it ended in a Fatal of its own), a label in a group that is a control sequence's argument
4647/// (`in_argument`) is none either. A rejected label stays in the title page, and the entry is unnamed
4648/// (`lx_store_api_unnamed_<name>`). Once per document.
4649fn verify_title_page_labels() -> Result<()> {
4650  use crate::latex_constructs::{SpeculativeRun, title_page_print_tokens, title_page_probe_run};
4651  if lookup_bool("lx_depositing_class_maketitle")
4652    || lookup_bool("lx_replaying_class_setter")
4653    || lookup_bool("lx_title_page_labels_verified")
4654  {
4655    return Ok(());
4656  }
4657  // (whose label: a rerouted setter's name, or an off-table one's; its store; the label's text; in an argument)
4658  let mut labelled: Vec<(bool, String, Token, String, bool)> = Vec::new();
4659  for name in rerouted_names() {
4660    if let Some(Stored::Tokens(label)) = lookup_value(&s!("lx_store_label_{name}")) {
4661      let store = T_CS!(&s!("\\{}", store_of(&name)));
4662      let in_argument = lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_label_in_argument_{name}"));
4663      labelled.push((true, name, store, label_plain_text(&label), in_argument));
4664    }
4665  }
4666  for name in lookup_string("lx_offtable_stores")
4667    .split(',')
4668    .filter(|n| !n.is_empty())
4669  {
4670    if let Some(Stored::Tokens(label)) = lookup_value(&s!("lx_offtable_label_{name}"))
4671      && let Some(Stored::Token(store)) = lookup_value(&s!("lx_offtable_store_{name}"))
4672    {
4673      let in_argument = lookup_bool(&s!("lx_offtable_label_in_argument_{name}"));
4674      labelled.push((
4675        false,
4676        name.to_string(),
4677        store,
4678        label_plain_text(&label),
4679        in_argument,
4680      ));
4681    }
4682  }
4683  if labelled.is_empty() {
4684    return Ok(());
4685  }
4686  assign_value("lx_title_page_labels_verified", true, Some(Scope::Global));
4687  let mut stores: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
4688  for (_, _, store, ..) in &labelled {
4689    if !stores.contains(store) {
4690      stores.push(*store);
4691    }
4692  }
4693  let run = title_page_probe_run(
4694    title_page_print_tokens(&sentinel_definitions(&stores, Sentinel::Letters)),
4695    false,
4696  )?;
4697  let text = match run {
4698    SpeculativeRun::Printed(text) => Some(text.to_lowercase()),
4699    SpeculativeRun::Aborted | SpeculativeRun::Unavailable | SpeculativeRun::OverBudget(_) => None,
4700  };
4701  for (rerouted, name, store, label, in_argument) in labelled {
4702    let kept = match &text {
4703      Some(text) => {
4704        let i = stores.iter().position(|t| *t == store).unwrap_or_default();
4705        printed_right_before(text, &sentinel_found(i, Sentinel::Letters), &label)
4706      },
4707      None => !in_argument,
4708    };
4709    DebugFeature!(
4710      "frontmatter",
4711      "FRONT title-page label of \\{name} ({label}): {}",
4712      if kept {
4713        "printed right before the store"
4714      } else {
4715        "not printed there"
4716      }
4717    );
4718    if kept {
4719      continue;
4720    }
4721    if rerouted {
4722      let unnamed = lookup_string(&s!("lx_store_api_unnamed_{name}"));
4723      if !unnamed.is_empty() {
4724        assign_value(
4725          &s!("lx_store_api_{name}"),
4726          Stored::String(pin(unnamed)),
4727          Some(Scope::Global),
4728        );
4729      }
4730      for key in ["lx_store_label_part_", "lx_store_label_"] {
4731        assign_value(&s!("{key}{name}"), false, Some(Scope::Global));
4732      }
4733    } else {
4734      assign_value(&s!("lx_offtable_label_{name}"), false, Some(Scope::Global));
4735    }
4736    Info!(
4737      "frontmatter",
4738      s!("\\{name}"),
4739      s!(
4740        "The class's title page does not print the label read before the store of \\{name} right before it: the entry has no name"
4741      )
4742    );
4743  }
4744  Ok(())
4745}
4746
4747/// The stores a speculative run recorded (`lx_sentinel_<run>`): their names.
4748fn sentinel_list(run: &str) -> Vec<SymStr> {
4749  lookup_string(&s!("lx_sentinel_{run}"))
4750    .split(',')
4751    .filter(|n| !n.is_empty())
4752    .map(|n| T_CS!(n).get_cs_name())
4753    .collect()
4754}
4755
4756/// The off-table stores ([`survey_off_table_stores`]) the document set — a value no longer the class's default and not
4757/// blank —, once per store (two setters of one store, sigplanconf.cls:939-942 `\copyrightDOI` and its `\let`
4758/// `\copyrightdoi`: one value): each setter's name, its store and the value.
4759fn offtable_candidates() -> Vec<(String, Token, Tokens)> {
4760  let mut out: Vec<(String, Token, Tokens)> = Vec::new();
4761  for name in lookup_string("lx_offtable_stores")
4762    .split(',')
4763    .filter(|n| !n.is_empty())
4764  {
4765    let Some(Stored::Token(store)) = lookup_value(&s!("lx_offtable_store_{name}")) else {
4766      continue;
4767    };
4768    if out.iter().any(|(_, s, _)| *s == store) {
4769      continue;
4770    }
4771    let Some(value) = parameterless_body(&store) else {
4772      continue;
4773    };
4774    let default = match lookup_value(&s!("lx_offtable_default_{name}")) {
4775      Some(Stored::Tokens(default)) => Some(default),
4776      _ => None,
4777    };
4778    if is_blank(&value)
4779      || default
4780        .as_ref()
4781        .is_some_and(|d| d.unlist_ref() == value.unlist_ref())
4782    {
4783      continue;
4784    }
4785    out.push((name.to_string(), store, value));
4786  }
4787  out
4788}
4789
4790/// The rerouted stores ([`reroute_raw_class_stores`]): each setter's name.
4791fn rerouted_names() -> Vec<String> {
4792  lookup_string("lx_rerouted_stores")
4793    .split(',')
4794    .filter(|n| !n.is_empty())
4795    .map(String::from)
4796    .collect()
4797}
4798
4799/// Before the title-page deposit and replay of a raw class's title page (`\lx@deposit@maketitle`, sect05.rs), in the
4800/// state they meet: what the title page prints is decided by TeX, not read off its code. Each candidate store is given
4801/// a sentinel value and the title page is digested speculatively (`title_page_probe_run`: output discarded,
4802/// assignments undone, diagnostics discarded), once with letters sentinels and once with digits ones
4803/// (`sentinel_runs`); a store both of whose sentinels reach the digested text is printed there (a numeric context —
4804/// `\setcounter{page}{\@fp}` — consumes the digits, TeX's recovery typesets the letters). A class's own booleans (sigplanconf.cls:87-90 `\def\@true{TT}`, :1118 `\if \@copyrightwanted
4805/// \@copyrightspace \fi`: 1603.03011 `[preprint,nocopyrightspace]`), its `\newif` switches (aiaa-tc.cls:199-200 under
4806/// `[handcarry]`), its `\@ifundefined` and `\ifx…\@empty` tests and what it only puts in a box or a running head
4807/// (`\setbox`, `\markboth`: 1704.03582, 1508.03599) are then what they are in pdflatex. Three runs:
4808/// - the class's whole title page with the document's values (`title_page_print_tokens`), the off-table stores the
4809///   document set (`offtable_candidates`) and the rerouted stores handed to the frontmatter as candidates
4810///   (`lx_sentinel_printed`): an off-table store is printed there (`hand_untypeset_stores`); a handed one was placed
4811///   by this `\maketitle` (#6b, `\lx@store@hand`);
4812/// - the deposit and the replay, each as it will run (`title_page_deposit_tokens`, `title_page_replay_tokens`),
4813///   the off-table candidates given sentinels (`lx_sentinel_deposit`, `lx_sentinel_replay`): a store either typesets,
4814///   when it is kept, is not handed on a second time.
4815///
4816/// Before them, the value of each rerouted store the document never set that is still undefined
4817/// (`decide_unset_nulls`). When no run can be made (a journal is open, or it is over the read budget, TP3), or the
4818/// deposit's or the replay's ended in a Fatal of its own (`sentinel_runs`), `lx_sentinel_ran` stays false and the
4819/// stores' readers are walked instead (`live_read_order`, `evaluated_switch`). The unclaimed-text net's candidates
4820/// (`net_candidates`) join the same runs, after K11's stores (`lx_sentinel_net_printed`, `_deposit`, `_replay`; with the refused begin codes' run,
4821/// `net_begin_code_tokens`); a run their sentinels make fail is made again without them, and the net then decides
4822/// nothing.
4823pub fn before_title_page_runs() -> Result<()> {
4824  if lookup_bool("lx_depositing_class_maketitle") || lookup_bool("lx_replaying_class_setter") {
4825    return Ok(());
4826  }
4827  assign_value("lx_sentinel_ran", false, Some(Scope::Global));
4828  assign_value("lx_net_ran", false, Some(Scope::Global));
4829  let rerouted = rerouted_names();
4830  let offtable = if lookup_bool("lx_offtable_handed") {
4831    Vec::new()
4832  } else {
4833    offtable_candidates()
4834  };
4835  // (the unclaimed-text net's candidates: [`net_candidates`]; not a macro a displaced setter's call wrote, whose text
4836  // the creators' contacts hold, [`route_and_hand_displaced`])
4837  let mut net = if lookup_bool("lx_net_handed") {
4838    Vec::new()
4839  } else {
4840    net_candidates()
4841  };
4842  let displaced: Vec<Token> = displaced_records().into_iter().map(|r| r.store).collect();
4843  net.retain(|store| !displaced.contains(store));
4844  if rerouted.is_empty() && offtable.is_empty() && net.is_empty() {
4845    return Ok(());
4846  }
4847  if !rerouted.is_empty() || !offtable.is_empty() {
4848    decide_unset_nulls(&rerouted)?;
4849  }
4850  let off_stores: Vec<Token> = offtable.iter().map(|(_, store, _)| *store).collect();
4851  let mut stores = off_stores.clone();
4852  for name in &rerouted {
4853    let store = T_CS!(&s!("\\{}", store_of(name)));
4854    if lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_handed_{name}")) && !stores.contains(&store) {
4855      stores.push(store);
4856    }
4857  }
4858  // (K11's runs are made when it has stores; the net's candidates join the same runs, after them)
4859  let k11 = !stores.is_empty();
4860  if !k11 && net.is_empty() {
4861    return Ok(());
4862  }
4863  let mut all = stores.clone();
4864  let mut off_and_net = off_stores.clone();
4865  for store in &net {
4866    if !all.contains(store) {
4867      all.push(*store);
4868      off_and_net.push(*store);
4869    }
4870  }
4871  use crate::latex_constructs::{
4872    title_page_deposit_tokens, title_page_print_tokens, title_page_replay_tokens,
4873  };
4874  let no_run = || {
4875    Info!(
4876      "frontmatter",
4877      "\\maketitle",
4878      "No speculative run of the class's title page could be made or kept (an assignment journal is open, the run could not be undone, or it was over the read budget): what it prints is read off its code"
4879    );
4880  };
4881  let Some((mut printed, mut letters)) =
4882    sentinel_runs(&all, &title_page_print_tokens, true, false)?
4883  else {
4884    no_run();
4885    return Ok(());
4886  };
4887  // (a run the net's sentinels ended — it printed nothing — is made again for K11's stores alone, as before the net)
4888  if k11 && !net.is_empty() && letters.is_empty() {
4889    net.clear();
4890    all.clone_from(&stores);
4891    off_and_net.clone_from(&off_stores);
4892    let Some(run) = sentinel_runs(&all, &title_page_print_tokens, true, false)? else {
4893      no_run();
4894      return Ok(());
4895    };
4896    (printed, letters) = run;
4897  }
4898  // (the confirming check with the document's own values: one more run when a store found printed has a plain value)
4899  let printed = if printed.split(',').any(|name| {
4900    all
4901      .iter()
4902      .find(|t| t.with_str(|cs| cs == name))
4903      .and_then(parameterless_body)
4904      .as_ref()
4905      .and_then(plain_value_text)
4906      .is_some()
4907  }) {
4908    let reading = crate::latex_constructs::title_page_reading::title_page_reading()?;
4909    match reading.outcome {
4910      crate::latex_constructs::title_page_reading::RunOutcome::Printed => {
4911        confirmed_printed(&all, &printed, &letters, &reading.text)
4912      },
4913      _ => printed,
4914    }
4915  } else {
4916    printed
4917  };
4918  DebugFeature!(
4919    "frontmatter",
4920    "FRONT title-page sentinels: printed [{printed}] of {} candidate stores; the letters run: {letters}",
4921    all.len()
4922  );
4923
4924  // (the deposit's and the replay's runs, over K11's off-table stores and the net's candidates; one that cannot be
4925  // made or kept with the net's is made again without them, and the net decides nothing)
4926  let typeset_runs = |stores: &[Token]| -> Result<Option<(String, String)>> {
4927    let (mut deposit, mut replay) = (String::new(), String::new());
4928    if stores.is_empty() {
4929      return Ok(Some((deposit, replay)));
4930    }
4931    match sentinel_runs(stores, &title_page_deposit_tokens, false, false)? {
4932      Some((list, _)) => deposit = list,
4933      None => return Ok(None),
4934    }
4935    if title_page_replay_tokens(&[]).is_some() {
4936      let replay_tokens =
4937        |defs: &[Token]| title_page_replay_tokens(defs).unwrap_or_else(|| Tokens::new(Vec::new()));
4938      match sentinel_runs(stores, &replay_tokens, false, false)? {
4939        Some((list, _)) => replay = list,
4940        None => return Ok(None),
4941      }
4942    }
4943    Ok(Some((deposit, replay)))
4944  };
4945  let (deposit, replay) = match typeset_runs(&off_and_net)? {
4946    Some(lists) => lists,
4947    None if !net.is_empty() && k11 => {
4948      net.clear();
4949      match typeset_runs(&off_stores)? {
4950        Some(lists) => lists,
4951        None => {
4952          no_run();
4953          return Ok(());
4954        },
4955      }
4956    },
4957    None => {
4958      no_run();
4959      return Ok(());
4960    },
4961  };
4962  // (each list split between K11's stores and the net's)
4963  let of = |list: &str, among: &[Token]| -> String {
4964    list
4965      .split(',')
4966      .filter(|name| among.iter().any(|t| t.with_str(|cs| cs == *name)))
4967      .collect::<Vec<_>>()
4968      .join(",")
4969  };
4970  if k11 {
4971    for (run, list) in [
4972      ("printed", of(&printed, &stores)),
4973      ("deposit", of(&deposit, &off_stores)),
4974      ("replay", of(&replay, &off_stores)),
4975    ] {
4976      assign_value(
4977        &s!("lx_sentinel_{run}"),
4978        Stored::String(pin(list)),
4979        Some(Scope::Global),
4980      );
4981    }
4982    assign_value("lx_sentinel_ran", true, Some(Scope::Global));
4983  }
4984  if !net.is_empty() {
4985    let net_printed = of(&printed, &net);
4986    // (A2: what the refused begin codes of the frontmatter environments print, [`net_begin_code_tokens`] — and the
4987    // title page does not —, handed on only when the document opens the environment after this `\maketitle`
4988    // ([`hand_net_begin_code_stores`]): the class prints it then, with the values it then has; never opened, or opened
4989    // before, nothing printed it there (review rev98c S1))
4990    let mut net_begin = String::new();
4991    if net_begin_code_tokens(&[]).is_some()
4992      && let Some((list, _)) = sentinel_runs(
4993        &net,
4994        &|defs: &[Token]| net_begin_code_tokens(defs).unwrap_or_default(),
4995        false,
4996        true,
4997      )?
4998    {
4999      for name in list.split(',').filter(|n| !n.is_empty()) {
5000        if !net_printed.split(',').any(|n| n == name) {
5001          if !net_begin.is_empty() {
5002            net_begin.push(',');
5003          }
5004          net_begin.push_str(name);
5005        }
5006      }
5007    }
5008    assign_value(
5009      "lx_sentinel_net_begin",
5010      Stored::String(pin(net_begin)),
5011      Some(Scope::Global),
5012    );
5013    for (run, list) in [
5014      ("printed", net_printed),
5015      ("deposit", of(&deposit, &net)),
5016      ("replay", of(&replay, &net)),
5017    ] {
5018      assign_value(
5019        &s!("lx_sentinel_net_{run}"),
5020        Stored::String(pin(list)),
5021        Some(Scope::Global),
5022      );
5023    }
5024    assign_value("lx_net_ran", true, Some(Scope::Global));
5025  }
5026  Ok(())
5027}
5028
5029/// The unclaimed-text net (FIDELITY_CHARTER §4 "No printed frontmatter word is lost"; OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES
5030/// #953): the stores a raw class's title page reads that no owner claims — K11's table and off-table stores are
5031/// claimed ([`frontmatter_owned_stores`]) — and that only its own title-page code prints. Candidates are stores, never a
5032/// run's text, so what a class hard-codes (a letterhead, "Received …", a copyright line) is never one: each control
5033/// sequence the class's title-page code reads (`live_read_order` from the roots, the code that runs) — its refused
5034/// `\maketitle`, its `\@maketitle`, its own `\@author` the kernel's replaced ([`CLASS_AUTHOR_ROOT`], sect05.rs
5035/// `keep_class_author_reader`: copernicus.cls:1329-1352's `\AB@affillist`, 0712.1312, 1401.4706, 1709.00722) and the
5036/// begin codes of its refused frontmatter environments (`\lx@dropped@abstract`: sig-alternate.cls:736-790 prints
5037/// `\the\tntoks`, 1206.6940, cs0405006) — whose value the document made since the raw file loaded
5038/// ([`net_value_changed`]); not the kernel's fields, an internal of ours (`\lx@…`), nor a store holding a claimed one
5039/// (the claimed store's sentinel would not reach the run through it). What the title page prints of them is decided by
5040/// the sentinel runs ([`before_title_page_runs`]), and what is printed and no kept run typeset becomes a note of the
5041/// author block ([`hand_net_stores`]), kept only where it holds words no frontmatter field holds
5042/// (`base_utilities::frontmatter::insert`, `keep_unclaimed_net_notes`).
5043fn net_candidates() -> Vec<Token> {
5044  let mut roots: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
5045  for cs in ["\\lx@dropped@maketitle", "\\@maketitle"] {
5046    let root = T_CS!(cs);
5047    if file_body(&root).is_some() {
5048      roots.push(root);
5049    }
5050  }
5051  if crate::latex_constructs::class_author_reader_body().is_some() {
5052    roots.push(T_CS!(CLASS_AUTHOR_ROOT));
5053  }
5054  roots.extend(net_begin_codes().into_iter().map(|(code, _)| code));
5055  if roots.is_empty() {
5056    return Vec::new();
5057  }
5058  let owned = frontmatter_owned_stores();
5059  let mut out: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
5060  let reads = live_read_order(&roots, &owned, true);
5061  let mut scratch: Option<FxHashSet<SymStr>> = None;
5062  DebugFeature!(
5063    "frontmatter",
5064    "FRONT net roots [{}] read [{}]",
5065    roots
5066      .iter()
5067      .map(ToString::to_string)
5068      .collect::<Vec<_>>()
5069      .join(","),
5070    reads
5071      .iter()
5072      .map(|r| with(*r, ToString::to_string))
5073      .collect::<Vec<_>>()
5074      .join(",")
5075  );
5076  for name in reads {
5077    let cs = T_CS!(&with(name, ToString::to_string));
5078    // (nor the job's name, a primitive's value the engine sets: core_interface.rs `install_jobname`, raa.cls's
5079    // manuscript line, 2110.11117)
5080    if owned.contains(&cs)
5081      || roots.contains(&cs)
5082      || out.contains(&cs)
5083      || cs.with_str(|n| n.starts_with("\\lx@") || n == "\\jobname")
5084    {
5085      continue;
5086    }
5087    if net_value_changed(&cs, &owned)
5088      && net_value(&cs).is_some_and(|value| holds_text(&value))
5089      && scratch
5090        .get_or_insert_with(stores_raw_code_writes)
5091        .contains(&cs.get_cs_name())
5092    {
5093      out.push(cs);
5094    }
5095  }
5096  DebugFeature!(
5097    "frontmatter",
5098    "FRONT net candidates: [{}]",
5099    out
5100      .iter()
5101      .map(ToString::to_string)
5102      .collect::<Vec<_>>()
5103      .join(",")
5104  );
5105  out
5106}
5107
5108/// The root the net's walk ([`live_read_order`]) reads the class's own `\@author` reader under (sect05.rs
5109/// `keep_class_author_reader`), a name with no meaning.
5110const CLASS_AUTHOR_ROOT: &str = "\\lx@class@@author";
5111
5112/// The refused begin codes of the frontmatter environments whose class code the kernel replays (sect05.rs
5113/// `replay_class_begin_code`: `{abstract}`), as a raw file defined them, of no parameters: what the class prints
5114/// when the document opens one — sig-alternate.cls:736-790's `\insert\footins{…\the\tntoks}`, its title note.
5115/// A begin code that collects its body is not run (it reads ahead to its `\end`, which a run of it alone does not
5116/// have: [`collects_its_body`]).
5117fn net_begin_codes() -> Vec<(Token, &'static str)> {
5118  ["abstract"]
5119    .into_iter()
5120    .map(|env| (T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@dropped@{env}")), env))
5121    .filter(|(cs, _)| {
5122      matches!(lookup_meaning(cs), Some(Stored::Expandable(ref d))
5123        if d.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File
5124          && d.get_parameters().is_none_or(|p| p.get_parameters().is_empty())
5125          && matches!(d.get_expansion(), Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(_))))
5126        && !collects_its_body(cs)
5127    })
5128    .collect()
5129}
5130
5131/// Does the code of `cs` reach a macro a raw file defined with a delimited parameter — one that reads the input up to a
5132/// delimiter (smfart.cls:510 `\@getgenabstext#1\end #2`, nrc1.cls:1558 `\NRC@gather@body#1\end`, wds10.sty:539
5133/// `\pickup#1\end#2`), the environment's body up to its `\end`, past a run's own input (2206.03779, 1910.12640)?
5134fn collects_its_body(cs: &Token) -> bool {
5135  live_read_order(&[*cs], &[], false).into_iter().any(|name| {
5136    matches!(lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(&with(name, ToString::to_string))), Some(Stored::Expandable(ref d))
5137      if d.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File
5138        && d.get_parameters().is_some_and(|p| p.get_parameters().iter().any(|q| !undelimited(q))))
5139  })
5140}
5141
5142/// A speculative run of the refused begin codes ([`net_begin_codes`]) after `inject` (the sentinel definitions), with
5143/// notes, labels and index entries as the title page's run has them (`title_page_print_tokens`); `None` when there are
5144/// none. Their `\insert`s reach the run's text as the title page's do. The run's group is not closed here: a begin code
5145/// leaves its own groups open for its end code (frontiersFPHY.cls:520 `\begingroup`), and the run closes what is left
5146/// open (`title_page_probe_run`), as the replay of one does (sect05.rs `replay_dropped_class_code`); it reads from
5147/// a mouth of its own (`title_page_probe_run`, isolated), so a begin code that collects its body up to its `\end` meets the
5148/// end of the run's input, not the document (smfart.cls:506-520; 2206.03779).
5149fn net_begin_code_tokens(inject: &[Token]) -> Option<Tokens> {
5150  let codes = net_begin_codes();
5151  if codes.is_empty() {
5152    return None;
5153  }
5154  let mut run =
5155    mouth::tokenize_internal(r"{\let\thanks\@firstofone\let\label\@gobble\let\index\@gobble")
5156      .unlist();
5157  run.extend_from_slice(inject);
5158  // (each with an empty body and its `\end`, which ends nothing here: a begin code collecting its body up to its
5159  // `\end` finds it — smfart.cls:506-520 `\@getgenabstext#1\end #2`, 2206.03779 — as pdflatex's empty environment
5160  // would give it)
5161  run.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(r"\let\end\@gobble").unlist());
5162  for (code, env) in codes {
5163    // (in the environment, as `\begin` puts the code there: latex.ltx `\begin` `\def\@currenvir{#1}`)
5164    run.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(r"\def\@currenvir").unlist());
5165    run.push(T_BEGIN!());
5166    run.extend(ExplodeText!(env));
5167    run.push(T_END!());
5168    run.push(code);
5169    run.push(T_CS!("\\par"));
5170    run.push(T_CS!("\\end"));
5171    run.push(T_BEGIN!());
5172    run.extend(ExplodeText!(env));
5173    run.push(T_END!());
5174  }
5175  Some(Tokens::new(run))
5176}
5177
5178/// Does a store's value hold text of its own — a character the code that runs typesets ([`executed_code`]: not a
5179/// definition's body nor a running head's argument)? A value that is only code prints no text of its own: oasics.cls:544
5180/// `\titlerunning` stores `{\let\footnote\@gobble\markboth{#1}{#1}}`, a font switch's robust wrapper `\protect\itshape`
5181/// (1709.00722, 2206.03779); a sentinel in its place would read as printed. A non-ASCII active character is a
5182/// character too: the glyph inputenc maps it to (`Москва` under `[T2A]{fontenc}`, each letter active through
5183/// t2aenc.dfu's `\DeclareUnicodeCharacter`; 105a r2), unlike an ASCII one (`~`, a tie).
5184fn holds_text(value: &Tokens) -> bool {
5185  executed_code(value.unlist_ref())
5186    .iter()
5187    .any(prints_a_character)
5188}
5189
5190/// Whether a token prints a character of its own: a letter, an other, or a non-ASCII active character (the glyph
5191/// inputenc maps it to). One test for what the net reads as a store's text ([`holds_text`]) and as the class's own text
5192/// in it ([`net_class_text`]): with the second reading ASCII letters only, a class label set in CJK or Cyrillic
5193/// (`\gdef\@xa{作者单位：#1}` after CJKutf8 or `[T2A]{fontenc}`) was text the store holds and not the class's, and was
5194/// kept as an unclaimed note (review rev105a2 finding 1).
5195fn prints_a_character(t: &Token) -> bool {
5196  match t.get_catcode() {
5197    Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER => true,
5198    Catcode::ACTIVE => t.with_str(|text| !text.is_ascii()),
5199    _ => false,
5200  }
5201}
5202
5203/// The stores a setter of a raw file's or of the document's writes — a definition's or a `\let`'s name, a token
5204/// register it names, in a macro's body (copernicus.cls's `\affil` `\xdef\AB@affillist{…}`, sig-alternate.cls's
5205/// `\titlenote` `\global\tntoks={#1}`) — that no macro of the format's, the pools' or the bindings' writes: theirs are
5206/// scratch stores (latex.ltx's `\@tempa`, `\toks@`: smfart.cls's title page reads `\toks@` through
5207/// `\uppercasenonmath`, 2206.03779), and a macro the document only defines (`\newcommand\SL{\mathrm{SL}}`, which
5208/// 2609.05055's title prints) is a shorthand, no store a setter fills. The net's candidates ([`net_candidates`]).
5209fn stores_raw_code_writes() -> FxHashSet<SymStr> {
5210  let mut raw: FxHashSet<SymStr> = FxHashSet::default();
5211  let mut ours: FxHashSet<SymStr> = FxHashSet::default();
5212  for (_, stored) in current_meanings_where(|stored| matches!(stored, Stored::Expandable(_))) {
5213    let Stored::Expandable(d) = stored else {
5214      continue;
5215    };
5216    let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = d.get_expansion() else {
5217      continue;
5218    };
5219    let written = if matches!(
5220      d.get_origin(),
5221      DefinitionOrigin::File | DefinitionOrigin::Document
5222    ) {
5223      &mut raw
5224    } else {
5225      &mut ours
5226    };
5227    let body = body.unlist_ref();
5228    for (i, t) in body.iter().enumerate() {
5229      if t.get_catcode() != Catcode::CS {
5230        continue;
5231      }
5232      if cs_in(t, definition::expandable::KERNEL_WRITERS_OF_NAMES)
5233        && let Some(name) = body.get(i + 1)
5234        && name.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS
5235      {
5236        written.insert(name.get_cs_name());
5237      } else if is_token_register(t) {
5238        written.insert(t.get_cs_name());
5239      }
5240    }
5241  }
5242  raw.retain(|name| !ours.contains(name));
5243  raw
5244}
5245
5246/// The value the net hands on of the store `cs`: a parameterless macro's body, a token register's contents.
5247fn net_value(cs: &Token) -> Option<Tokens> {
5248  if is_token_register(cs) {
5249    return cs.with_str(|name| match lookup_register_quiet(name) {
5250      Some(RegisterValue::Tokens(value)) => Some(value),
5251      _ => None,
5252    });
5253  }
5254  parameterless_body(cs)
5255}
5256
5257/// Did the document make the value of the store `cs` since the raw file that defined it loaded, and is it not blank?
5258/// A macro's definition is the document's (`DefinitionOrigin::Document`: a class setter it called, in the preamble
5259/// or the body), not one a raw file made (its default; a replayed class setter's store, read as the class's) nor one
5260/// the begin-document hooks made (`note_document_hooks`: a class's `\AtBeginDocument{\gdef\@journal{…}}`); a token
5261/// register's contents differ from those it had when the last raw file before it loaded (`note_raw_register_defaults`;
5262/// empty for one the document allocated). A store whose value holds a store a frontmatter owner claims (`owned`) is not
5263/// the net's.
5264fn net_value_changed(cs: &Token, owned: &[Token]) -> bool {
5265  match lookup_meaning(cs) {
5266    Some(Stored::Expandable(d)) => {
5267      if d.get_origin() != DefinitionOrigin::Document
5268        || d
5269          .get_parameters()
5270          .is_some_and(|p| !p.get_parameters().is_empty())
5271      {
5272        return false;
5273      }
5274      let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = d.get_expansion() else {
5275        return false;
5276      };
5277      if is_blank(body) || body.unlist_ref().iter().any(|t| owned.contains(t)) {
5278        return false;
5279      }
5280      let hook_made = cs.with_str(|name| match lookup_value(&s!("lx_net_hook_{name}")) {
5281        Some(Stored::Expandable(made)) => Rc::ptr_eq(&made, &d),
5282        _ => false,
5283      });
5284      !hook_made
5285    },
5286    Some(Stored::Register(r)) if matches!(r.register_type, RegisterType::Tokens) => {
5287      let Some(value) = net_value(cs) else {
5288        return false;
5289      };
5290      if is_blank(&value) || value.unlist_ref().iter().any(|t| owned.contains(t)) {
5291        return false;
5292      }
5293      let default = cs.with_str(
5294        |name| match lookup_value(&s!("lx_net_toks_default_{name}")) {
5295          Some(Stored::Tokens(default)) => default,
5296          _ => Tokens::default(),
5297        },
5298      );
5299      default.unlist_ref() != value.unlist_ref()
5300    },
5301    _ => false,
5302  }
5303}
5304
5305/// What [`note_document_hooks`] reads before the begin-document hooks: the Document-origin definitions, by address,
5306/// each kept alive with them so no address is reused by a definition made since (review rev98c N1); and the token
5307/// registers' contents.
5308type HooksBefore = (FxHashSet<usize>, Vec<Stored>, HashMap<SymStr, Tokens>);
5309
5310thread_local! {
5311  /// [`HooksBefore`], from the snapshot before the hooks ran.
5312  static NET_HOOKS_BEFORE: std::cell::RefCell<HooksBefore> =
5313    std::cell::RefCell::new((FxHashSet::default(), Vec::new(), HashMap::default()));
5314}
5315
5316/// Around the begin-document hooks (sect02.rs `\begin{document}`): before them (`before`), the Document-origin
5317/// definitions that exist and the token registers' contents; after them — `\AtBeginDocument`'s, and the
5318/// `begindocument/end` hook's, which `\begin{document}` reads after itself (`\lx@net@document@hooks@done`) —, each
5319/// definition they made is recorded as the hooks' (`lx_net_hook_<name>`), and each token register they changed has the
5320/// contents they left as its raw default (`lx_net_toks_default_<name>`): a class default for the net
5321/// (`net_value_changed`), as a class's `\AtBeginDocument{\setjournal{…}}` filling `\jtoks` is (review rev98c S2).
5322pub fn note_document_hooks(before: bool) {
5323  if !lookup_bool("lx_net_raw_loaded") {
5324    return;
5325  }
5326  let address = |stored: &Stored| match stored {
5327    Stored::Expandable(d) => Some(Rc::as_ptr(d).cast::<()>() as usize),
5328    _ => None,
5329  };
5330  let made = current_meanings_where(
5331    |stored| matches!(stored, Stored::Expandable(d) if d.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::Document),
5332  );
5333  let registers = current_meanings_where(
5334    |stored| matches!(stored, Stored::Register(r) if matches!(r.register_type, RegisterType::Tokens)),
5335  );
5336  let contents = |key: &SymStr| -> Tokens {
5337    with(*key, |name| match lookup_register_quiet(name) {
5338      Some(RegisterValue::Tokens(value)) => value,
5339      _ => Tokens::default(),
5340    })
5341  };
5342  if before {
5343    let set: FxHashSet<usize> = made.iter().filter_map(|(_, m)| address(m)).collect();
5344    let kept: Vec<Stored> = made.into_iter().map(|(_, m)| m).collect();
5345    let toks: HashMap<SymStr, Tokens> = registers
5346      .iter()
5347      .map(|(key, _)| (*key, contents(key)))
5348      .collect();
5349    NET_HOOKS_BEFORE.with(|c| *c.borrow_mut() = (set, kept, toks));
5350    return;
5351  }
5352  let (set, _kept, toks) = NET_HOOKS_BEFORE.with(|c| std::mem::take(&mut *c.borrow_mut()));
5353  for (key, meaning) in made {
5354    if address(&meaning).is_some_and(|a| !set.contains(&a)) {
5355      let name = with(key, ToString::to_string);
5356      assign_value(&s!("lx_net_hook_{name}"), meaning, Some(Scope::Global));
5357    }
5358  }
5359  for (key, _) in registers {
5360    let now = contents(&key);
5361    if toks
5362      .get(&key)
5363      .is_none_or(|was| was.unlist_ref() != now.unlist_ref())
5364    {
5365      let name = with(key, ToString::to_string);
5366      assign_value(
5367        &s!("lx_net_toks_default_{name}"),
5368        Stored::Tokens(now),
5369        Some(Scope::Global),
5370      );
5371    }
5372  }
5373}
5374
5375/// After a raw class or package loaded: the contents of each token register not yet recorded, its raw default for the
5376/// net ([`net_value_changed`]).
5377fn note_raw_register_defaults() {
5378  assign_value("lx_net_raw_loaded", true, Some(Scope::Global));
5379  let registers = current_meanings_where(
5380    |stored| matches!(stored, Stored::Register(r) if matches!(r.register_type, RegisterType::Tokens)),
5381  );
5382  for (key, _) in registers {
5383    let name = with(key, ToString::to_string);
5384    let record = s!("lx_net_toks_default_{name}");
5385    if lookup_value(&record).is_some() {
5386      continue;
5387    }
5388    let value = match lookup_register_quiet(&name) {
5389      Some(RegisterValue::Tokens(value)) => value,
5390      _ => Tokens::default(),
5391    };
5392    assign_value(&record, Stored::Tokens(value), Some(Scope::Global));
5393  }
5394}
5395
5396/// The text of the class's own code in each of the net's stores `stores` (for `keep_unclaimed_net_notes`, insert.rs:
5397/// words the document did not give are not its unclaimed text): the characters of the raw files' macros that name the
5398/// store — its setter's own text around the argument, e-jc.sty:100-101 `\def\the@dateline{Submitted:
5399/// \ignorespaces#1; Accepted: …\copy@right}` (2410.15356), a language's captions that set it, smfart.cls's
5400/// `\def\keywordsname{Key words and phrases}` run by babel's `\selectlanguage` in the document (2206.11835, 2609.17377)
5401/// — and of the raw macros the store's value reads, two deep (`\copy@right`'s class default).
5402fn net_class_text(stores: &[SymStr]) -> Vec<String> {
5403  let letters = |body: &[Token], out: &mut String| {
5404    let mut param = false;
5405    // (a run of active bytes, one token per byte of a UTF-8 character as CJKutf8 or inputenc reads the class file —
5406    // `作` is the three active tokens `ä½\u{9c}` — is read as the character its macros print, so it matches the text the
5407    // store prints: review rev105a2 finding 1)
5408    let mut bytes: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
5409    // (each valid UTF-8 sequence its character; a byte outside one its own Latin-1 character, as `[latin1]{inputenc}`
5410    // reads `é` as one active byte: review rev105a3 finding 2)
5411    let flush = |bytes: &mut Vec<u8>, out: &mut String| {
5412      let mut rest: &[u8] = bytes;
5413      while !rest.is_empty() {
5414        match std::str::from_utf8(rest) {
5415          Ok(text) => {
5416            out.push_str(text);
5417            break;
5418          },
5419          Err(error) => {
5420            let (valid, invalid) = rest.split_at(error.valid_up_to());
5421            out.push_str(std::str::from_utf8(valid).unwrap_or_default());
5422            out.push(char::from(invalid[0]));
5423            rest = &invalid[1..];
5424          },
5425        }
5426      }
5427      bytes.clear();
5428    };
5429    for t in body {
5430      let byte = (t.get_catcode() == Catcode::ACTIVE)
5431        .then(|| {
5432          t.with_str(|c| {
5433            let mut chars = c.chars();
5434            match (chars.next(), chars.next()) {
5435              (Some(ch), None) if ('\u{80}'..='\u{ff}').contains(&ch) => Some(ch as u8),
5436              _ => None,
5437            }
5438          })
5439        })
5440        .flatten();
5441      if let Some(byte) = byte
5442        && !param
5443      {
5444        bytes.push(byte);
5445        continue;
5446      }
5447      flush(&mut bytes, out);
5448      match t.get_catcode() {
5449        Catcode::PARAM => param = true,
5450        _ if prints_a_character(t) && !std::mem::take(&mut param) => {
5451          t.with_str(|c| out.push_str(c));
5452        },
5453        _ => {
5454          param = false;
5455          out.push(' ');
5456        },
5457      }
5458    }
5459    flush(&mut bytes, out);
5460    out.push(' ');
5461  };
5462  let setters = current_meanings_where(
5463    |stored| matches!(stored, Stored::Expandable(d) if d.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File),
5464  );
5465  stores
5466    .iter()
5467    .map(|key| {
5468      let store = T_CS!(&with(*key, ToString::to_string));
5469      let mut out = String::new();
