5470      // (each body once: a `\let` or `\futurelet` copy of a setter is the same code — `\@ifnextchar`'s `\@let@token`
5471      // holds `\xa` after peeking at it — and its words are the class's text once, not twice: review rev105a3 finding 1)
5472      let mut read: Vec<&Vec<Token>> = Vec::new();
5473      for (_, setter) in &setters {
5474        if let Stored::Expandable(d) = setter
5475          && let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = d.get_expansion()
5476          && body.unlist_ref().contains(&store)
5477          && !read.contains(&body.unlist_ref())
5478        {
5479          read.push(body.unlist_ref());
5480          letters(body.unlist_ref(), &mut out);
5481        }
5482      }
5483      let mut seen: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
5484      let mut reads: Vec<(Token, usize)> = net_value(&store)
5485        .map(|v| v.unlist_ref().iter().map(|t| (*t, 0)).collect())
5486        .unwrap_or_default();
5487      while let Some((t, depth)) = reads.pop() {
5488        if t.get_catcode() != Catcode::CS || seen.contains(&t) || depth >= 2 {
5489          continue;
5490        }
5491        seen.push(t);
5492        if let Some(body) = file_body(&t) {
5493          letters(body.unlist_ref(), &mut out);
5494          reads.extend(body.unlist_ref().iter().map(|b| (*b, depth + 1)));
5495        }
5496      }
5497      out
5498    })
5499    .collect()
5500}
5501
5502/// The net's hand-on at `\maketitle` (`\lx@store@untypeset`, after the title-page deposit and replay): each candidate
5503/// ([`net_candidates`]) the title page prints (`lx_sentinel_net_printed`, [`before_title_page_runs`]) that no kept title-page
5504/// run typeset (`lx_sentinel_net_deposit`, `lx_sentinel_net_replay`), in the order the title page prints them, as a note of the author
5505/// block — `\lx@annotate@frontmatter{ltx:creator}{ltx:note}[role=note,label=addresses:block,_net=<name>]{<value>}`, the
5506/// shared nameless creator below the authors (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #159) — its value read as the class's code
5507/// reads it. Its lines that only repeat what the frontmatter holds are taken out when it is placed
5508/// (`keep_unclaimed_net_notes`). Once per document; without a run, nothing (the net decides by TeX's output only).
5509fn hand_net_stores() -> Result<Vec<Digested>> {
5510  if lookup_bool("lx_net_handed") || !lookup_bool("lx_net_ran") {
5511    return Ok(Vec::new());
5512  }
5513  assign_value("lx_net_handed", true, Some(Scope::Global));
5514  let mut typeset: Vec<SymStr> = Vec::new();
5515  if lookup_bool("lx_title_page_deposit_kept") {
5516    typeset.extend(sentinel_list("net_deposit"));
5517  }
5518  if lookup_bool("lx_title_page_replay_kept") {
5519    typeset.extend(sentinel_list("net_replay"));
5520  }
5521  let handed: Vec<SymStr> = sentinel_list("net_printed")
5522    .into_iter()
5523    .filter(|key| !typeset.contains(key))
5524    .collect();
5525  net_note_calls(handed)
5526}
5527
5528/// When the document opens a frontmatter environment after the `\maketitle` that ran the net (`\lx@abstract@class@setters`,
5529/// right after the class's refused begin code is replayed there): the net's candidates only that begin code prints
5530/// (`lx_sentinel_net_begin`, [`before_title_page_runs`]), as notes of the author block, their values as they are now
5531/// (sig-alternate.cls:736-790's `\the\tntoks` under `{abstract}`: 1206.6940, cs0405006). Once; not after the authors
5532/// were sealed. A document that never opens the environment, or opens it before `\maketitle`, prints none of them
5533/// (review rev98c S1: noabs, absfirst).
5534pub fn hand_net_begin_code_stores() -> Result<()> {
5535  if !lookup_bool("lx_net_ran")
5536    || lookup_bool("lx_depositing_frontmatter_fields")
5537    || lookup_bool("lx_replaying_class_setter")
5538    || frontmatter_entry_sealed("ltx:creator", "author")
5539  {
5540    return Ok(());
5541  }
5542  let pending = sentinel_list("net_begin");
5543  if pending.is_empty() {
5544    return Ok(());
5545  }
5546  assign_value(
5547    "lx_sentinel_net_begin",
5548    Stored::String(pin("")),
5549    Some(Scope::Global),
5550  );
5551  // (frontmatter entries queued, no box of their own)
5552  let _ = net_note_calls(pending)?;
5553  Ok(())
5554}
5555
5556/// The net's notes ([`hand_net_stores`]) of the stores `handed`, digested.
5557fn net_note_calls(handed: Vec<SymStr>) -> Result<Vec<Digested>> {
5558  let mut calls: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
5559  let class_text = net_class_text(&handed);
5560  for (key, class_text) in handed.into_iter().zip(class_text) {
5561    let name = with(key, ToString::to_string);
5562    let cs = T_CS!(&name);
5563    let Some(value) = net_value(&cs) else {
5564      continue;
5565    };
5566    let bare = name.trim_start_matches('\\');
5567    assign_value(
5568      &s!("lx_net_class_text_{bare}"),
5569      Stored::String(pin(class_text)),
5570      Some(Scope::Global),
5571    );
5572    calls.extend(
5573      mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!(
5574        "\\lx@annotate@frontmatter{{ltx:creator}}{{ltx:note}}[role=note,label=addresses:block,_net={bare}]"
5575      )))
5576      .unlist(),
5577    );
5578    calls.push(T_BEGIN!());
5579    calls.extend(without_labels(&value).unlist());
5580    calls.push(T_END!());
5581    Info!(
5582      "frontmatter",
5583      s!("{name}"),
5584      s!(
5585        "The class's title page prints {name}, which no frontmatter field holds and no title-page run here typeset: it is handed on as a note of the author block"
5586      )
5587    );
5588  }
5589  if calls.is_empty() {
5590    return Ok(Vec::new());
5591  }
5592  Ok(vec![digest_store_calls(calls)?])
5593}
5594
5595/// Silent-loss 77h/23b: the value a rerouted store the document never set, still undefined, takes in the title-page
5596/// deposit and replay (`\lx@captured@stores`): the one under which the title page prints fewer characters — a rule over
5597/// the text TeX typesets for each candidate in speculative runs of the deposit and replay as they will run
5598/// (`title_page_probe_run`). Undefined, it
5599/// raised an error that dropped the whole deposit — an.cls:532 `\ch@ckobl{institute}{…}`, which runs in the class's own
5600/// `\maketitle` the kernel's replaces, took the paper's "Key words" line with it (row 23b). `\relax` keeps a class's
5601/// "is it undefined?" test true: ptptex.cls:88-104 prints its journal line and "(Received …)" unless
5602/// `\@ifundefined{@pubinfo}`/`{@recdate}` (hep-th9803240, 1309.6905, 0911.1235, 0708.3877, 0801.2466, 1009.4028,
5603/// 1007.1507, cond-mat0411234; jpsj: 0903.1491, 0912.1097, 1101.2977, 1203.5392); `\@empty` keeps an emptiness test
5604/// true: `\ifx\@institute\@empty\else Institute: \@institute\fi` after the class's own `\maketitle` defaulted it to
5605/// `\@empty`. All `\relax` first, then all `\@empty`: when the title page prints as much text, all stay `\relax`; else
5606/// each store alone `\@empty`, kept so when the title page then prints less (a tie keeps `\relax`). Recorded as
5607/// `lx_store_unset_empty_<name>`.
5608fn decide_unset_nulls(rerouted: &[String]) -> Result<()> {
5609  use crate::latex_constructs::{
5610    SpeculativeRun, title_page_deposit_tokens, title_page_probe_run, title_page_replay_tokens,
5611  };
5612  let unset: Vec<&String> = rerouted
5613    .iter()
5614    .filter(|name| {
5615      let key = s!("lx_store_unset_empty_{name}");
5616      assign_value(&key, false, Some(Scope::Global));
5617      !lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_handed_{name}"))
5618        && lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(&s!("\\{}", store_of(name)))).is_none()
5619    })
5620    .collect();
5621  // (a deposit runs unless `\@maketitle` is the kernel's, or empty)
5622  let deposits = !crate::latex_constructs::maketitle_is_kernels()
5623    && match lookup_meaning(&T_CS!("\\@maketitle")) {
5624      Some(Stored::Expandable(d)) => {
5625        matches!(d.get_expansion(), Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(t)) if !t.is_empty())
5626      },
5627      _ => true,
5628    };
5629  let replay = title_page_replay_tokens(&[]);
5630  if unset.is_empty() || (!deposits && replay.is_none()) {
5631    return Ok(());
5632  }
5633  // (`None`: no run, or one that ended in a Fatal of its own — which is no "fewer characters": the value that killed
5634  // its run is not chosen, `\relax` stays)
5635  let ink = || -> Result<Option<usize>> {
5636    let mut tokens = title_page_deposit_tokens(&[]).unlist();
5637    if let Some(replay) = title_page_replay_tokens(&[]) {
5638      tokens.extend(replay.unlist());
5639    }
5640    Ok(match title_page_probe_run(Tokens::new(tokens), false)? {
5641      SpeculativeRun::Printed(text) => Some(text.chars().filter(|c| !c.is_whitespace()).count()),
5642      SpeculativeRun::Aborted | SpeculativeRun::Unavailable | SpeculativeRun::OverBudget(_) => None,
5643    })
5644  };
5645  let Some(base) = ink()? else {
5646    return Ok(());
5647  };
5648  // (all `\@empty` first: when the title page prints as much, no store's value matters)
5649  let keys: Vec<String> = unset
5650    .iter()
5651    .map(|name| s!("lx_store_unset_empty_{name}"))
5652    .collect();
5653  for key in &keys {
5654    assign_value(key, true, Some(Scope::Global));
5655  }
5656  let all_empty = ink()?;
5657  for key in &keys {
5658    assign_value(key, false, Some(Scope::Global));
5659  }
5660  if all_empty.is_none_or(|n| n == base) {
5661    return Ok(());
5662  }
5663  for name in &unset {
5664    let key = s!("lx_store_unset_empty_{name}");
5665    assign_value(&key, true, Some(Scope::Global));
5666    let emptier = ink()?.is_some_and(|n| n < base);
5667    assign_value(&key, emptier, Some(Scope::Global));
5668    if emptier {
5669      Info!(
5670        "frontmatter",
5671        s!("\\{name}"),
5672        s!("The unset store of \\{name} is empty in the title page: undefined it would print more")
5673      );
5674    }
5675  }
5676  // (each decided alone: chosen together they are what each was chosen against, unless they interact)
5677  Ok(())
5678}
5679
5680/// The stores whose values the frontmatter owns: the kernel's title fields (and their short forms) and the raw class's
5681/// stores K11 hands to the frontmatter — the rerouted table setters' (`reroute_raw_class_stores`) and the off-table
5682/// ones (`survey_off_table_stores`). A class command run in place after the document body began puts them back as
5683/// they were (sect05.rs `sealed_storing_run`, silent-loss #8).
5684pub fn frontmatter_owned_stores() -> Vec<Token> {
5685  let mut stores: Vec<Token> = KERNEL_FIELDS
5686    .iter()
5687    .chain(&["\\@shorttitle", "\\@shortauthor"])
5688    .map(|cs| T_CS!(*cs))
5689    .collect();
5690  for name in rerouted_names() {
5691    stores.push(T_CS!(&s!("\\{}", store_of(&name))));
5692  }
5693  for name in lookup_string("lx_offtable_stores")
5694    .split(',')
5695    .filter(|n| !n.is_empty())
5696  {
5697    if let Some(Stored::Token(store)) = lookup_value(&s!("lx_offtable_store_{name}")) {
5698      stores.push(store);
5699    }
5700  }
5701  stores.dedup();
5702  stores
5703}
5704
5705/// Silent-loss #6b: the rerouted stores this `\maketitle` placed — handed to the frontmatter and printed by the class's
5706/// title page (`lx_sentinel_printed`, [`before_title_page_runs`]; without a speculative run, read by it:
5707/// `lx_store_title_page_reads_<name>`) — whose setter, called again, only re-sets the store (`\lx@store@hand`). A store
5708/// the title page does not print stays the later call's: a class abstract's end code `Keywords: \@keywords` prints the
5709/// value set after `\maketitle` (review probe `late`).
5710fn settle_placed_stores() {
5711  for name in rerouted_names() {
5712    let placed = title_page_prints_store(&name) && lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_handed_{name}"));
5713    assign_value(&s!("lx_store_placed_{name}"), placed, Some(Scope::Global));
5714  }
5715}
5716
5717/// The state naming the store whose frontmatter entries are being queued (`\lx@store@from`): their title-page
5718/// provenance (`frontmatter_push`, base_utilities `TITLE_PAGE_ENTRY`: a late abstract's run holding one the title page
5719/// may print, [`title_page_may_print`], is not moved, OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #903).
5720pub const STORE_ENTRY_FROM: &str = "lx_store_entry_from";
5721
5722/// `\lx@store@from{<name>}`: the entries queued next are handed on from the store `\<name>`; empty, from none.
5723fn store_from_tokens(name: &str) -> Vec<Token> {
5724  mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!("\\lx@store@from{{{name}}}"))).unlist()
5725}
5726
5727/// Digest the frontmatter calls of handed-on stores, `\lx@store@from`-bracketed, and clear the provenance after them
5728/// however the digestion ended: a call that stopped before its closing `\lx@store@from{}` stamps no later entry.
5729fn digest_store_calls(calls: Vec<Token>) -> Result<Digested> {
5730  let digested = digest(Tokens::new(calls));
5731  assign_value(
5732    STORE_ENTRY_FROM,
5733    Stored::String(pin("")),
5734    Some(Scope::Global),
5735  );
5736  digested
5737}
5738
5739/// May the class's title page print the store of `\<name>`, whose entry carries its title-page provenance
5740/// (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #903)? Unless the speculative run of the whole title page was made and its sentinel did
5741/// not reach it ([`before_title_page_runs`], `lx_sentinel_printed`): a store the class prints elsewhere — dmtcs's
5742/// `\keywords` after its abstract, 0905.1102 — is not the title page's; with no run made, it may be.
5743pub fn title_page_may_print(name: &str) -> bool {
5744  !lookup_bool("lx_sentinel_ran") || {
5745    let store = T_CS!(&s!("\\{}", store_of(name))).get_cs_name();
5746    sentinel_list("printed").contains(&store)
5747  }
5748}
5749
5750/// Does the class's title page print the store of `\<name>`: its sentinel reached the speculative run of the whole title
5751/// page (`lx_sentinel_printed`, [`before_title_page_runs`]) — without a run, its code reads it
5752/// (`lx_store_title_page_reads_<name>`)?
5753fn title_page_prints_store(name: &str) -> bool {
5754  if lookup_bool("lx_sentinel_ran") {
5755    let store = T_CS!(&s!("\\{}", store_of(name))).get_cs_name();
5756    sentinel_list("printed").contains(&store)
5757  } else {
5758    lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_title_page_reads_{name}"))
5759  }
5760}
5761
5762/// Silent-loss #6a: an abstract or keywords store `\maketitle` handed on is the class's own entry (`_store`,
5763/// [`labelled_api`]), which the document's own abstract does not replace, only when the title page prints it
5764/// ([`title_page_prints_store`]) — not when its code only reads it: `\hypersetup{pdfsubject={\@abst}}` in `\@maketitle`
5765/// beside the document's `{abstract}`, which pdflatex prints once (review probe `md`). Such an entry is an ordinary one
5766/// (base_utilities.rs `frontmatter_release_store`), as before the class's title page was consulted.
5767fn release_unprinted_store_entries() {
5768  for name in rerouted_names() {
5769    if lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_deferred_{name}"))
5770      && lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_handed_{name}"))
5771      && !title_page_prints_store(&name)
5772    {
5773      frontmatter_release_store(&store_of(&name));
5774    }
5775  }
5776}
5777
5778/// The frontmatter call `api` of the store `\<name>` as an ordinary entry's, without the class's store's tag
5779/// ([`labelled_api`]): a value no title page printed — set where no `\maketitle` follows, or after the one that read the
5780/// store.
5781fn untagged_api(api: &str, name: &str) -> String {
5782  let tag = s!("_store={}", store_of(name));
5783  api
5784    .replacen(&s!("[{tag}]"), "", 1)
5785    .replacen(&s!("{tag},"), "", 1)
5786}
5787
5788/// Does the class's own refused `\<field>` (`\lx@dropped@<field>`, state.rs) hand its argument to the setter `\<name>`
5789/// (jfpc.cls:122 `\renewcommand{\title}[1]{\titre{#1}}`, :126 `\author` → `\auteurs`)? Then that setter's store is the
5790/// class's record of the field.
5791fn class_field_setter(field: &str, name: &str) -> bool {
5792  let Some(body) = parameterless_or_any_body(&T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@dropped@{field}"))) else {
5793    return false;
5794  };
5795  let body = body.unlist_ref();
5796  let setter = T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"));
5797  body.iter().enumerate().any(|(i, t)| {
5798    *t == setter && {
5799      let k = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
5800      body
5801        .get(k)
5802        .is_some_and(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN)
5803        && body
5804          .get(k + 1)
5805          .is_some_and(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::ARG)
5806        && body
5807          .get(k + 2)
5808          .is_some_and(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::END)
5809    }
5810  })
5811}
5812
5813/// The token body of `cs`, whatever its parameters.
5814fn parameterless_or_any_body(cs: &Token) -> Option<Tokens> {
5815  match lookup_meaning(cs) {
5816    Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) => match defn.get_expansion() {
5817      Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) => Some(body.clone()),
5818      _ => None,
5819    },
5820    _ => None,
5821  }
5822}
5823
5824/// [`hand_untypeset_stores`] without a speculative run ([`before_title_page_runs`]): what the class's title page reads,
5825/// walked through its live code (the switches as they are, `evaluated_switch`), in reading order — the code of its
5826/// refused `\maketitle`, else of LaTeX's, which runs `\@maketitle` and, when the class has none, prints the kernel
5827/// fields — and what a kept deposit or replay may have typeset (any code it reaches, a stored definition's too).
5828fn walked_title_page_reads() -> (Vec<SymStr>, FxHashSet<SymStr>) {
5829  let fields: Vec<Token> = KERNEL_FIELDS.iter().map(|cs| T_CS!(*cs)).collect();
5830  let dropped = T_CS!("\\lx@dropped@maketitle");
5831  let maketitle = T_CS!("\\@maketitle");
5832  let printed_roots: Vec<Token> = if file_body(&dropped).is_some() {
5833    vec![dropped]
5834  } else if file_body(&maketitle).is_some() {
5835    vec![maketitle]
5836  } else {
5837    fields.clone()
5838  };
5839  let mut blocked = fields.clone();
5840  blocked.extend(["\\thetitle", "\\theauthor", "\\thedate"].map(|cs| T_CS!(cs)));
5841  for name in rerouted_names() {
5842    if lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_handed_{name}")) {
5843      blocked.push(T_CS!(&s!("\\{}", store_of(&name))));
5844    }
5845  }
5846  let printed = live_read_order(&printed_roots, &[], true);
5847  let mut typeset: FxHashSet<SymStr> = FxHashSet::default();
5848  if lookup_bool("lx_title_page_deposit_kept") {
5849    typeset.extend(live_read_order(&[maketitle], &blocked, false));
5850  }
5851  if lookup_bool("lx_title_page_replay_kept") {
5852    let mut replay_blocked = blocked.clone();
5853    replay_blocked.push(maketitle);
5854    typeset.extend(live_read_order(&[dropped], &replay_blocked, false));
5855  }
5856  (printed, typeset)
5857}
5858
5859/// Silent-loss #5 (a). At `\maketitle`, after the title-page deposit and replay (`\lx@store@untypeset`): each off-table
5860/// store the document set ([`offtable_candidates`]) that the class's title page prints — its sentinel reached the
5861/// speculative run of the whole title page ([`before_title_page_runs`]; without one, [`walked_title_page_reads`]) — and
5862/// that no title-page run kept here typeset (its sentinel did not reach the run of the deposit or replay that was kept)
5863/// is handed to the frontmatter, in the order the title page prints them, with no visible label of ours (the
5864/// meta XSLT shows a note's `@role` as its label, LaTeXML-meta-xhtml.xsl:79-85): a store the class's own refused
5865/// `\title` hands its argument to ([`class_field_setter`]) is the document's `ltx:title` when it has none; the store of
5866/// the names the class's own `\@author` prints — the one its refused `\author` hands on, else the first store it reads
5867/// (`lx_offtable_author_reads`) — its authors when it has none (jfpc.cls:122-126, 176-191: 1503.05530), and a store its
5868/// `\@author` prints beside the names rows of their author block (`\lx@add@store@authorblock`), attached to the creators
5869/// as any affiliation is, not a note on the document (both are hover popups under the default `ltx_authors_1line`
5870/// layout, LaTeXML.css:74-88 and :107-120: the gain is what the rows are, not where they show); a store that only repeats the creators' names is not handed on ([`repeats_creator_names`]:
5871/// nowfnt's names-only cover list, 2308.02698); any other an `ltx:pubnote` (Base_Utility.pool.ltxml:634, a note about
5872/// the document), its `\and` between authors' blocks a line break (2108.12618), named by the label the title page prints
5873/// before it (`lx_offtable_label_<name>`) when it prints one. A run that typeset it keeps it, so nothing is shown
5874/// twice; a store the title page prints only under a switch that is off — a `\newif` (aiaa-tc.cls:199-200
5875/// `\aiaa@conference` under `[handcarry]`), a class's own boolean (sigplanconf.cls:1118, 1603.03011's "nnnnnnn.nnnnnnn"
5876/// placeholder DOI) — or only in a box or a running head is not printed and not handed on. Perl drops them all (no
5877/// binding, its `\@maketitle` relaxed, latex_constructs.pool.ltxml:1105). Then the unclaimed-text net's stores, the
5878/// ones no owner claims ([`hand_net_stores`]).
5879fn hand_untypeset_stores() -> Result<Vec<Digested>> {
5880  let mut out = hand_offtable_stores()?;
5881  out.extend(hand_net_stores()?);
5882  Ok(out)
5883}
5884
5885/// K11's part of [`hand_untypeset_stores`]: its off-table stores.
5886fn hand_offtable_stores() -> Result<Vec<Digested>> {
5887  if lookup_string("lx_offtable_stores").is_empty() || lookup_bool("lx_offtable_handed") {
5888    return Ok(Vec::new());
5889  }
5890  assign_value("lx_offtable_handed", true, Some(Scope::Global));
5891  let candidates = offtable_candidates();
5892  if candidates.is_empty() {
5893    return Ok(Vec::new());
5894  }
5895  let (printed, typeset) = if lookup_bool("lx_sentinel_ran") {
5896    let mut typeset: FxHashSet<SymStr> = FxHashSet::default();
5897    if lookup_bool("lx_title_page_deposit_kept") {
5898      typeset.extend(sentinel_list("deposit"));
5899    }
5900    if lookup_bool("lx_title_page_replay_kept") {
5901      typeset.extend(sentinel_list("replay"));
5902    }
5903    (sentinel_list("printed"), typeset)
5904  } else {
5905    walked_title_page_reads()
5906  };
5907  // in the order the title page prints them
5908  let mut handed: Vec<(usize, &(String, Token, Tokens))> = candidates
5909    .iter()
5910    .filter_map(|candidate| {
5911      let key = candidate.1.get_cs_name();
5912      printed
5913        .iter()
5914        .position(|cs| *cs == key)
5915        .filter(|_| !typeset.contains(&key))
5916        .map(|at| (at, candidate))
5917    })
5918    .collect();
5919  handed.sort_by_key(|(at, _)| *at);
5920  // The stores the class's own `\@author` reads (`lx_offtable_author_reads`, jfpc.cls:188-191): the one its refused
5921  // `\author` hands its argument to ([`class_field_setter`]), else the first, holds the names; the others beside it are
5922  // the author block's rows (`\lx@add@store@authorblock`).
5923  let author_reads = lookup_string("lx_offtable_author_reads");
5924  let author_reads: Vec<&str> = author_reads.split(',').filter(|n| !n.is_empty()).collect();
5925  let names_store = candidates
5926    .iter()
5927    .find(|(name, ..)| class_field_setter("author", name))
5928    .map(|(name, ..)| name.as_str())
5929    .or_else(|| {
5930      author_reads.first().and_then(|store| {
5931        candidates
5932          .iter()
5933          .find(|(_, cs, _)| cs.with_str(|n| n == *store))
5934          .map(|(name, ..)| name.as_str())
5935      })
5936    });
5937  let mut has_creator = has_front_matter("ltx:creator");
5938  let creator_words = creator_name_words();
5939  let mut calls: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
5940  let mut rows: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
5941  for (_, (name, store, value)) in handed {
5942    let in_author = store.with_str(|cs| author_reads.contains(&cs));
5943    let (head, what) = if class_field_setter("title", name) && !has_front_matter("ltx:title") {
5944      (
5945        mouth::tokenize_internal(r"\lx@add@title").unlist(),
5946        "the document's title",
5947      )
5948    } else if names_store == Some(name.as_str()) && !has_creator {
5949      has_creator = true;
5950      (
5951        mouth::tokenize_internal(r"\lx@add@authors@adaptive").unlist(),
5952        "the document's authors",
5953      )
5954    } else if in_author
5955      && names_store.is_some()
5956      && has_creator
5957      && !(!matches!(
5958        lookup_value(&s!("lx_offtable_label_{name}")),
5959        Some(Stored::Tokens(_))
5960      ) && repeats_creator_names(value, &creator_words))
5961    {
5962      // (after the names, which make the creators these rows annotate)
5963      rows.extend(
5964        mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!(
5965          "\\lx@add@store@authorblock{{{name}}}"
5966        )))
5967        .unlist(),
5968      );
5969      rows.push(T_BEGIN!());
5970      if let Some(Stored::Tokens(label)) = lookup_value(&s!("lx_offtable_label_{name}")) {
5971        rows.extend(label.unlist());
5972      }
5973      rows.extend(value.unlist_ref().iter().copied());
5974      rows.push(T_END!());
5975      Info!(
5976        "frontmatter",
5977        s!("\\{name}"),
5978        s!(
5979          "The class's title page prints the store of \\{name} in its author block, which no title-page run here typeset: it is handed on as rows of the authors"
5980        )
5981      );
5982      continue;
5983    } else if !matches!(
5984      lookup_value(&s!("lx_offtable_label_{name}")),
5985      Some(Stored::Tokens(_))
5986    ) && repeats_creator_names(value, &creator_words)
5987    {
5988      Info!(
5989        "frontmatter",
5990        s!("\\{name}"),
5991        s!(
5992          "The class's title page prints the store of \\{name}, which only repeats the authors' names: not handed on"
5993        )
5994      );
5995      continue;
5996    } else {
5997      let mut head = mouth::tokenize_internal(r"\lx@add@frontmatter{ltx:pubnote}").unlist();
5998      if let Some(Stored::Tokens(label)) = lookup_value(&s!("lx_offtable_label_{name}")) {
5999        head.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal("[name={").unlist());
6000        head.extend(label.unlist());
6001        head.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal("}]").unlist());
6002      }
6003      (head, "a note on the document")
6004    };
6005    // (a note the class's title page printed: its title-page provenance, base_utilities `TITLE_PAGE_ENTRY`)
6006    let note = what == "a note on the document";
6007    if note {
6008      calls.extend(store_from_tokens(name));
6009    }
6010    calls.extend(head);
6011    calls.push(T_BEGIN!());
6012    // (in a note, the class's `\and` between authors' blocks — nowfnt.cls:813-818 starts a new block — is a line break
6013    // between them: 2108.12618's cover list, which ran "…@gmail.com Matan Gavish")
6014    let and = T_CS!("\\and");
6015    calls.extend(value.unlist_ref().iter().map(|t| {
6016      if *t == and && what == "a note on the document" {
6017        T_CS!("\\\\")
6018      } else {
6019        *t
6020      }
6021    }));
6022    calls.push(T_END!());
6023    if note {
6024      calls.extend(store_from_tokens(""));
6025    }
6026    Info!(
6027      "frontmatter",
6028      s!("\\{name}"),
6029      s!(
6030        "The class's title page prints the store of \\{name}, which no title-page run here typeset: it is handed on as {what}"
6031      )
6032    );
6033  }
6034  calls.extend(rows);
6035  if calls.is_empty() {
6036    return Ok(Vec::new());
6037  }
6038  Ok(vec![digest_store_calls(calls)?])
6039}
6040
6041/// The words of each creator's name in the frontmatter (each creator's own digested content, its personname; not the
6042/// contacts annotating it), for [`repeats_creator_names`].
6043fn creator_name_words() -> Vec<FxHashSet<String>> {
6044  let mut out: Vec<FxHashSet<String>> = Vec::new();
6045  with_value("frontmatter", |v| {
6046    if let Some(Stored::HashTagData(frontmatter)) = v
6047      && let Some(creators) = frontmatter.get("ltx:creator")
6048    {
6049      for creator in creators {
6050        let mut text = String::new();
6051        for item in &creator.content {
6052          if let TagContent::Box(d) = item {
6053            ink_text(d, &mut text);
6054            text.push(' ');
6055          }
6056        }
6057        let words: FxHashSet<String> = text.split_whitespace().map(String::from).collect();
6058        if !words.is_empty() {
6059          out.push(words);
6060        }
6061      }
6062    }
6063  });
6064  out
6065}
6066
6067/// Does the store value `value` only repeat the creators' names — is every line of it (split at `\and`, `\\`,
6068/// `\newline` and `\par`, commas and the word "and" between names) plain text, characters and spaces only, each of whose
6069/// words is a word of a creator's name,
6070/// and does it name every creator (each creator's words are among its words: `creators`, [`creator_name_words`])? A
6071/// cover's author list that holds only the names (nowfnt.cls:803-806 `\maintitleauthorlist`, "AUTHORS FOR COVER
6072/// PAGE": 2308.02698) is a duplicate of the creators, a print flourish of the cover (the page-furniture ruling); one
6073/// that carries more (2108.12618's affiliations and e-mails) is not, nor is one that names some of the authors only — a
6074/// role, "Corresponding author: \@corr" with `\corresponding{Bob Jones}` (review probe `corr`; and a store the class
6075/// labels is never dropped, `hand_untypeset_stores`). By words, as the creators may hold a name in another order or
6076/// split (`\author[1]{Sung, Yoonchang}` gives two creators, SYNC_STATUS row "nowfnt creators").
6077fn repeats_creator_names(value: &Tokens, creators: &[FxHashSet<String>]) -> bool {
6078  if creators.is_empty() {
6079    return false;
6080  }
6081  let breaks = ["\\and", "\\\\", "\\newline", "\\par"].map(|cs| T_CS!(cs));
6082  let mut text = String::new();
6083  for t in value.unlist_ref() {
6084    match t.get_catcode() {
6085      // (the commas between the names)
6086      Catcode::OTHER if *t == T_OTHER!(",") => text.push(' '),
6087      Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER => text.push_str(&t.to_string()),
6088      Catcode::SPACE | Catcode::ACTIVE => text.push(' '),
6089      Catcode::BEGIN | Catcode::END => {},
6090      Catcode::CS if breaks.contains(t) => text.push(' '),
6091      _ => return false,
6092    }
6093  }
6094  // (and the conjunction before the last, as `\and` is: "Ann Able and Bob Baker")
6095  let words: FxHashSet<&str> = text
6096    .split_whitespace()
6097    .filter(|word| !matches!(*word, "and" | "&"))
6098    .collect();
6099  !words.is_empty()
6100    && words
6101      .iter()
6102      .all(|word| creators.iter().any(|creator| creator.contains(*word)))
6103    && creators
6104      .iter()
6105      .all(|creator| creator.iter().all(|word| words.contains(word.as_str())))
6106}
6107
6108/// The table's keywords setters: those whose frontmatter API is `\lx@add@keywords`.
6109fn keywords_setters() -> impl Iterator<Item = (&'static str, &'static str)> {
6110  STORE_SETTERS
6111    .iter()
6112    .copied()
6113    .filter(|(_, api)| api.starts_with("\\lx@add@keywords"))
6114}
6115
6116/// The tokens of `body` from `i` on, spaces skipped.
6117fn skip_spaces(body: &[Token], mut i: usize) -> usize {
6118  while body
6119    .get(i)
6120    .is_some_and(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE)
6121  {
6122    i += 1;
6123  }
6124  i
6125}
6126
6127/// How a definition nested in a body makes its command: `\def`, `\gdef`, `\renewcommand` replace a meaning the name
6128/// has; `\newcommand` and `\providecommand` keep it (LaTeX's `\newcommand` of a defined name is an error that leaves it,
6129/// latex.ltx `\@ifdefinable`).
6130#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
6131enum Definer {
6132  Replaces,
6133  Keeps,
6134}
6135
6136/// Where a definition's body first uses its argument `#1` — as stored in it (`#1`), or doubled in an enclosing
6137/// definition's body (`##1`, kept as a parameter token and `1`).
6138fn argument_at(body: &[Token]) -> Option<usize> {
6139  (0..body.len()).find(|&k| argument_length(body, k).is_some())
6140}
6141
6142/// The number of tokens of a use of `#1` at `body[k]` ([`argument_at`]), if there is one there.
6143fn argument_length(body: &[Token], k: usize) -> Option<usize> {
6144  let t = body.get(k)?;
6145  if t.get_catcode() == Catcode::ARG && t.with_str(|s| s == "1") {
6146    return Some(1);
6147  }
6148  (t.get_catcode() == Catcode::PARAM
6149    && body
6150      .get(k + 1)
6151      .is_some_and(|d| d.get_catcode() != Catcode::PARAM && d.with_str(|s| s == "1")))
6152  .then_some(2)
6153}
6154
6155/// A one-argument command `\<name>` that `body` defines — `\def\name#1{`, `\newcommand{\name}[1]{` (with any `##`
6156/// doubling and `\long`/`\global` prefix), and nothing that reads a delimited or optional argument: its definer and
6157/// its body, as stored (a doubled `##1` kept as a parameter token and `1`) — the first whose body uses its argument
6158/// ([`argument_at`]), else the first. A definition that drops or only warns about its argument is not a setter of it
6159/// (beilstein.cls:854 `\newcommand{\keywords}[1]{\ClassWarning{beilstein}{Keywords should not be part of …}}` beside
6160/// :853's typesetting one, fbithesis.cls:272 `{\fbi@warning{\keywords}}`). `None` when it defines none.
6161fn nested_one_arg_definition(body: &[Token], name: &str) -> Option<(Definer, Vec<Token>)> {
6162  let target = T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"));
6163  let mut first: Option<(Definer, Vec<Token>)> = None;
6164  for (i, t) in body.iter().enumerate() {
6165    if t.get_catcode() != Catcode::CS {
6166      continue;
6167    }
6168    let definer = t.with_str(|s| match s {
6169      "\\def" | "\\gdef" | "\\edef" | "\\xdef" => Some((true, Definer::Replaces)),
6170      "\\renewcommand" | "\\DeclareRobustCommand" => Some((false, Definer::Replaces)),
6171      "\\newcommand" | "\\providecommand" => Some((false, Definer::Keeps)),
6172      _ => None,
6173    });
6174    let Some((primitive, definer)) = definer else {
6175      continue;
6176    };
6177    let mut j = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
6178    if primitive {
6179      if body.get(j) != Some(&target) {
6180        continue;
6181      }
6182      // the parameter text: `#1` (stored `#`… `1`, a doubled `##1` in a definition's own body)
6183      let mut digits: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
6184      j += 1;
6185      while let Some(p) = body.get(j)
6186        && p.get_catcode() != Catcode::BEGIN
6187      {
6188        match p.get_catcode() {
6189          Catcode::PARAM => {},
6190          Catcode::ARG | Catcode::OTHER => digits.push(p.with_str(str::to_string)),
6191          _ => {
6192            digits.push(String::new());
6193            break;
6194          },
6195        }
6196        j += 1;
6197      }
6198      if digits == ["1"]
6199        && let Some(past) = tokens_group_end(body, j)
6200      {
6201        let own = &body[j + 1..past - 1];
6202        if argument_at(own).is_some() {
6203          return Some((definer, own.to_vec()));
6204        }
6205        first.get_or_insert_with(|| (definer, own.to_vec()));
6206      }
6207      continue;
6208    }
6209    if body.get(j).is_some_and(|b| *b == T_OTHER!("*")) {
6210      j = skip_spaces(body, j + 1);
6211    }
6212    if body.get(j) == Some(&target) {
6213      j += 1;
6214    } else if body
6215      .get(j)
6216      .is_some_and(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN)
6217      && body.get(skip_spaces(body, j + 1)) == Some(&target)
6218    {
6219      j = skip_spaces(body, skip_spaces(body, j + 1) + 1);
6220      if body.get(j).is_none_or(|b| b.get_catcode() != Catcode::END) {
6221        continue;
6222      }
6223      j += 1;
6224    } else {
6225      continue;
6226    }
6227    j = skip_spaces(body, j);
6228    let one_arg = [T_OTHER!("["), T_OTHER!("1"), T_OTHER!("]")];
6229    if body.get(j..j + 3) == Some(&one_arg[..]) {
6230      let k = skip_spaces(body, j + 3);
6231      if let Some(past) = tokens_group_end(body, k) {
6232        let own = &body[k + 1..past - 1];
6233        if argument_at(own).is_some() {
6234          return Some((definer, own.to_vec()));
6235        }
6236        first.get_or_insert_with(|| (definer, own.to_vec()));
6237      }
6238    }
6239  }
6240  first
6241}
6242
6243/// The commands a body hands control to: each control sequence; `\begin{X}`/`\end{X}` as `\X`/`\endX`; a
6244/// `\csname X\endcsname` of characters as `\X` (environ.sty's `\csname env@X@process\endcsname`); a command whose
6245/// redefinition the kernel's lock refused also as the refused definition (`\lx@dropped@X`); and the kernel's locked
6246/// `\maketitle` as the class's `\@maketitle`, which it digests (`\lx@deposit@maketitle`, sect05.rs) as LaTeX's
6247/// `\maketitle` calls it.
6248fn handed_to(body: &[Token], out: &mut Vec<Token>) {
6249  let chars_until = |from: usize, stop: &dyn Fn(&Token) -> bool| -> Option<(String, usize)> {
6250    let mut name = String::new();
6251    let mut k = from;
6252    while let Some(t) = body.get(k) {
6253      if stop(t) {
6254        return Some((name, k + 1));
6255      }
6256      if !matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER) {
6257        return None;
6258      }
6259      t.with_str(|s| name.push_str(s));
6260      k += 1;
6261    }
6262    None
6263  };
6264  let mut i = 0;
6265  while i < body.len() {
6266    let t = body[i];
6267    i += 1;
6268    if !matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::CS | Catcode::ACTIVE) {
6269      continue;
6270    }
6271    if (t == T_CS!("\\begin") || t == T_CS!("\\end"))
6272      && body
6273        .get(i)
6274        .is_some_and(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN)
6275      && let Some((env, past)) = chars_until(i + 1, &|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::END)
6276    {
6277      let prefix = if t == T_CS!("\\end") { "end" } else { "" };
6278      out.push(T_CS!(&s!("\\{prefix}{env}")));
6279      i = past;
6280      continue;
6281    }
6282    if t == T_CS!("\\csname")
6283      && let Some((cs, past)) = chars_until(i, &|b| *b == T_CS!("\\endcsname"))
6284    {
6285      out.push(T_CS!(&s!("\\{cs}")));
6286      i = past;
6287      continue;
6288    }
6289    out.push(t);
6290    let dropped = T_CS!(&t.with_cs_name(|cs| s!("\\lx@dropped@{}", cs.trim_start_matches('\\'))));
6291    if lookup_meaning(&dropped).is_some() {
6292      out.push(dropped);
6293    } else if t == T_CS!("\\maketitle") {
6294      out.push(T_CS!("\\@maketitle"));
6295    }
6296  }
6297}
6298
6299/// The definition of `target` a body makes with `\def`, `\gdef`, `\edef` or `\xdef` (a `\long`/`\global`/
6300/// `\protected` prefix kept): the tokens from the prefix through the definition's closing brace, its parameters as
6301/// stored (a doubled `##1` is a parameter token and `1`), so digesting them defines `target` as the body would. `None`
6302/// when the body makes none, or only one that reads the body's own arguments (it depends on the call), or only one
6303/// inside a conditional (`\ifshowfoo\def\foo{…}\fi`: whether the body makes it depends on a test that is not run
6304/// here — pdflatex leaves `\foo` undefined when the test is false).
6305fn nested_definition_of(body: &[Token], target: Token) -> Option<Vec<Token>> {
6306  let mut conditional_depth = 0usize;
6307  for (i, t) in body.iter().enumerate() {
6308    if t.get_catcode() != Catcode::CS {
6309      continue;
6310    }
6311    match conditional_kind(t) {
6312      Some(ConditionalType::If) => {
6313        conditional_depth += 1;
6314        continue;
6315      },
6316      Some(ConditionalType::Fi) => {
6317        conditional_depth = conditional_depth.saturating_sub(1);
6318        continue;
6319      },
6320      _ => {},
6321    }
6322    if conditional_depth > 0
6323      || !t.with_str(|s| matches!(s, "\\def" | "\\gdef" | "\\edef" | "\\xdef"))
6324    {
6325      continue;
6326    }
6327    let j = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
6328    if body.get(j) != Some(&target) {
6329      continue;
6330    }
6331    let mut k = j + 1;
6332    while body
6333      .get(k)
6334      .is_some_and(|p| p.get_catcode() != Catcode::BEGIN)
6335    {
6336      k += 1;
6337    }
6338    let Some(past) = tokens_group_end(body, k) else {
6339      continue;
6340    };
6341    if body[j..past]
6342      .iter()
6343      .any(|p| p.get_catcode() == Catcode::ARG)
6344    {
6345      continue;
6346    }
6347    let mut from = i;
6348    while from > 0
6349      && body[from - 1].with_str(|s| matches!(s, "\\long" | "\\global" | "\\protected"))
6350    {
6351      from -= 1;
6352    }
6353    return Some(body[from..past].to_vec());
6354  }
6355  None
6356}
6357
6358/// Whether `t` opens (`\if…`) or closes (`\fi`) a conditional: by its meaning, or, undefined, by an `\if` name (a
6359/// `\newif` switch the class declares later).
6360fn conditional_kind(t: &Token) -> Option<ConditionalType> {
6361  match lookup_definition(t) {
6362    Ok(Some(defn)) => defn.get_conditional_type(),
6363    _ => t
6364      .with_str(|s| s.starts_with("\\if"))
6365      .then_some(ConditionalType::If),
6366  }
6367}
6368
6369/// An undefined `token` that an open environment defines for its body in the class's own definition, which the
6370/// kernel's environment shadows: a `\<env>` the class (or the document) made, which `\begin{<env>}` never runs when the
6371/// kernel's `\begin{<env>}` constructor is defined (sect01.rs), or one the kernel's lock refused (`\lx@dropped@<env>`,
6372/// state.rs) — bmcart.cls:712-717 `\def\@figure[#1]{…\def\csentence##1{…}`
6373/// (its captions' lead sentence, 2305.08284, 1611.01327, 1909.07026) and :1505-1510 `\def\abstract@fmt[#1]{…
6374/// \def\parttitle##1{…}` (its structured abstract's headings, 2007.07886, 1907.07303, 2106.01700): the definition
6375/// is made where the call stands, as the class's environment would have made it, and the call retried
6376/// ([`latexml_core::binding::kernel_autoload::try_refused_environment_definition`]). Searched: the open environments
6377/// innermost first (the kernel's abstract, no environment frame, first inside its body; a frontmatter field's own
6378/// setter before them) whose class code never ran (refused, or shadowed by a constructor), each one's definitions and
6379/// the commands they hand control to (`handed_to`), only definitions a raw file or the document made, outside any
6380/// conditional, at most 64 per environment. An environment whose begin code ran is not searched:
6381/// `\newenvironment{myenv}{\ifshowfoo\def\foo{…}\fi}{}` leaves `\foo` undefined in its body, as in pdflatex. Guard:
6382/// `perfect_kernel_batch68v::an_environment_whose_code_ran_is_not_searched`. OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #647.
6383pub fn refused_environment_definition(token: &Token) -> bool {
6384  if token.get_catcode() != Catcode::CS || lookup_bool("lx_refused_environment_search") {
6385    return false;
6386  }
6387  let mut envs: Vec<String> = with_stacked_values_sym(pin("current_environment"), |vals| {
6388    vals.iter().rev().map(|v| v.to_string()).collect()
6389  });
6390  if lookup_bool("lx_in_kernel_abstract") {
6391    envs.insert(0, "abstract".to_string());
6392  }
6393  // A frontmatter field's content is typeset by the class where its setter put it: the commands the class's own
6394  // setter (refused: `\lx@dropped@author`) and what it hands control to define for the field's text — vmsta.cls:278-283
6395  // `\csdef{name@m}{\def\fnm##1{##1}\def\snm##1{##1}…}`, which its `\@@author` applies to each author
6396  // (`\author{\inits{Yu.}\fnm{Yuliya}\snm{Mishura}}`: 1508.02842, 1411.4499, 1601.01109).
6397  let item = lookup_string("lx_frontmatter_item");
6398  if let Some(field) = item.strip_prefix("ltx:") {
6399    envs.insert(
6400      0,
6401      if field == "creator" {
6402        "author".to_string()
6403      } else {
6404        field.to_string()
6405      },
6406    );
6407  }
6408  class_body_definition(token, &envs)
6409}
6410
6411/// Define `token` as the first of `envs` (environment or command names) defines it for its body in the class's own
6412/// code (`\lx@dropped@<name>` or `\<name>`, and what they hand control to): [`refused_environment_definition`].
6413fn class_body_definition(token: &Token, envs: &[String]) -> bool {
6414  let mut definition: Option<Vec<Token>> = None;
6415  'envs: for env in envs.iter().filter(|e| !e.is_empty()) {
6416    // Only code that never ran: the class's definition the kernel's lock refused (`\lx@dropped@<name>`), and its
6417    // `\<name>` when a constructor `\begin{<name>}` shadows it (`\begin`, sect01.rs). An environment whose own
6418    // `\<name>` began it has made what its body defines; a command it did not make stays undefined, as in pdflatex.
6419    let mut queue: VecDeque<Token> = VecDeque::new();
6420    let dropped = T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@dropped@{env}"));
6421    if lookup_meaning(&dropped).is_some() {
6422      queue.push_back(dropped);
6423    }
6424    if lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(&s!("\\begin{{{env}}}"))).is_some() {
6425      queue.push_back(T_CS!(&s!("\\{env}")));
6426    }
6427    let mut seen: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
6428    let mut next: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
6429    while let Some(cs) = queue.pop_front() {
6430      if seen.contains(&cs) || seen.len() >= 64 {
6431        continue;
6432      }
6433      seen.push(cs);
6434      let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(&cs) else {
6435        continue;
6436      };
6437      if !matches!(
6438        defn.get_origin(),
6439        DefinitionOrigin::File | DefinitionOrigin::Document
6440      ) {
6441        continue;
6442      }
6443      let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion() else {
6444        continue;
6445      };
6446      let body = body.unlist_ref();
6447      if let Some(found) = nested_definition_of(body, *token) {
6448        definition = Some(found);
6449        break 'envs;
6450      }
6451      next.clear();
6452      handed_to(body, &mut next);
6453      queue.extend(next.iter().copied());
6454    }
6455  }
6456  let Some(definition) = definition else {
6457    return false;
6458  };
6459  assign_value("lx_refused_environment_search", true, Some(Scope::Global));
6460  let made = with_origin(DefinitionOrigin::File, || digest(Tokens::new(definition)));
6461  assign_value("lx_refused_environment_search", false, Some(Scope::Global));
6462  made.is_ok() && lookup_meaning(token).is_some()
6463}
6464
6465/// The table's keywords setters that the class's (or the document's) abstract defines for its body and the kernel's
6466/// locked `{abstract}` therefore never runs, with their API and definer: a one-argument definition
6467/// ([`nested_one_arg_definition`]) in the refused `\abstract` or `\endabstract` (`\lx@dropped@…`, state.rs) or in a
6468/// command they hand control to ([`handed_to`]), read only in definitions a raw file or the document made — at most
6469/// 512 of them, nearest first — each with its API, definer and the class's own body for it.
6470fn abstract_setter_definitions() -> Vec<(&'static str, &'static str, Definer, Vec<Token>)> {
6471  let mut found: Vec<(&'static str, &'static str, Definer, Vec<Token>)> = Vec::new();
6472  let mut queue: VecDeque<Token> = VecDeque::from([
6473    T_CS!("\\lx@dropped@abstract"),
6474    T_CS!("\\lx@dropped@endabstract"),
6475  ]);
6476  if queue.iter().all(|t| lookup_meaning(t).is_none()) {
6477    return found;
6478  }
6479  let mut seen: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
6480  let mut next: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
6481  while let Some(cs) = queue.pop_front() {
6482    if seen.contains(&cs) || seen.len() >= 512 {
6483      continue;
6484    }
6485    seen.push(cs);
6486    let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(&cs) else {
6487      continue;
6488    };
6489    if !matches!(
6490      defn.get_origin(),
6491      DefinitionOrigin::File | DefinitionOrigin::Document
6492    ) {
6493      continue;
6494    }
6495    let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion() else {
6496      continue;
6497    };
6498    let body = body.unlist_ref();
6499    for (name, api) in keywords_setters() {
6500      // (a setter that typesets its argument wins over one found before that does not)
6501      let at = found.iter().position(|(n, ..)| *n == name);
6502      if at.is_some_and(|k| argument_at(&found[k].3).is_some()) {
6503        continue;
6504      }
6505      if let Some((definer, own)) = nested_one_arg_definition(body, name) {
6506        match at {
6507          None => found.push((name, api, definer, own)),
6508          Some(k) if argument_at(&own).is_some() => found[k] = (name, api, definer, own),
6509          Some(_) => {},
6510        }
6511      }
6512    }
6513    next.clear();
6514    handed_to(body, &mut next);
6515    queue.extend(next.iter().copied());
6516  }
6517  found
6518}
6519
6520/// LaTeX's font declarations taking no argument: the sizes (latex.ltx `\tiny` … `\Huge`), the NFSS family, series and
6521/// shape switches with `\normalfont`, `\em` and `\selectfont`, the LaTeX 2.09 ones (`\rm` … `\tt`) and `\normalcolor`.
6522pub(crate) const FONT_DECLARATIONS: &[&str] = &[
6523  "\\tiny",
6524  "\\scriptsize",
6525  "\\footnotesize",
6526  "\\small",
6527  "\\normalsize",
6528  "\\large",
6529  "\\Large",
6530  "\\LARGE",
6531  "\\huge",
6532  "\\Huge",
6533  "\\normalfont",
6534  "\\rmfamily",
6535  "\\sffamily",
6536  "\\ttfamily",
6537  "\\bfseries",
6538  "\\mdseries",
6539  "\\itshape",
6540  "\\upshape",
6541  "\\slshape",
6542  "\\scshape",
6543  "\\sscshape",
6544  "\\swshape",
6545  "\\ulcshape",
6546  "\\em",
6547  "\\selectfont",
6548  "\\rm",
6549  "\\sf",
6550  "\\tt",
6551  "\\bf",
6552  "\\it",
6553  "\\sl",
6554  "\\sc",
6555  "\\normalcolor",
6556];
6557
6558/// Whether `t` means one of [`FONT_DECLARATIONS`] (2508.08560's `\tt`, `\LARGE`; [`means_one_of`]).
6559pub(crate) fn font_declaration(t: &Token) -> bool { means_one_of(t, FONT_DECLARATIONS) }
6560
6561/// Whether `t` means one of the commands `names`: by name, or a `\let` copy of one (`\ifx`'s test, `x_equals`) other
6562/// than one of `\relax` (latex.ltx's `\let\normalcolor\relax` until color loads). A copy shares its source's
6563/// definition, so a definition of `t`'s own name is no copy and is read by name only.
6564pub(crate) fn means_one_of(t: &Token, names: &[&str]) -> bool {
6565  cs_in(t, names)
6566    || (lookup_meaning(t)
6567      .and_then(|m| m.to_definition())
6568      .is_some_and(|d| *d.get_cs() != *t)
6569      && !x_equals(t, &TOKEN_RELAX)
6570      && names.iter().any(|name| x_equals(t, &T_CS!(*name))))
6571}
6572
6573/// Besides [`FONT_DECLARATIONS`], the declarations and spacing that typeset nothing (paragraph shape, breaks), taking
6574/// no argument: [`layout`] reads both (also the declarations an author line's separator takes the gap before:
6575/// `base_utilities::spaced_name_separators`).
6576const LAYOUT: &[&str] = &[
6577  "\\noindent",
6578  "\\indent",
6579  "\\par",
6580  "\\ignorespaces",
6581  "\\relax",
6582  "\\leavevmode",
6583  "\\unskip",
6584  "\\nobreak",
6585  "\\centering",
6586  "\\raggedright",
6587  "\\raggedleft",
6588  "\\bgroup",
6589  "\\smallskip",
6590  "\\medskip",
6591  "\\bigskip",
6592];
6593/// Whether `t` means a command that typesets nothing and takes no argument: a font declaration or one of [`LAYOUT`].
6594pub(crate) fn layout(t: &Token) -> bool { font_declaration(t) || means_one_of(t, LAYOUT) }
6595/// Spacing and font commands that typeset nothing, with the number of braced arguments they take (`\vspace*` too).
6596const LAYOUT_ARGS: &[(&str, usize)] = &[
6597  ("\\fontsize", 2),
6598  ("\\fontfamily", 1),
6599  ("\\fontseries", 1),
6600  ("\\fontshape", 1),
6601  ("\\fontencoding", 1),
6602  ("\\usefont", 4),
6603  ("\\vspace", 1),
6604  ("\\addvspace", 1),
6605  ("\\hspace", 1),
6606];
6607/// Glue and dimension parameters (and skips) set or inserted by a glue that follows them: `\rightskip5.5pc`,
6608/// `\vskip 12\p@`, `\leftskip2pc\rightskip2pc plus1fill` (pasa.cls:56, tADR2e.cls:85).
6609const GLUE_SETTERS: &[&str] = &[
6610  "\\leftskip",
6611  "\\rightskip",
6612  "\\parindent",
6613  "\\parskip",
6614  "\\vskip",
6615  "\\hskip",
6616  "\\kern",
6617];
6618/// Sectioning commands whose (starred) heading carries the label: paper.cls:621 `\paragraph*{\keywordname:}`,
6619/// acmconf.cls:340 `\section*{KEYWORDS}`.
6620const HEADINGS: &[&str] = &[
6621  "\\section",
6622  "\\subsection",
6623  "\\subsubsection",
6624  "\\paragraph",
6625  "\\subparagraph",
6626];
6627/// Font commands whose argument is label text.
6628const STYLED: &[&str] = &[
6629  "\\textbf", "\\textit", "\\textsc", "\\textsf", "\\emph", "\\textrm",
6630];
6631
6632/// `t` is a control sequence named in `set`: `set.iter().any(|cs| *t == T_CS!(*cs))` (the same predicate as `Token`
6633/// equality), read in place, with no symbol interned per entry.
6634pub(crate) fn cs_in(t: &Token, set: &[&str]) -> bool {
6635  t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS && t.with_str(|s| set.contains(&s))
6636}
6637
6638/// The expansion of `t` when it is a parameterless macro with a token body.
6639fn parameterless_body(t: &Token) -> Option<Tokens> {
6640  let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(t) else {
6641    return None;
6642  };
6643  if defn
6644    .get_parameters()
6645    .is_some_and(|p| !p.get_parameters().is_empty())
6646  {
6647    return None;
6648  }
6649  match defn.get_expansion() {
6650    Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) => Some(body.clone()),
6651    _ => None,
6652  }
6653}
6654
6655/// The index past the glue `body[i..]` starts with — an optional `=`, a sign, a number and a unit (two letters, or a
6656/// dimension command as `\p@`), then `plus`/`minus` glue (a `fil` unit there) — or `None` when it is not one.
6657fn glue_end(body: &[Token], i: usize) -> Option<usize> {
6658  let ch = |k: usize| -> Option<char> {
6659    body
6660      .get(k)
6661      .filter(|t| matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::OTHER | Catcode::LETTER))
6662      .and_then(|t| t.with_str(|s| s.chars().next()))
6663  };
6664  let dimen = |mut k: usize, fil: bool| -> Option<usize> {
6665    k = skip_spaces(body, k);
6666    while ch(k).is_some_and(|c| c == '-' || c == '+') {
6667      k = skip_spaces(body, k + 1);
6668    }
6669    let digits = k;
6670    while ch(k).is_some_and(|c| c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '.' || c == ',') {
6671      k += 1;
6672    }
6673    let had_digits = k > digits;
6674    k = skip_spaces(body, k);
6675    if let Some(t) = body.get(k)
6676      && t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS
6677    {
6678      // `12\p@`, `\z@`, `0.5\baselineskip`: a dimension or glue command
6679      return matches!(
6680        lookup_meaning(t),
6681        Some(Stored::Register(_)) | Some(Stored::Expandable(_))
6682      )
6683      .then_some(k + 1);
6684    }
6685    if !had_digits {
6686      return None;
6687    }
6688    let unit: String = (k..k + 2).filter_map(ch).collect();
6689    const UNITS: &[&str] = &[
6690      "pt", "pc", "in", "cm", "mm", "bp", "dd", "cc", "sp", "em", "ex",
6691    ];
6692    if fil && unit == "fi" && ch(k + 2) == Some('l') {
6693      let mut e = k + 3;
6694      while ch(e) == Some('l') {
6695        e += 1;
6696      }
6697      return Some(e);
6698    }
6699    UNITS.contains(&unit.as_str()).then_some(k + 2)
6700  };
6701  let mut k = skip_spaces(body, i);
6702  if ch(k) == Some('=') {
6703    k += 1;
6704  }
6705  k = dimen(k, false)?;
6706  for keyword in ["plus", "minus"] {
6707    let j = skip_spaces(body, k);
6708    let word: String = (j..j + keyword.len()).filter_map(ch).collect();
6709    if word == keyword {
6710      k = dimen(j + keyword.len(), true)?;
6711    }
6712  }
6713  Some(k)
6714}
6715
6716/// A label's text as it is collected from the class's code: its characters and text macros, the layout around them
6717/// dropped — a source space, a horizontal skip or a kern between two pieces (a class macro's too: `\quad`) read as one
6718/// space, an explicit space (`\ `, `~`) kept as one — so that neither a font nor a layout command reaches the `name` it
6719/// becomes, nor the keywords after it: emsprocart's `{\noindent\bfseries{\keywordname.}\vspace{\z@}}` is "Keywords."
6720/// (1202.4161, 1006.0632, 1610.08545), ieice.cls's `{\bfseries key words:\ \ignorespaces}` "key words:" (2002.00666,
6721/// 1604.03276, 2509.21881), lni.cls's `\noindent{\bfseries Keywords:}` "Keywords:" (1604.03313). A class macro that is
6722/// text stays itself, read when the label is (a language's `\keywordname`).
6723#[derive(Default)]
6724struct LabelText {
6725  text:          Vec<Token>,
6726  pending_space: bool,
6727}
6728
6729impl LabelText {
6730  /// A space between pieces of the label, if one follows.
6731  fn space(&mut self) { self.pending_space |= !self.text.is_empty(); }
6732
6733  /// A piece of the label's text (a space token: an explicit one).
6734  fn push(&mut self, t: Token) {
6735    let is_space = t.get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE;
6736    let after_space = self
6737      .text
6738      .last()
6739      .is_some_and(|l| l.get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE);
6740    if self.pending_space && !is_space && !after_space {
6741      self.text.push(T_SPACE!());
6742    }
6743    self.pending_space = false;
6744    if is_space && (after_space || self.text.is_empty()) {
6745      return;
6746    }
6747    self.text.push(t);
6748  }
6749
6750  /// Is there text in it: a letter, or a class macro that is text?
6751  fn has_text(&self) -> bool {
6752    self
6753      .text
6754      .iter()
6755      .any(|t| matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::CS))
6756  }
6757}
6758
6759/// Read one item of a label's code from `body[i]` into `out`, returning the index past it: layout that typesets nothing,
6760/// or label material, of which its text is kept ([`LabelText`]) — `None` for anything else: an assignment, a definition,
6761/// a list, a box, a conditional, math. `depth` bounds the class macros followed.
6762fn label_item(body: &[Token], i: usize, depth: usize, out: &mut LabelText) -> Option<usize> {
6763  let t = body[i];
6764  match t.get_catcode() {
6765    Catcode::SPACE => {
6766      out.space();
6767      return Some(i + 1);
6768    },
6769    Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER => {
6770      out.push(t);
6771      return Some(i + 1);
6772    },
6773    Catcode::ACTIVE if t == T_ACTIVE!('~') => {
6774      out.push(T_SPACE!());
6775      return Some(i + 1);
6776    },
6777    Catcode::BEGIN => {
6778      // a group of label material: `{\bfseries Keywords:}`, `{\itshape Some key words}` (biometrika.cls:526)
6779      let past = tokens_group_end(body, i)?;
6780      label_items(&body[i + 1..past - 1], depth, out)?;
6781      return Some(past);
6782    },
6783    Catcode::CS => {},
6784    _ => return None,
6785  }
6786  if layout(&t) {
6787    return Some(i + 1);
6788  }
6789  if t == T_CS!("\\ ") {
6790    out.push(T_SPACE!());
6791    return Some(i + 1);
6792  }
6793  if t == T_CS!("\\/") {
6794    return Some(i + 1);
6795  }
6796  if let Some((cs, nargs)) = LAYOUT_ARGS.iter().find(|(cs, _)| cs_in(&t, &[cs])) {
6797    let mut k = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
6798    if body.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("*")) {
6799      k += 1;
6800    }
6801    for _ in 0..*nargs {
6802      k = tokens_group_end(body, skip_spaces(body, k))?;
6803    }
6804    if *cs == "\\hspace" {
6805      out.space();
6806    }
6807    return Some(k);
6808  }
6809  if cs_in(&t, GLUE_SETTERS) {
6810    // (a horizontal skip or a kern between two pieces: one space, `\textbf{Key}\kern3pt\textbf{words}`)
6811    if t == T_CS!("\\hskip") || t == T_CS!("\\kern") {
6812      out.space();
6813    }
6814    return glue_end(body, i + 1);
6815  }
6816  if cs_in(&t, HEADINGS) || cs_in(&t, STYLED) {
6817    // a heading's argument is the label, the heading the class's presentation of it; a font command's is label text
6818    let mut k = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
6819    if cs_in(&t, HEADINGS) && body.get(k) == Some(&T_OTHER!("*")) {
6820      k = skip_spaces(body, k + 1);
6821    }
6822    let past = tokens_group_end(body, k)?;
6823    label_items(&body[k + 1..past - 1], depth, out)?;
6824    return Some(past);
6825  }
6826  // a macro of the class's own: its label text (`\keywordname`, letters only) or its layout (pasa.cls:56
6827  // `\def\keywordfont{\small\rightskip5.5pc}`)
6828  let expansion = parameterless_body(&t)?;
6829  let expansion = expansion.unlist_ref();
6830  if !expansion.is_empty()
6831    && expansion.iter().all(|e| {
6832      matches!(
6833        e.get_catcode(),
6834        Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER | Catcode::SPACE
6835      )
6836    })
6837  {
6838    out.push(t);
6839    return Some(i + 1);
6840  }
6841  if depth == 0 {
6842    return None;
6843  }
6844  // (read into the label itself, so that a space it makes stays — `\textbf{Key}\quad\textbf{words}` — and refused
6845  // when it typeset text)
6846  let typeset = out.text.len();
6847  label_items(expansion, depth - 1, out)?;
6848  (out.text.len() == typeset).then_some(i + 1)
6849}
6850
6851/// Read every item of a label's code ([`label_item`]), or `None` when one is not label material or layout.
6852fn label_items(body: &[Token], depth: usize, out: &mut LabelText) -> Option<()> {
6853  let mut k = 0;
6854  while k < body.len() {
6855    k = label_item(body, k, depth, out)?;
6856  }
6857  Some(())
6858}
6859
6860/// The class's label in the begin code of a `{keywords}` environment that typesets only a label: the code is layout
6861/// that typesets nothing ([`layout`], [`LAYOUT_ARGS`], [`GLUE_SETTERS`], a class macro made of them) around label
6862/// material — text, a heading's or font command's argument, a group of them, a class macro that is text — returned as
6863/// its text ([`LabelText`]): paper.cls:621 `\small\paragraph*{\keywordname:}`, pasa.cls:101-103
6864/// `\noindent\keywordfont\textbf{Keywords: }`, biometrika.cls:525-527, irmaems.cls:1131 `\noindent\small Keywords:`,
6865/// ieice.cls:952-954, emsprocart.cls (1202.4161), lni.cls (1604.03313's copy).
6866/// Code doing anything else — a flag its title page reads (`\global\keywordstrue`, tADR2e.cls:450), a box it fills
6867/// (ar-1col's `\global\setbox\@kwdbox\vbox\bgroup`), a list, a definition (TeX Live's lni.cls:561-565 `\def\and`), a
6868/// conditional (TeX Live's emsprocart.cls `\if@twocolumn`) — is not this shape, and the environment stays the class's.
6869fn label_only_begin_code(body: &[Token]) -> Option<Tokens> {
6870  let mut out = LabelText::default();
6871  label_items(body, 2, &mut out)?;
6872  // (an explicit space the label ends with is the class's spacing before the keywords, not the label's)
6873  while out
6874    .text
6875    .last()
6876    .is_some_and(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE)
6877  {
6878    out.text.pop();
6879  }
6880  out.has_text().then(|| Tokens::new(out.text))
6881}
6882
6883/// The class's label in a keywords setter's body, ahead of its argument: the code before its first `#1`, without the
6884/// group or font command the argument is in (`\emph{##1}`, `{\raggedright ##1\par}`), read as
6885/// [`label_only_begin_code`] reads a begin code — astlb.cls:200 `\medskip Key words:~~\emph{##1}` "Key words:"
6886/// (1202.2889), pazhastl.cls:191 `\medskip\emph{Keywords:}~~##1` "Keywords:" (astro-ph0306137) — or `None` when it
6887/// is not that shape: wiley-article.cls:449's `\allcaps[…]{keywords}` (1706.00599), mtt.cls:181's `\item` and
6888/// language-chosen `\mtt@keywords` (1310.0585) keep the default name.
6889fn label_before_argument(body: &[Token]) -> Option<Tokens> {
6890  let prefix = &body[..argument_at(body)?];
6891  let mut open: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
6892  for (k, t) in prefix.iter().enumerate() {
6893    match t.get_catcode() {
6894      Catcode::BEGIN => open.push(k),
6895      Catcode::END => {
6896        open.pop();
6897      },
6898      _ => {},
6899    }
6900  }
6901  let mut dropped = vec![false; prefix.len()];
6902  for &k in &open {
6903    dropped[k] = true;
6904    let mut p = k;
6905    while p > 0 && prefix[p - 1].get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE {
6906      p -= 1;
6907    }
6908    if p > 0 && (cs_in(&prefix[p - 1], STYLED) || cs_in(&prefix[p - 1], HEADINGS)) {
6909      dropped[p - 1] = true;
6910    }
6911  }
6912  let kept: Vec<Token> = prefix
6913    .iter()
6914    .zip(&dropped)
6915    .filter(|(_, d)| !**d)
6916    .map(|(t, _)| *t)
6917    .collect();
6918  label_only_begin_code(&kept)
6919}
6920
6921/// The label in the line before `body[at]` ([`ClassReaders::title_page_label`]; a line starts at a line break, a group's
6922/// or an environment's edge, another value, or a branch's `\else`/`\or` — the whole test's start when the branch holds
6923/// no label before the store; a phrase, at a `.`, `!`, `?`, `;` or `,`): the start of the label's tokens, the end of
6924/// what a deposit takes out with it (past `body[at]` when the label is in the store's own group, else the start of that
6925/// group; past the test's `\fi` when the label is before the test), and the label's text.
6926fn line_label_before(body: &[Token], at: usize) -> Option<(usize, usize, Tokens, bool)> {
6927  let is_value = |t: &Token| {
6928    t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS
6929      && matches!(lookup_meaning(t), Some(Stored::Expandable(ref d))
6930        if d.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File
6931          && d.get_parameters().is_none_or(|p| p.get_parameters().is_empty()))
6932      && parameterless_body(t).is_some_and(|b| {
6933        !b.unlist_ref().is_empty()
6934          && !b.unlist_ref().iter().all(|e| {
6935            matches!(
6936              e.get_catcode(),
6937              Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER | Catcode::SPACE
6938            )
6939          })
6940      })
6941  };
6942  let mut end = at + 1;
6943  let mut k = at;
6944  let mut start = 0;
6945  let mut after_break = false;
6946  // (the label is in the store's own group, which is the argument of a control sequence: `\footnote{KW: \@keywords}`,
6947  // `\href{mailto:\@email}{…}`)
6948  let mut in_argument = false;
6949  // (past the group last skipped, which starts at `k` while it is the item before)
6950  let mut group_past: Option<usize> = None;
6951  // (the conditional whose branch holds the store alone, the label before it: its opening and the index past its `\fi`)
6952  let mut conditional: Option<(usize, usize)> = None;
6953  // (the store is printed alone by the commands passed so far, each with what it prints nothing of:
6954  // `\href{mailto:\@corremail}{\textcolor{black}{\@corremail}}`, acl2023.sty:186 — the line goes on before them, brief 97b)
6955  let mut wrapped = false;
6956  // (an implicit `\bgroup` or `\egroup`, `\let` to a brace, opens or closes a group as one does: the store's own group
6957  // in `\insert\footins\bgroup\footnotesize Keywords: \@keywords\par\egroup`, 86g r3 review probe i3)
6958  let group_catcode = |t: &Token| {
6959    if *t == T_CS!("\\bgroup") {
6960      Catcode::BEGIN
6961    } else if *t == T_CS!("\\egroup") {
6962      Catcode::END
6963    } else {
6964      t.get_catcode()
6965    }
6966  };
6967  while k > 0 {
6968    let t = body[k - 1];
6969    match group_catcode(&t) {
6970      Catcode::END => {
6971        // a group before the store: one item of the line
6972        group_past = Some(k);
6973        let mut depth = 0i32;
6974        let mut open = None;
6975        for j in (0..k).rev() {
6976          match group_catcode(&body[j]) {
6977            Catcode::END => depth += 1,
6978            Catcode::BEGIN => {
6979              depth -= 1;
6980              if depth == 0 {
6981                open = Some(j);
6982                break;
6983              }
6984            },
6985            _ => {},
6986          }
6987        }
6988        k = open?;
6989        continue;
6990      },
6991      Catcode::BEGIN => {
6992        // the store's own group: the line goes on before it when the group holds only layout before the store
6993        let mut inner = LabelText::default();
6994        if (end == at + 1 || wrapped)
6995          && label_items(&body[k..end.min(at)], 0, &mut inner).is_some()
6996          && !inner.has_text()
6997        {
6998          end = k - 1;
6999          k -= 1;
7000          if k > 0 && (cs_in(&body[k - 1], STYLED) || layout(&body[k - 1])) {
7001            end = k - 1;
7002            k -= 1;
7003          } else if let Some((command, true)) = argument_of(body, k) {
7004            // (the printed argument of a command that prints nothing else: a link's text, a colour's)
7005            end = command;
7006            k = command;
7007            wrapped = true;
7008          }
7009          continue;
7010        }
7011        in_argument = body[..k - 1]
7012          .iter()
7013          .rev()
7014          .find(|b| b.get_catcode() != Catcode::SPACE)
7015          .is_some_and(|b| {
7016            b.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS && !cs_in(b, STYLED) && !cs_in(b, HEADINGS)
7017          });
7018        start = k;
7019        break;
7020      },
7021      Catcode::CS
7022        if t == T_CS!("\\par")
7023          || t == T_CS!("\\\\")
7024          || t == T_CS!("\\newline")
7025          || t == T_CS!("\\cr") =>
7026      {
7027        after_break = t != T_CS!("\\par");
7028        start = k;
7029        break;
7030      },
7031      // a branch's start starts the line in it: medima.sty:322-347 prints each history date in the `\else` branch of
7032      // its emptiness test, `\ifx\@finalform\@empty\relax\else \finalformhead~\@finalform\\ \fi`, the branch printed
7033      // exactly when the store has a value (2104.10903, 2307.16262; review rev95d B2) — `\maketitle`'s run of the title
7034      // page still checks the label prints right before the store (`verify_title_page_labels`)
7035      Catcode::CS if cs_in(&t, &["\\else", "\\or"]) => {
7036        // — unless the branch holds no label before the store: then the label is printed before the whole test,
7037        // `Received: \ifx\@received\@empty--\else\@received\fi` "Received:", and the test leaves the title page with
7038        // it (its `\fi` included), the store's branch being the one printed when the store has a value (review
7039        // rev95d-r2)
7040        let mut inner = LabelText::default();
7041        if conditional.is_none()
7042          && label_items(&body[k..end.min(at)], 0, &mut inner).is_some()
7043          && !inner.has_text()
7044          && let Some((open, past)) = enclosing_conditional(body, k - 1, at)
7045        {
7046          conditional = Some((open, past));
7047          k = open;
7048          continue;
7049        }
7050        start = k;
7051        break;
7052      },
7053      // an environment's begin or end starts the line after it: `\end{center}\noindent Resumen: \@resumen` (review
7054      // probes `d_own2`, `d_own3`), `\begin{quote}\itshape\@resumen` (`d_nolab`: no label)
7055      Catcode::CS
7056        if (t == T_CS!("\\begin") || t == T_CS!("\\end"))
7057          && group_past.is_some()
7058          && body
7059            .get(k)
7060            .is_some_and(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN) =>
7061      {
7062        start = group_past.unwrap_or(k);
7063        break;
7064      },
7065      Catcode::CS if is_value(&t) => {
7066        start = k;
7067        break;
7068      },
7069      _ => {},
7070    }
7071    k -= 1;
7072  }
7073  if after_break {
7074    // `\\*[<skip>]`
7075    start = skip_spaces(body, start);
7076    if body.get(start) == Some(&T_OTHER!("*")) {
7077      start = skip_spaces(body, start + 1);
7078    }
7079    if body.get(start) == Some(&T_OTHER!("[")) {
7080      start += body[start..end].iter().position(|b| *b == T_OTHER!("]"))? + 1;
7081    }
7082  }
7083  let mut label_end = if end == at + 1 { at } else { end };
7084  if let Some((open, past)) = conditional {
7085    label_end = label_end.min(open);
7086    end = past;
7087  }
7088  // The label is the phrase printed right before the store: a sentence before it in the same line stays where it is
7089  // (`\footnote{Funded. KW: \@keywords}`, review probe `fnm`: "KW:", the footnote keeps "Funded."), and so does a
7090  // clause before a `;` or a `,` — pazha2.cls:166-167's `Received \@submitted; revised \@revised; accepted
7091  // \@accepted` names the revised date "revised" (2408.01858, 2607.07582; review rev95d-r2).
7092  let mut depth = 0i32;
7093  let mut phrase = start;
7094  // (where the deposit's part starts: the phrase, or the `;` or `,` joining its clause to the one before, which leaves
7095  // with it — the title page keeps no "; ;" of the dates it hands on)
7096  let mut part = start;
7097  // (a store right after a `;` or `,` has no label of its own: the separator ends the clause before it —
7098  // iaus.cls's `{\itshape Proceedings IAU Symposium No. \@volume, \@pubyear}`, 0707.3527)
7099  let mut after_separator = false;
7100  for j in start..label_end {
7101    match body[j].get_catcode() {
7102      Catcode::BEGIN => depth += 1,
7103      Catcode::END => depth -= 1,
7104      Catcode::OTHER
7105        if depth == 0
7106          && body[j].with_str(|c| matches!(c, "." | "!" | "?" | ";" | ","))
7107          && body
7108            .get(j + 1)
7109            .is_some_and(|b| b.get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE) =>
7110      {
7111        let next = skip_spaces(body, j + 1);
7112        let separator = body[j].with_str(|c| matches!(c, ";" | ","));
7113        if next < label_end {
7114          phrase = next;
7115          part = if separator { j } else { next };
7116        } else {
7117          after_separator = separator;
7118        }
7119      },
7120      _ => {},
7121    }
7122  }
7123  if after_separator {
7124    return None;
7125  }
7126  let label = label_only_begin_code(&body[phrase..label_end])?;
7127  Some((part, end, label, in_argument))
7128}
7129
7130/// The text `body` prints right before `body[at]` ([`ClassReaders::prints_default_name_before`]): its characters back
7131/// to the first code that is no text — a branch's `\else`, another store, a primitive —, font and layout commands
7132/// passed over (a layout command's braced arguments too: `\hspace{1.5mm}`), a brace read as a word's edge, an explicit space (`~`, `\ `) read as one, and a parameterless macro of
7133/// a raw file's read as its own characters (wiley2sp.cls:296 `\def\receivedname{{\normalfont Received:}\ }`).
7134fn text_right_before(body: &[Token], at: usize) -> String {
7135  // (the open brace of the group a close brace at `body[k]` ends)
7136  let group_open = |k: usize| -> Option<usize> {
7137    let mut depth = 0usize;
7138    for j in (0..=k).rev() {
7139      match body[j].get_catcode() {
7140        Catcode::END => depth += 1,
7141        Catcode::BEGIN => {
7142          depth -= 1;
7143          if depth == 0 {
7144            return Some(j);
7145          }
7146        },
7147        _ => {},
7148      }
7149    }
7150    None
7151  };
7152  let mut pieces: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
7153  let mut k = at;
7154  let mut read = 0;
7155  while k > 0 && read < 64 {
7156    k -= 1;
7157    read += 1;
7158    let t = &body[k];
7159    match t.get_catcode() {
7160      Catcode::SPACE => pieces.push(" ".to_string()),
7161      Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER => pieces.push(t.to_string()),
7162      Catcode::ACTIVE if *t == T_ACTIVE!('~') => pieces.push(" ".to_string()),
7163      // (a layout command's braced arguments print nothing — rose_v01.cls:259's `\text{Received}\hspace{1.5mm}
7164      // \@received`, 0907.2032 —; any other brace bounds a word: `\vskip 2.5mm{\footnotesize Received:` prints "2.5mm"
7165      // and "Received:")
7166      Catcode::END => {
7167        // (the argument groups ending here, back to the command they follow)
7168        let mut first = group_open(k);
7169        while let Some(o) = first {
7170          let b = skip_spaces_back(body, o);
7171          if b > 0 && body[b - 1].get_catcode() == Catcode::END {
7172            first = group_open(b - 1);
7173            continue;
7174          }
7175          let mut cs = b;
7176          if cs > 0 && body[cs - 1] == T_OTHER!("*") {
7177            cs -= 1;
7178          }
7179          if cs > 0
7180            && LAYOUT_ARGS
7181              .iter()
7182              .any(|(name, _)| cs_in(&body[cs - 1], &[name]))
7183          {
7184            k = cs - 1;
7185          }
7186          break;
7187        }
7188        pieces.push(" ".to_string());
7189      },
7190      Catcode::BEGIN => pieces.push(" ".to_string()),
7191      Catcode::CS if *t == T_CS!("\\ ") || *t == T_CS!("\\space") => pieces.push(" ".to_string()),
7192      Catcode::CS if layout(t) || cs_in(t, STYLED) => {},
7193      Catcode::CS => match lookup_meaning(t) {
7194        Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) if defn.get_origin() == DefinitionOrigin::File => {
7195          match parameterless_body(t) {
7196            Some(expansion) => pieces.push(
7197              expansion
7198                .unlist_ref()
7199                .iter()
7200                .filter(|e| matches!(e.get_catcode(), Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER))
7201                .map(Token::to_string)
7202                .collect(),
7203            ),
7204            None => break,
7205          }
7206        },
7207        _ => break,
7208      },
7209      _ => break,
7210    }
7211  }
7212  pieces.iter().rev().map(String::as_str).collect()
7213}
7214
7215/// The index of the first of the spaces `body[..i]` ends with (`i` when none).
7216fn skip_spaces_back(body: &[Token], mut i: usize) -> usize {
7217  while i > 0 && body[i - 1].get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE {
7218    i -= 1;
7219  }
7220  i
7221}
7222
7223/// The conditional whose `\else` or `\or` is `body[branch]` and whose branch from there holds `body[at]`
7224/// ([`line_label_before`]): the index of its opening `\if…` (its `\unless` prefix too) and the index past its `\fi`,
7225/// the two in one brace level — `None` when either is not in `body`.
7226fn enclosing_conditional(body: &[Token], branch: usize, at: usize) -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
7227  let mut depth = 0usize;
7228  let mut open = None;
7229  for j in (0..branch).rev() {
7230    match conditional_kind(&body[j]) {
7231      Some(ConditionalType::Fi) => depth += 1,
7232      Some(ConditionalType::If) if depth == 0 => {
7233        open = Some(j);
7234        break;
7235      },
7236      Some(ConditionalType::If) => depth -= 1,
7237      _ => {},
7238    }
7239  }
7240  let mut open = open?;
7241  if open > 0
7242    && matches!(
7243      conditional_kind(&body[open - 1]),
7244      Some(ConditionalType::Unless)
7245    )
7246  {
7247    open -= 1;
7248  }
7249  let mut depth = 0usize;
7250  for j in at + 1..body.len() {
7251    match conditional_kind(&body[j]) {
7252      Some(ConditionalType::If) => depth += 1,
7253      Some(ConditionalType::Fi) if depth == 0 => {
7254        return Tokens::slice_is_balanced(&body[open..=j]).then_some((open, j + 1));
7255      },
7256      Some(ConditionalType::Fi) => depth -= 1,
7257      _ => {},
7258    }
7259  }
7260  None
7261}
7262
7263/// Does a keywords setter's body only typeset its label and its argument (layout, label material and `#1`, read as
7264/// [`label_only_begin_code`] reads a begin code)? Then it typesets nothing the kernel's abstract lacks, and an abstract
7265/// kept in place may run it: kwmtt.cls's `\par\noindent{\bfseries Keywords:} \itshape##1`, not mtt.cls:181's `\item`
7266/// of the class's own abstract list (1310.0585).
7267fn typesets_label_and_argument(body: &[Token]) -> bool {
7268  let mut text: Vec<Token> = Vec::with_capacity(body.len());
7269  let mut k = 0;
7270  while k < body.len() {
