7271    if let Some(length) = argument_length(body, k) {
7272      text.push(T_LETTER!("x"));
7273      k += length;
7274    } else {
7275      text.push(body[k]);
7276      k += 1;
7277    }
7278  }
7279  label_only_begin_code(&text).is_some()
7280}
7281
7282/// Is a `{keywords}` environment's begin code environ.sty's `\Collect@Body\X`, whose `\X` stores the collected body
7283/// — with the class's own wrapper around it — in one macro that only the title-page code reads ([`ClassReaders`])?
7284/// cclayout.sty:71-73 `\long\def\for@cc@keywords#1{\gdef\@keywords@macro{\begin{@abstr}{\cckeywordsname}#1\end
7285/// {@abstr}}}` `\newenvironment{keywords}{\Collect@Body\for@cc@keywords}{}`, `\@keywords@macro` read only by its
7286/// `\maketitle` (:889-898): the keywords of 1905.07821 and the cc papers reached neither the frontmatter nor the
7287/// body. The wrapper is the class's presentation of the keywords, as rule (a)'s title page is.
7288fn collects_body_for_the_title_page(begin: &[Token], readers: &mut Option<ClassReaders>) -> bool {
7289  let code: Vec<Token> = begin
7290    .iter()
7291    .filter(|t| t.get_catcode() != Catcode::SPACE)
7292    .copied()
7293    .collect();
7294  let [collect, setter] = code[..] else {
7295    return false;
7296  };
7297  if collect != T_CS!("\\Collect@Body") {
7298    return false;
7299  }
7300  let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(&setter) else {
7301    return false;
7302  };
7303  let one_arg = defn.get_parameters().is_some_and(
7304    |p| matches!(p.get_parameters().as_slice(), [only] if !only.optional && undelimited(only)),
7305  );
7306  let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion() else {
7307    return false;
7308  };
7309  if !one_arg || defn.get_origin() != DefinitionOrigin::File {
7310    return false;
7311  }
7312  let body = body.unlist_ref();
7313  let mut i = 0;
7314  while body
7315    .get(i)
7316    .is_some_and(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE || cs_in(t, DEFINITION_FRAME))
7317  {
7318    i += 1;
7319  }
7320  if !body.get(i).is_some_and(|t| cs_in(t, STORE_DEFINERS)) {
7321    return false;
7322  }
7323  let k = skip_spaces(body, i + 1);
7324  let Some(store) = body
7325    .get(k)
7326    .copied()
7327    .filter(|c| c.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS)
7328  else {
7329    return false;
7330  };
7331  let Some(past) = tokens_group_end(body, k + 1) else {
7332    return false;
7333  };
7334  argument_at(&body[k + 2..past - 1]).is_some()
7335    && skip_spaces(body, past) == body.len()
7336    && readers
7337      .get_or_insert_with(ClassReaders::survey)
7338      .read_only_by_the_title_page(&store)
7339}
7340
7341/// Reroute each table keywords setter the raw class `cls` defined as a `{keywords}`-like environment whose begin
7342/// code only typesets a label ([`label_only_begin_code`]) — `\<name>` taking no argument with its `\end<name>` — to the
7343/// frontmatter's keywords, named by the class's label (`\lx@class@keywords`): the environment as
7344/// `\lx@begin@keywords[name={label}]` … `\lx@end@keywords`, as mn2e's binding's (mn2e_support_sty.rs), the command
7345/// with a braced argument that ends the keywords as `\lx@add@keywords[name={label}]{…}`; the class's own begin and end
7346/// code stay for any other use (`\keywords {alpha}, beta`, a bare `\keywords alpha \endkeywords`). A document's own
7347/// environment, defined after the class, replaces it.
7348fn reroute_keywords_environments(cls: &str, readers: &mut Option<ClassReaders>) -> Result<()> {
7349  let mut rerouted: Vec<&str> = Vec::new();
7350  for (name, _) in keywords_setters() {
7351    if defined_by_a_binding(name)? {
7352      continue;
7353    }
7354    let begin = T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"));
7355    let end = T_CS!(&s!("\\end{name}"));
7356    let Some(defn) = lookup_definition(&begin)? else {
7357      continue;
7358    };
7359    if lookup_definition(&end)?.is_none()
7360      || defn
7361        .get_parameters()
7362        .is_some_and(|p| !p.get_parameters().is_empty())
7363      || !matches!(defn.get_origin(), DefinitionOrigin::File)
7364    {
7365      continue;
7366    }
7367    let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion() else {
7368      continue;
7369    };
7370    let label = match label_only_begin_code(body.unlist_ref()) {
7371      Some(label) => Some(label),
7372      None if collects_body_for_the_title_page(body.unlist_ref(), readers) => None,
7373      None => continue,
7374    };
7375    // The class's own begin and end code stay, for a bare `\keywords text` (`\lx@class@keywords`) and its
7376    // `\endkeywords` (`\lx@class@keywords@end`) outside the environment.
7377    for (own, cs) in [
7378      ("\\lx@class@keywords@", &begin),
7379      ("\\lx@class@keywords@end@", &end),
7380    ] {
7381      assign_meaning(
7382        &T_CS!(&s!("{own}{name}")),
7383        lookup_meaning(cs).unwrap_or(Stored::None),
7384        Some(Scope::Global),
7385      );
7386    }
7387    if let Some(label) = label {
7388      assign_value(
7389        &s!("lx_class_keywords_label_{name}"),
7390        Stored::Tokens(label),
7391        Some(Scope::Global),
7392      );
7393    }
7394    for (cs, reader) in [
7395      (begin, "\\lx@class@keywords{"),
7396      (end, "\\lx@class@keywords@end{"),
7397    ] {
7398      let mut call = mouth::tokenize_internal(reader).unlist();
7399      call.extend(ExplodeText!(name));
7400      call.push(T_END!());
7401      DefMacro!(cs, None, Tokens::new(call));
7402    }
7403    rerouted.push(name);
7404  }
7405  if !rerouted.is_empty() {
7406    Info!(
7407      "frontmatter",
7408      cls,
7409      s!(
7410        "Rerouted the raw class's keywords environment to the frontmatter API: {{{}}}",
7411        rerouted.join("}, {")
7412      )
7413    );
7414  }
7415  Ok(())
7416}
7417
7418/// The call `api` (`\lx@add@keywords{#1}`, the table's) named by the class's `label`, when there is one:
7419/// `\lx@add@keywords[name={label}]{#1}` (astlb.cls:200's "Key words:", 1202.2889; paper.cls:621's `\keywordname:`,
7420/// synthetic: kwpaper.cls's command forms).
7421fn named_keywords_call(api: &str, label: Option<Tokens>) -> Vec<Token> {
7422  let Some(label) = label else {
7423    return mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(api.to_string())).unlist();
7424  };
7425  let (command, rest) = api.split_at(api.find(['[', '{']).unwrap_or(api.len()));
7426  let mut call = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!("{command}[name={{"))).unlist();
7427  call.extend(label.unlist());
7428  call.push(T_END!());
7429  call.push(T_OTHER!("]"));
7430  call.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(rest.to_string())).unlist());
7431  call
7432}
7433
7434/// Does the input go on with a braced group that ends the keywords — `\par`, a blank line, an `\end…` or the end of
7435/// the input after it, spaces skipped? Every token read is put back. `\keywords {alpha}, beta, gamma` is not that:
7436/// its first group is part of the keywords (synthetic, the review's t3 shape). Nor is a group a `\section`,
7437/// `\maketitle` or `\begin{…}` follows: there the class's own code runs, as before 68n.
7438fn braced_group_ends_the_keywords() -> Result<bool> {
7439  let Some(open) = read_token()? else {
7440    return Ok(false);
7441  };
7442  let mut read = vec![open];
7443  let mut ends = false;
7444  if open.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN {
7445    let (group, close) = read_balanced_with_close(ExpansionLevel::Off, false, false)?;
7446    read.extend(group.unlist());
7447    if let Some(close) = close {
7448      read.push(close);
7449      loop {
7450        let next = read_token()?;
7451        if let Some(t) = next
7452          && matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::SPACE | Catcode::COMMENT)
7453        {
7454          read.push(t);
7455          continue;
7456        }
7457        ends = next.is_none_or(|t| {
7458          t == T_CS!("\\par")
7459            || (t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS && t.with_str(|s| s.starts_with("\\end")))
7460        });
7461        read.extend(next);
7462        break;
7463      }
7464    }
7465  }
7466  unread(Tokens::new(read));
7467  Ok(ends)
7468}
7469
7470/// The calls handing the last `\author` call's tail (`\lx@author@handed`) to the creators it made.
7471/// `at_maketitle`: no `\author` call follows, so when this one made every author, all are queued and
7472/// the tail's unmarked lines may be placed by the marks (`affiliation_calls`); else they are rows of
7473/// this call's creators, as a later author's marks are not yet known.
7474/// A tail holding `\and` at its top level (a `\coauthor` appending `\and #1`: jaa.cls, pramana.cls `\addauthor`) goes on
7475/// the block with more authors, which LaTeX's `\@maketitle` prints as more columns: what comes before its first `\and`
7476/// is rows of this call's creators, the rest one more author block (`\lx@add@authors@append`), split at its `\and`s and
7477/// read as the `\author` argument is (RR1 gap 24; witness 2511.15315).
7478/// At `\maketitle` with no `\author` call handed, the tail of a binding's `\@author` the setters never stored
7479/// ([`seen_author_tail`]: authblk's, 2511.15315).
7480/// Either route reads a tail only where the title page in force prints it: at `\maketitle`, as the title-page reading
7481/// says (`latex_constructs::title_page_reading`: its run of the page a raw file or the document wrote, else the walk of
7482/// it, else the declaration of the binding owning `\author`, `fm_author_tail_declared`), before it (an appending
7483/// `\author`'s flush) by the walk and the declaration alone ([`own_title_page_prints_author`]). One that prints only
7484/// the title (a document's or a raw class's `\maketitle` or `\@maketitle`) prints neither the names nor the lines
7485/// appended (review revFX24 finding 1); a document's addition to LaTeX's `\@maketitle`, or a `\let` copy of it, prints
7486/// both (revFX24b, TP1); amsart under a document's `\@maketitle` printing `\@author` prints the tail its own page
7487/// would not (FX26's RED, TP1).
7488fn author_tail_calls(at_maketitle: bool) -> Result<Vec<Token>> {
7489  // (`under_block`: the binding route's tail, printed under the whole block)
7490  let (handed, made, under_block) = match lookup_value("lx_author_handed") {
7491    Some(Stored::Tokens(handed)) => (handed, lookup_int("lx_author_made"), false),
7492    _ if at_maketitle => match seen_author_tail()? {
7493      Some((seen, made)) => (seen, made, true),
7494      None => return Ok(Vec::new()),
7495    },
7496    _ => return Ok(Vec::new()),
7497  };
7498  let Some(current) = store_value("author")? else {
7499    return Ok(Vec::new());
7500  };
7501  let (handed, current) = (handed.unlist_ref(), current.unlist_ref());
7502  if current.len() <= handed.len() || current[..handed.len()] != *handed {
7503    return Ok(Vec::new());
7504  }
7505  let tail = &current[handed.len()..];
7506  let printed = if at_maketitle {
7507    crate::latex_constructs::title_page_reading::title_page_reading()?.tail_printed()
7508  } else {
7509    own_title_page_prints_author().or_else(fm_author_tail_declared)
7510  };
7511  if printed != Some(true) {
7512    return Ok(Vec::new());
7513  }
7514  // (the tail's first top-level `\and`, by spelling as the stores' numbered split is: P26)
7515  let and = T_CS!("\\and");
7516  let mut depth = 0i32;
7517  let mut split = None;
7518  for (i, token) in tail.iter().enumerate() {
7519    match token.get_catcode() {
7520      Catcode::BEGIN => depth += 1,
7521      Catcode::END => depth -= 1,
7522      _ if depth == 0 && *token == and => {
7523        split = Some(i);
7524        break;
7525      },
7526      _ => {},
7527    }
7528  }
7529  let (rows, more) = match split {
7530    Some(i) => (&tail[..i], Some(Tokens::new(tail[i + 1..].to_vec()))),
7531    None => (tail, None),
7532  };
7533  let mut calls = row_calls(Tokens::new(rows.to_vec()), made, at_maketitle, under_block)?;
7534  if let Some(more) = more.filter(|more| !is_blank(more)) {
7535    calls.extend(Invocation!(T_CS!("\\lx@add@authors@append"), vec![None, Some(more)]).unlist());
7536  }
7537  Ok(calls)
7538}
7539
7540/// The value of `\@author` when the frontmatter last took an author from a binding's setter that keeps its own list
7541/// (`lx_author_seen`, `note_author_seen`), when creators are queued. The tail appended after it is printed under the
7542/// whole block: every queued creator's — where the title page in force prints it ([`author_tail_calls`]: authblk's own
7543/// `\@author` prints the block, authblk.sty:167-171, llncs's `\@maketitle` prints `\@author`, llncs.cls:1035, as their
7544/// bindings declare, `AuthorPage::Block`; not amsart's page, which prints `\authors`, nor elsarticle's `\elsauthors`).
7545fn seen_author_tail() -> Result<Option<(Tokens, i64)>> {
7546  let Some(Stored::Tokens(seen)) = lookup_value("lx_author_seen") else {
7547    return Ok(None);
7548  };
7549  let creators = queued_creator_count();
7550  if creators == 0 {
7551    return Ok(None);
7552  }
7553  Ok(Some((seen, creators as i64)))
7554}
7555
7556/// Does the title page of the file's or the document's own print `\@author`, as the code that runs at `\maketitle`
7557/// reads it? A stand-in copy's own title page answers for itself ([`copy_title_page_prints`]). Else the title page a raw
7558/// file or the document defined — the refused `\maketitle` (`\lx@dropped@maketitle`), else a `\@maketitle` of the
7559/// file's or the document's own — is walked through the live code it reaches (`reads_of_branches`, the switches as they
7560/// are; the macros a raw file or the document defined): it prints `\@author` when it reads it, or reaches LaTeX's or a
7561/// binding's `\maketitle` or `\@maketitle` by its current meaning (a `\let` copy of the current `\maketitle` the body
7562/// calls), or the kernel's marker of LaTeX's own `\@maketitle` (`\lx@kernel@maketitle`: an addition to it or a `\let`
7563/// copy of it, revFX24b), which print it (article.cls:247; a binding's title page is the frontmatter's). A copy of
7564/// `\@author` is not seen (RED `article_author_tail_printed_through_let_copy_of_author_is_lost_red`). `None` when there
7565/// is no such title page: the page is LaTeX's or the binding's (`fm_author_tail_declared`). A raw class's own
7566/// `\maketitle` that prints only the title, or a document's `\renewcommand\maketitle`/`\def\@maketitle`, does not
7567/// (review revFX24 finding 1; 2511.15315's procdoc2.cls:77 does). The title-page reading's answer where its run cannot
7568/// give one (`latex_constructs::title_page_reading`), and the answer before `\maketitle`.
7569pub(crate) fn own_title_page_prints_author() -> Option<bool> {
7570  if let Some(prints) = copy_title_page_prints(&T_CS!("\\@author")) {
7571    return Some(prints);
7572  }
7573  let own = |cs: &Token| body_from(cs, &[DefinitionOrigin::File, DefinitionOrigin::Document]);
7574  let maketitle = T_CS!("\\@maketitle");
7575  // (a `\@maketitle` of the file's or the document's own only where it is the page in force: the `\maketitle` that runs
7576  // runs it, `own_maketitle`, TP1b)
7577  let root = own(&T_CS!("\\lx@dropped@maketitle"))
7578    .or_else(|| own(&maketitle).filter(|_| crate::latex_constructs::own_maketitle()))?;
7579  let target = T_CS!("\\@author").get_cs_name();
7580  // (LaTeX's title page, which prints `\@author`: the kernel's `\maketitle` and `\@maketitle`, and a copy of either)
7581  let latex_maketitle = T_CS!("\\maketitle");
7582  let title_pages: Vec<Stored> = [latex_maketitle, maketitle]
7583    .iter()
7584    .filter_map(lookup_meaning)
7585    .collect();
7586  let is_title_page = |cs: &Token| {
7587    lookup_meaning(cs).is_some_and(|meaning| match &meaning {
7588      Stored::Expandable(defn) => title_pages
7589        .iter()
7590        .any(|page| matches!(page, Stored::Expandable(p) if Rc::ptr_eq(p, defn))),
7591      _ => false,
7592    })
7593  };
7594  let mut seen: FxHashSet<SymStr> = FxHashSet::default();
7595  let mut work = vec![root];
7596  while let Some(body) = work.pop() {
7597    for cs in reads_of_branches(&executed_code(body.unlist_ref()), true) {
7598      let key = cs.get_cs_name();
7599      if key == target {
7600        return Some(true);
7601      }
7602      if !seen.insert(key) {
7603        continue;
7604      }
7605      match own(&cs) {
7606        Some(callee) => work.push(callee),
7607        None if is_title_page(&cs) => return Some(true),
7608        // (LaTeX's own `\@maketitle` or the kernel's marker in it, by identity, reached directly, in an addition to it or
7609        // in a `\let` copy: the class's page, which the declaration answers for)
7610        None if crate::latex_constructs::is_latex_maketitle(&cs) => {
7611          return fm_author_tail_declared();
7612        },
7613        None => {},
7614      }
7615    }
7616  }
7617  Some(false)
7618}
7619
7620/// What `\@author` held when the frontmatter last took its authors: the value the kernel's `\author` stored
7621/// (`lx_author_handed`), else the value it held as a binding's setter queued them (`lx_author_seen`); what a document
7622/// appends to it follows (`\lx@author@tail`, the title-page reading's marks).
7623pub(crate) fn author_taken_prefix() -> Option<Tokens> {
7624  ["lx_author_handed", "lx_author_seen"]
7625    .iter()
7626    .find_map(|key| match lookup_value(key) {
7627      Some(Stored::Tokens(prefix)) => Some(prefix),
7628      _ => None,
7629    })
7630}
7631
7632/// Records the value of `\@author` as an author is queued (`queue_front_matter`): the
7633/// prefix a binding's `\@author` tail follows (`seen_author_tail`). The kernel `\author`'s own record
7634/// (`\lx@author@handed`) clears it.
7635pub fn note_author_seen() {
7636  let value = match store_value("author") {
7637    Ok(Some(value)) => Stored::Tokens(value),
7638    _ => Stored::Bool(false),
7639  };
7640  assign_value("lx_author_seen", value, Some(Scope::Global));
7641}
7642
7643/// The calls handing a tail's rows (no `\and` in them) to the `made` creators of the call it follows (`author_tail_calls`).
7644/// `under_block`: the tail a binding's `\@author` prints under the whole block (`seen_author_tail`) is an unmarked address
7645/// the block's authors share, the frontmatter API's `\lx@add@affiliation@shared` (author_macros.rs): its e-mail lines
7646/// placed as a block's address lines are (one address for each author of the row in their order, else each on the author
7647/// it spells, else one contact an address on the block's shared creator: `shared_affiliation_calls`,
7648/// `place_address_runs`), its other lines one author-block row of every creator — not the e-mail line a row of every
7649/// creator (2511.15315's `\g@addto@macro\@author{\\[0.5em]\small\texttt{…}, …}` under authblk's block: three addresses,
7650/// each spelling its author; review revFX24 S2).
7651fn row_calls(tail: Tokens, made: i64, at_maketitle: bool, under_block: bool) -> Result<Vec<Token>> {
7652  // (a call that made no creator has none to hand its tail to)
7653  if made <= 0 || is_blank(&tail) {
7654    return Ok(Vec::new());
7655  }
7656  // (the call queued its block whole, one entry whose creators the emitter makes at the flush, so a count of the call's
7657  // queued entries reaches its last name alone: where the call made every queued creator, the tail is every creator's,
7658  // Perl's `annotate=all` — `relocateAnnotations`' every parent, Base_Utility.pool.ltxml:520; review revZ4b finding 4)
7659  let annotate = if made as usize == queued_creator_count() {
7660    s!("all")
7661  } else {
7662    made.to_string()
7663  };
7664  let attr = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!(
7665    "_store=author,annotate={annotate},role=authorblock"
7666  )));
7667  if under_block {
7668    return Ok(
7669      Invocation!(T_CS!("\\lx@add@affiliation@shared"), vec![
7670        Some(attr),
7671        Some(tail)
7672      ])
7673      .unlist(),
7674    );
7675  }
7676  let marked = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(
7677    "_store=author,role=authorblock".to_string(),
7678  ));
7679  let authors_queued = at_maketitle && made as usize == queued_creator_count();
7680  affiliation_calls(
7681    Some(attr),
7682    Some(marked),
7683    None,
7684    tail,
7685    queued_authors_marked()?,
7686    authors_queued,
7687  )
7688}
7689
7690/// A store value of spaces only.
7691fn is_blank(value: &Tokens) -> bool {
7692  value
7693    .unlist_ref()
7694    .iter()
7695    .all(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::SPACE)
7696}
7697
7698/// The value of the setter `\<name>`'s store (`\@<name>`, or the one rule (a) found: [`store_of`]): its expansion, when it is a parameterless macro (a
7699/// `\newcommand`-defined value carries an empty parameter list, as in `\lx@deposit@maketitle`).
7700pub(crate) fn store_value(name: &str) -> Result<Option<Tokens>> {
7701  let Some(defn) = lookup_definition(&T_CS!(&s!("\\{}", store_of(name))))? else {
7702    return Ok(None);
7703  };
7704  Ok(match defn.get_expansion() {
7705    Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body))
7706      if defn
7707        .get_parameters()
7708        .is_none_or(|p| p.get_parameters().is_empty()) =>
7709    {
7710      Some(body.clone())
7711    },
7712    _ => None,
7713  })
7714}
7715
7716/// Does the title page print the store of `\<name>` in one line with another value of the class's, as they are now:
7717/// one of the other values of its lines the class left printing nothing (`lx_store_line_values_<name>`,
7718/// `reroute_raw_class_stores`) prints text ([`prints_as_a_value`])?
7719fn prints_in_a_line_with_a_value(name: &str) -> bool {
7720  matches!(lookup_value(&s!("lx_store_line_values_{name}")),
7721    Some(Stored::Tokens(values)) if values.unlist_ref().iter().any(prints_as_a_value))
7722}
7723
7724/// At `\maketitle` (`\lx@store@defaults`), or at the frontmatter fallback in a document without
7725/// one (`at_maketitle` false; again at insertion, a no-op once read): hand the captured stores not yet handed on to their
7726/// frontmatter API, once — every creator-annotating or creator-making store the document set (in
7727/// the order it set them), every named abstract or keywords store it set (`labelled_api`: the class's store, which
7728/// LaTeX expands at `\maketitle`), and, only at `\maketitle` (nothing reads one otherwise), any store left
7729/// at its class default that the title page prints (lion-msc.cls:196-203 `\gdef\@affiliation{Huygens-Kamerlingh Onnes
7730/// Laboratory, …}`, which pdflatex prints; not tlp.cls:697-728's running-head placeholders,
7731/// `ClassReaders::read_by_the_title_page`). A blank store is skipped. The creator-making stores go
7732/// after the annotating ones when there is an author (their annotations stay the authors'), before
7733/// them when there is none (an affiliation then annotates the speaker); a creator-annotating store
7734/// with no creator at all is not handed on and stays the class's to print.
7735pub fn harvest_stores(at_maketitle: bool) -> Result<Vec<Digested>> {
7736  if lookup_bool("lx_stores_harvested") {
7737    return Ok(Vec::new());
7738  }
7739  let names = lookup_string("lx_rerouted_stores");
7740  // (a printed token register, [`toks_store`], is read at `\maketitle` only: its page style is the title page's)
7741  let toks = if at_maketitle {
7742    lookup_string("lx_toks_stores")
7743  } else {
7744    String::new()
7745  };
7746  if names.is_empty() && toks.is_empty() {
7747    return Ok(Vec::new());
7748  }
7749  assign_value("lx_stores_harvested", true, Some(Scope::Global));
7750  let captured: Vec<&str> = names.split(',').filter(|n| !n.is_empty()).collect();
7751  let set_order = lookup_string("lx_store_set_order");
7752  let mut ordered: Vec<&str> = set_order
7753    .split(',')
7754    .filter(|n| captured.contains(n))
7755    .collect();
7756  for name in &captured {
7757    if !ordered.contains(name) {
7758      ordered.push(name);
7759    }
7760  }
7761  // A store set after the last harvest was handed on when it was set (`\lx@store@late`): a harvest
7762  // re-armed for a superseding `\maketitle` (`\lx@maketitle@supersede`) does not hand it again.
7763  let late = lookup_string("lx_stores_late");
7764  ordered.retain(|name| !late.split(',').any(|n| n == *name));
7765  assign_value(
7766    "lx_stores_late",
7767    Stored::String(pin("")),
7768    Some(Scope::Global),
7769  );
7770  // Without a `\maketitle` nothing reads a class default (pdflatex prints none): only the stores the
7771  // document set are handed on.
7772  if !at_maketitle {
7773    ordered.retain(|name| lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_set_{name}")));
7774  }
7775  // An editor or speaker store makes its creator after the authors' annotations are placed; with no
7776  // author, before them, so they annotate it.
7777  let has_author = has_front_matter("ltx:creator");
7778  let makes_creator = ordered.iter().any(|name| {
7779    creates_a_creator(name)
7780      && store_value(name)
7781        .ok()
7782        .flatten()
7783        .is_some_and(|v| !is_blank(&v))
7784  });
7785  if has_author {
7786    ordered.sort_by_key(|name| creates_a_creator(name));
7787  } else {
7788    ordered.sort_by_key(|name| !creates_a_creator(name));
7789  }
7790  let has_creator = has_author || makes_creator;
7791  let mut calls: Vec<Token> = Vec::new();
7792  // (the stores handed on here: a second setter of one, a class's `\let\keyword\keywords`, hands it on no second time —
7793  // review probes `al1`, `al2`)
7794  let mut handed: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
7795  for name in ordered {
7796    let Some(api) = store_api(name) else {
7797      continue;
7798    };
7799    // (without a `\maketitle`, no title page prints the store: an ordinary entry, `untagged_api`)
7800    let api = if at_maketitle {
7801      api
7802    } else {
7803      untagged_api(&api, name)
7804    };
7805    let api = api.as_str();
7806    // (a deferred store's value as the setter left it, when the store no longer holds it: a class's own `\maketitle`
7807    // that runs — not refused — prints it and clears it, ptp-prep.clo:27-34 `\@abst … \gdef\@abst{}`, before the
7808    // fallback reads the stores: 0906.3938, 0907.2968)
7809    let recorded = match lookup_value(&s!("lx_store_value_{name}")) {
7810      Some(Stored::Tokens(value)) if lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_deferred_{name}")) => Some(value),
7811      _ => None,
7812    };
7813    let value = match store_value(name)? {
7814      Some(value) if !is_blank(&value) => value,
7815      current => match recorded.or(current) {
7816        Some(value) => value,
7817        None => continue,
7818      },
7819    };
7820    if is_blank(&value) {
7821      continue;
7822    }
7823    if annotates_a_creator(name) {
7824      if !has_creator {
7825        continue;
7826      }
7827    } else if !creates_a_creator(name)
7828      && lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_set_{name}"))
7829      && !lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_deferred_{name}"))
7830    {
7831      // handed on when it was set (a named abstract or keywords store only stored, `labelled_api`)
7832      continue;
7833    }
7834    // A class default only the title page prints (`ClassReaders::read_by_the_title_page`).
7835    if !lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_set_{name}"))
7836      && !lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_title_page_reads_{name}"))
7837    {
7838      continue;
7839    }
7840    // A store the title page prints in one line with another value of the class's, now that the document has set them
7841    // (`ClassReaders::store_lines`): the line stays the class's, which the deposit prints whole, the store un-nulled
7842    // (`\lx@captured@stores`) — `{\itshape submitted \@submitted; e-mail \@email\par}` with the document's
7843    // `\submitted{1 May 2026}` (review rev79a Q1).
7844    if at_maketitle && prints_in_a_line_with_a_value(name) {
7845      Info!(
7846        "frontmatter",
7847        "\\maketitle",
7848        s!(
7849          "The raw class's \\{name} stays the class's: its title page prints it in one line with another value"
7850        )
7851      );
7852      continue;
7853    }
7854    let store = store_of(name);
7855    if handed.contains(&store) {
7856      assign_value(&s!("lx_store_handed_{name}"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
7857      continue;
7858    }
7859    // (an `\and` list of affiliations, its separators by meaning (`holds_and_separators`): whether the class's title
7860    // page numbers its entries, which the authors' numeric marks pick — `class_numbered_institutes`, at
7861    // `\lx@add@store@affiliations`)
7862    if at_maketitle
7863      && api.starts_with("\\lx@add@store@affiliations")
7864      && holds_and_separators(value.unlist_ref())?
7865    {
7866      note_and_numbering(&T_CS!(&s!("\\{store}")))?;
7867    }
7868    handed.push(store);
7869    note_store_handed(name);
7870    // (what the title page prints of it is the class's title page's, its title-page provenance, base_utilities
7871    // `TITLE_PAGE_ENTRY`; a creator and what annotates one are no frontmatter a late abstract's cover can follow)
7872    let from = at_maketitle && !annotates_a_creator(name) && !creates_a_creator(name);
7873    if from {
7874      calls.extend(store_from_tokens(name));
7875    }
7876    let (before, after) = api.split_once("#1").unwrap_or((api, ""));
7877    calls.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(before.to_string())).unlist());
7878    calls.extend(value.unlist());
7879    calls.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(after.to_string())).unlist());
7880    if from {
7881      calls.extend(store_from_tokens(""));
7882    }
7883  }
7884  // A printed token register the document filled ([`toks_store`]): the table's call of its value, led by the label the
7885  // class prints before it.
7886  for name in toks.split(',').filter(|n| !n.is_empty()) {
7887    let (Some(value), Some(api)) = (toks_value(name), store_api(name)) else {
7888      continue;
7889    };
7890    if is_blank(&value) {
7891      continue;
7892    }
7893    let mut content = match lookup_value(&s!("lx_toks_label_{name}")) {
7894      Some(Stored::Tokens(label)) => label.unlist(),
7895      _ => Vec::new(),
7896    };
7897    content.extend(value.unlist());
7898    calls.extend(store_from_tokens(name));
7899    let (before, after) = api.split_once("#1").unwrap_or((api.as_str(), ""));
7900    calls.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(before.to_string())).unlist());
7901    calls.extend(content);
7902    calls.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(after.to_string())).unlist());
7903    calls.extend(store_from_tokens(""));
7904  }
7905  let digested = if calls.is_empty() {
7906    Vec::new()
7907  } else {
7908    vec![digest_store_calls(calls)?]
7909  };
7910  // (what this `\maketitle` placed, whose setter called again only re-sets the store, is settled once its title page
7911  // ran: `settle_placed_stores`)
7912  Ok(digested)
7913}
7914
7915LoadDefinitions!({
7916  // A class's affiliation-kind store (`\institution`, `\department`, `\institute`, `\inst`, …) is
7917  // printed once under the whole author list (bfhlayout.sty:744-754, courseoutline.cls:150), so its
7918  // affiliations annotate every creator (`annotate=all`; marker-labelled ones go by their labels
7919  // when the authors carry marks).
7920  // Each is tagged `_store=<name>`: an empty one (a default that only warns, umich-thesis.cls:71) is
7921  // not kept (`insert_frontmatter_entry`).
7922  DefMacro!("\\lx@add@store@affiliations{}{}", sub[(name, stuff)] {
7923    let name = name.to_string();
7924    // (an llncs-style `\institute{A \and B}` the class numbers 1, 2 on its title page: the entries labelled by their
7925    // numbers, which the authors' numeric marks — `$^{1}$`, `\inst{1}` — request, `class_numbered_institutes`)
7926    // (its e-mail lines whose command prints nothing in place unwrapped, each address the entry's: cjaa.cls's `\email`
7927    // setting `\@thanks`, astro-ph0508574, 0911.3853, 0706.3426)
7928    let (stuff, numbered) = match class_numbered_institutes(&stuff, Some(&s!("\\{}", store_of(&name))))? {
7929      Some(numbered) => (store_unprinted_emails_unwrapped(numbered)?, true),
7930      None => (stuff, false),
7931    };
7932    let attr = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!("_store={name},annotate=all")));
7933    let marked = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!("_store={name}")));
7934    // Superscript marks label the affiliations only when the authors carry marks to match; else
7935    // they are the class's text, and the affiliations annotate every author.
7936    Ok(Tokens::new(affiliation_calls(
7937      Some(attr),
7938      Some(marked),
7939      None,
7940      stuff,
7941      numbered || queued_authors_marked()?,
7942      true,
7943    )?))
7944  });
7945  // An off-table store the class's own `\@author` prints beside the names (jfpc.cls:188-191 `\@lesinstitutions`,
7946  // `\@lesmels`; 1503.05530): rows of the author block (`role=authorblock`, unlabelled unless the class prints a label
7947  // before it), annotating every creator, as an `\author` call's tail is (`author_tail_calls`): the creators' data,
7948  // where an `ltx:pubnote` made them a note on the document (`hand_untypeset_stores`; under `ltx_authors_1line` a
7949  // creator's contacts are a hover popup too, LaTeXML.css:74-88).
7950  DefMacro!("\\lx@add@store@authorblock{}{}", sub[(name, stuff)] {
7951    let name = name.to_string();
7952    let attr = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!("_store={name},annotate=all,role=authorblock")));
7953    let marked = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!("_store={name},role=authorblock")));
7954    Ok(Tokens::new(affiliation_calls(
7955      Some(attr),
7956      Some(marked),
7957      None,
7958      stuff,
7959      queued_authors_marked()?,
7960      true,
7961    )?))
7962  });
7963  // The author block: what code appends to `\@author` after `\author` stored it — a template's
7964  // `\address`, `\correspondence`, `\email` rows (`\g@addto@macro\@author{\\…}`; hindawi's template,
7965  // cjs-rcs-article.cls:361 `\affil`) — is what LaTeX's `\@maketitle` prints with the names
7966  // (article.cls:247). The creators carry the names already, so each `\author` call's tail is handed
7967  // to the creators that call made, as author-block rows (`role=authorblock`, unlabelled). Perl drops
7968  // it (latex_constructs.pool.ltxml:1076-1079, :1099-1107). `\lx@author@handed` (after the creators
7969  // are queued) records what `\author` stored and how many creators it made; `\lx@author@flush`
7970  // (before a redefined, appending `\author` stores the next) and `\lx@author@tail` (at `\maketitle`)
7971  // hand the tail on. Only a tail after exactly what `\author` stored: a class that rebuilds
7972  // `\@author` in its own form (revtex's `{{#2}{}}`, acmart) is left alone. A tail holding `\and`
7973  // goes on with more authors (`author_tail_calls`, FX24: jaa.cls/pramana.cls `\addauthor`); a binding
7974  // whose `\author` stores nothing in `\@author` (authblk) has its tail read at `\maketitle`
7975  // (`seen_author_tail`, 2511.15315).
7976  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@author@handed", sub[_args] {
7977    let stored = store_value("author")?.filter(|stored| !is_blank(stored));
7978    match stored {
7979      Some(stored) => {
7980        let made = queued_creator_count().saturating_sub(lookup_int("lx_author_creators_before") as usize);
7981        assign_value("lx_author_handed", Stored::Tokens(stored), Some(Scope::Global));
7982        assign_value("lx_author_made", Stored::Int(made as i64), Some(Scope::Global));
7983      },
7984      None => assign_value("lx_author_handed", Stored::Bool(false), Some(Scope::Global)),
7985    }
7986     // (the kernel `\author` stored `\@author`: not a binding's untouched one, `seen_author_tail`)
7987    assign_value("lx_author_seen", Stored::Bool(false), Some(Scope::Global));
7988  });
7989  DefMacro!("\\lx@author@flush", sub[_args] {
7990    // Only an appending `\author` (a class redefined it) keeps the earlier authors; the kernel's
7991    // replaces them and `\@author` with them, the tail too, as LaTeX does.
7992    let calls = if lookup_bool("\\author:redefined") { author_tail_calls(false)? } else { Vec::new() };
7993    assign_value("lx_author_handed", Stored::Bool(false), Some(Scope::Global));
7994    assign_value(
7995      "lx_author_creators_before",
7996      Stored::Int(queued_creator_count() as i64),
7997      Some(Scope::Global),
7998    );
7999    Ok(Tokens::new(calls))
8000  });
8001  DefMacro!("\\lx@author@tail", sub[_args] {
8002    // (what `\@author` held before a document appended to it, for this `\maketitle`'s title-page readings, which mark
8003    // where the appended part begins: its records are cleared here, the check reads after)
8004    crate::latex_constructs::title_page_reading::note_author_prefix(author_taken_prefix());
8005    let calls = author_tail_calls(true)?;
8006    assign_value("lx_author_handed", Stored::Bool(false), Some(Scope::Global));
8007    assign_value("lx_author_seen", Stored::Bool(false), Some(Scope::Global));
8008    Ok(Tokens::new(calls))
8009  });
8010  // A rerouted setter records that the document set its store, in order.
8011  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@store@set{}", sub[(name)] {
8012    let name = name.to_string();
8013    assign_value(&s!("lx_store_set_{name}"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
8014    let order = lookup_string("lx_store_set_order");
8015    if !order.split(',').any(|n| n == name) {
8016      let order = if order.is_empty() { name } else { s!("{order},{name}") };
8017      assign_value("lx_store_set_order", Stored::String(pin(order)), Some(Scope::Global));
8018    }
8019  });
8020  // A store handed to the frontmatter: the deposited `\@maketitle` reads it as empty, and the label its title page
8021  // prints before it (`labelled_api`), now the entry's name, is taken out with it.
8022  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@store@handed{}", sub[(name)] {
8023    note_store_handed(&name.to_string());
8024  });
8025  // The frontmatter entries queued from here on are handed on from the store `\<name>` (empty: no more), the
8026  // title-page provenance (base_utilities `TITLE_PAGE_ENTRY`).
8027  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@store@from{}", sub[(name)] {
8028    assign_value(STORE_ENTRY_FROM, Stored::String(pin(name.to_string())), Some(Scope::Global));
8029  });
8030  // A store handed on as it is set (`#2`, its frontmatter call): once the `\maketitle` whose title page printed it has
8031  // placed its value (`lx_store_placed_<name>`, `settle_placed_stores`: handed on, and its sentinel reached the
8032  // speculative run of the title page), the setter called again only re-sets the store, for what reads it later, and
8033  // does not replace the placed entry — the title page printed the first value: a class's `\keywords{alpha, beta}`
8034  // before `\maketitle` and `\keywords{gamma}` after it, "KW: alpha, beta" in the PDF (silent-loss #6b; as a `.bbl`'s
8035  // re-call of a raw class's `\doi` after the body began, 1710.01977, re-sets only the store under the seal,
8036  // `seal_digested_frontmatter`). A store the title page does not print takes the later value (a class abstract's end
8037  // code `Keywords: \@keywords`), and one first set after `\maketitle` is still handed on (pittetd.cls:503-527's
8038  // abstract prints keywords set there).
8039  // A named abstract or keywords store (`labelled_api`) set before `\maketitle` only stores: `\maketitle` hands the
8040  // store on (`harvest_stores`); set after it, it is handed on at once, as `\lx@store@late` hands one.
8041  DefMacro!("\\lx@store@hand{}{}", sub[(name, call)] {
8042    let name = name.to_string();
8043    let harvested = lookup_bool("lx_stores_harvested");
8044    let deferred = lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_deferred_{name}"));
8045    if deferred && !harvested {
8046      // (the value it stores, for a harvest that finds the store cleared, `harvest_stores`)
8047      if let Some(value) = store_value(&name)? {
8048        assign_value(&s!("lx_store_value_{name}"), Stored::Tokens(value), Some(Scope::Global));
8049      }
8050      return Ok(Tokens::new(Vec::new()));
8051    }
8052    if harvested && lookup_bool(&s!("lx_store_placed_{name}")) {
8053      Info!(
8054        "frontmatter",
8055        s!("\\{name}"),
8056        s!("\\{name} set again after \\maketitle placed it re-sets the class's store; the frontmatter keeps the title page's value")
8057      );
8058      return Ok(Tokens::new(Vec::new()));
8059    }
8060    let mut out = store_from_tokens(&name);
8061    out.extend(
8062      mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!("\\lx@store@handed{{{name}}}"))).unlist(),
8063    );
8064    if deferred {
8065      note_store_late(&name);
8066      // (the call `\maketitle` settled — named by the class's label only when its title page printed it,
8067      // `verify_title_page_labels` —, of the value just stored)
8068      // (an ordinary entry: the title page that read the store printed another value, `untagged_api`)
8069      if let (Some(api), Some(value)) = (store_api(&name), store_value(&name)?) {
8070        let api = untagged_api(&api, &name);
8071        let (before, after) = api.split_once("#1").unwrap_or((api.as_str(), ""));
8072        out.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(before.to_string())).unlist());
8073        out.extend(value.unlist());
8074        out.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(after.to_string())).unlist());
8075        out.extend(store_from_tokens(""));
8076        return Ok(Tokens::new(out));
8077      }
8078    }
8079    out.extend(call.unlist());
8080    out.extend(store_from_tokens(""));
8081    Ok(Tokens::new(out))
8082  });
8083  // A creator-annotating or creator-making store set after `\maketitle` read the stores: its value
8084  // — the setter's argument, not an accumulated store (`\g@addto@macro`) — is handed on at once.
8085  DefMacro!("\\lx@store@late{}{}", sub[(name, value)] {
8086    let name = name.to_string();
8087    if !lookup_bool("lx_stores_harvested") {
8088      return Ok(Tokens::new(Vec::new()));
8089    }
8090    let Some(api) = store_api(&name) else {
8091      return Ok(Tokens::new(Vec::new()));
8092    };
8093    let api = api.as_str();
8094    note_store_late(&name);
8095    let mut calls =
8096      mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(s!("\\lx@store@handed{{{name}}}"))).unlist();
8097    let (before, after) = api.split_once("#1").unwrap_or((api, ""));
8098    calls.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(before.to_string())).unlist());
8099    calls.extend(value.unlist());
8100    calls.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(after.to_string())).unlist());
8101    Ok(Tokens::new(calls))
8102  });
8103  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@store@untypeset", sub[_args] {
8104    if lookup_bool("lx_depositing_class_maketitle") || lookup_bool("lx_replaying_class_setter") {
8105      return Ok(Vec::new());
8106    }
8107    settle_placed_stores();
8108    release_unprinted_store_entries();
8109    hand_untypeset_stores()
8110  });
8111  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@store@defaults", sub[_args] {
8112    verify_title_page_labels()?;
8113    hand_copy_frontmatter_defaults();
8114    // (the displaced setters' calls the title page prints, the authors' contacts before any store makes a creator of
8115    // its own: [`route_and_hand_displaced`])
8116    let mut harvested = route_and_hand_displaced()?;
8117    harvested.extend(harvest_stores(true)?);
8118    Ok(harvested)
8119  });
8120  // The first token of the kernel abstract's body (`\lx@begin@abstract`, base_utilities.rs), in the frame it is
8121  // digested in: the table's keywords setters the refused class abstract defines for its body
8122  // ([`abstract_setter_definitions`]) are defined there, for the body only, as the frontmatter's keywords named by
8123  // the class's label where its code shows one ([`label_before_argument`]: astlb.cls:200 "Key words:", 1202.2889) —
8124  // made as the class makes them: a `\def` replaces the document's own `\keywords` there, as in pdflatex, a
8125  // `\newcommand` leaves it (1706.00599, 1202.2889, 1310.0585).
8126  // An abstract kept in place as a supplement's (after the document body began, `\lx@add@frontmatter@until`) keeps
8127  // its keywords in place with it, and the paper's keywords stay the frontmatter's (mtt.cls papers' English and
8128  // Ukrainian abstracts at the end: 1310.0585, 1310.0589, 1212.3890): there the setter is the class's own definition
8129  // when it only typesets its label and argument ([`typesets_label_and_argument`]), else a paragraph of the class's
8130  // label where its code shows one and the keywords — mtt.cls:181's `\item` belongs to the class's own abstract
8131  // list, which the kernel's abstract is not, and its language-chosen `\mtt@keywords` cannot be read, so its
8132  // keywords have no label in place: never the English default name where pdflatex prints "Ключовi слова:". A
8133  // setter that does not typeset its argument (it warns, or drops it) is the class's own definition, as in pdflatex.
8134  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@abstract@class@setters", {
8135    // The body is the abstract's, for [`refused_environment_definition`]: the kernel's abstract is no environment
8136    // frame (`\@currenvir` stays the enclosing one).
8137    assign_value("lx_in_kernel_abstract", true, Some(Scope::Local));
8138    if lookup_bool("lx_abstract_begun") {
8139      assign_value("lx_abstract_begun", false, Some(Scope::Global));
8140      assign_value("lx@until@begun", "abstract", Some(Scope::Local));
8141    }
8142    let in_place = lookup_bool("lx_frontmatter_kept_in_place");
8143    let setters = abstract_setter_definitions();
8144    // (whether the document defined each before the class's begin code runs: a `\newcommand` there leaves it)
8145    let mut defined = Vec::with_capacity(setters.len());
8146    for (name, ..) in &setters {
8147      defined.push(lookup_definition(&T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}")))?.is_some());
8148    }
8149    // The class's refused begin code runs here for its effects, held in the abstract's group
8150    // (`replay_class_begin_code`, sect05.rs: frontiersFPHY.cls:520 `\let\section\absection`, 1508.06756); the
8151    // keywords setters below are the body's.
8152    let keep_out: Vec<SymStr> = setters
8153      .iter()
8154      .map(|(name, ..)| meaning_key(&T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"))))
8155      .collect();
8156    // (not for a brace-less `\abstract`: `\lx@abstract@braceless`, sect05.rs)
8157    let braceless = lookup_bool("lx_abstract_braceless");
8158    if braceless {
8159      assign_value("lx_abstract_braceless", false, Some(Scope::Global));
8160    } else {
8161      crate::latex_constructs::replay_class_begin_code("abstract", &keep_out)?;
8162      hand_net_begin_code_stores()?;
8163    }
8164    for ((name, api, definer, own), defined) in setters.into_iter().zip(defined) {
8165      let cs = T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"));
8166      if definer == Definer::Keeps && defined {
8167        continue;
8168      }
8169      let label = label_before_argument(&own);
8170      let body = if argument_at(&own).is_none() || (in_place && typesets_label_and_argument(&own)) {
8171        own
8172      } else if in_place {
8173        // (no label when the class's cannot be read: the default name is English where the PDF's need not be)
8174        let mut body = mouth::tokenize_internal("\\par").unlist();
8175        if let Some(label) = label {
8176          body.push(T_BEGIN!());
8177          body.extend(label.unlist());
8178          body.push(T_END!());
8179          body.push(T_SPACE!());
8180        }
8181        body.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal("#1").unlist());
8182        body
8183      } else {
8184        named_keywords_call(api, label)
8185      };
8186      DefMacro!(cs, convert_latex_args(1, None)?, Tokens::new(body));
8187      Info!(
8188        "frontmatter",
8189        s!("\\{name}"),
8190        s!("The class's abstract defines \\{name} for its body: it sets the abstract's keywords")
8191      );
8192    }
8193  });
8194  // A raw class's label-only `{keywords}` (`reroute_keywords_environments`): `\begin{keywords}` … `\end{keywords}`
8195  // is the frontmatter's keywords, read up to the end; called as a command, `\keywords{…}` (paper.cls's papers write
8196  // both) is the keywords of its argument when that group ends them — `\par`, a blank line or an `\end…` follows it
8197  // ([`braced_group_ends_the_keywords`]); any other `\keywords`, whose extent only the class's layout knows
8198  // (`\keywords {alpha}, beta, gamma`), runs the class's own begin code (`\lx@class@keywords@<name>`), as before.
8199  DefMacro!("\\lx@class@keywords{}", sub[(name)] {
8200    let name = name.to_string();
8201    let label = match lookup_value(&s!("lx_class_keywords_label_{name}")) {
8202      Some(Stored::Tokens(label)) => Some(label),
8203      _ => None,
8204    };
8205    let current = Expand!(Tokens!(T_CS!("\\@currenvir"))).to_string();
8206    let api = if current == name {
8207      "\\lx@begin@keywords"
8208    } else if braced_group_ends_the_keywords()? {
8209      "\\lx@add@keywords"
8210    } else {
8211      return Ok(Tokens!(T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@class@keywords@{name}"))));
8212    };
8213    Ok(Tokens::new(named_keywords_call(api, label)))
8214  });
8215  // Its `\end<name>`: in the environment the end of the frontmatter's keywords, else (a bare `\keywords alpha
8216  // \endkeywords`) the class's own end code, kept as `\lx@class@keywords@end@<name>` (paper.cls:622 `\par\bigskip`).
8217  DefMacro!("\\lx@class@keywords@end{}", sub[(name)] {
8218    let name = name.to_string();
8219    let current = Expand!(Tokens!(T_CS!("\\@currenvir"))).to_string();
8220    Ok(if current == name {
8221      Tokens!(T_CS!("\\lx@end@keywords"))
8222    } else {
8223      Tokens!(T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@class@keywords@end@{name}")))
8224    })
8225  });
8226  // Fired by `input_definitions` after a `.cls` is loaded raw
8227  // (latexml_core/src/binding/content.rs).
8228  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@class@loaded@raw{}", sub[(cls)] {
8229    let cls = cls.to_string();
8230    note_raw_register_defaults();
8231    crate::latex_constructs::keep_class_author_reader();
8232    reroute_raw_class_stores(&cls)?;
8233    reroute_in_place_contact_setters(&cls)?;
8234    reroute_displaced_setters(&cls)?;
8235  });
8236  // After the begin-document hooks, the `begindocument/end` one too, which `\begin{document}` reads after itself
8237  // (sect02.rs): what they made is a class default for the net ([`note_document_hooks`]).
8238  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@net@document@hooks@done", {
8239    note_document_hooks(false);
8240  });
8241  // A displaced setter's call ([`reroute_displaced_setters`]): the file's own setter runs under a meaning recorder, and
8242  // what it stored of its argument is recorded ([`DISPLACED_RECORDS`]). The recorder is no assignment journal, which
8243  // does not nest: a journal the setter's own code opens — a speculative read of the mark it prints
8244  // (`speculative_label_ink`; utarticle.cls:125's `\address` prints `\@makeadmark{\@tempb}`, 1209.6020), a replay —
8245  // still opens (review rev99b B4). Not in a speculative run or a replay, whose stores are undone.
8246  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@displaced@begin", {
8247    let records =
8248      !lookup_bool("lx_replaying_class_setter") && !lookup_bool("lx_depositing_class_maketitle");
8249    if records {
8250      begin_meaning_recorder();
8251    }
8252    let open = lookup_string(DISPLACED_OPEN);
8253    assign_value(
8254      DISPLACED_OPEN,
8255      Stored::from(s!("{open}{}", u8::from(records))),
8256      Some(Scope::Global),
8257    );
8258  });
8259  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@displaced@end{}{}", sub[(name, arg)] {
8260    let mut open = lookup_string(DISPLACED_OPEN);
8261    let recorded = open.pop() == Some('1');
8262    assign_value(DISPLACED_OPEN, Stored::from(open), Some(Scope::Global));
8263    if !recorded {
8264      DebugFeature!("frontmatter", "FRONT displaced {name}: not recorded");
8265      return Ok(Vec::new());
8266    }
8267    let assigned = end_meaning_recorder();
8268    let name = name.to_string();
8269    // (an argument of fewer than two letters is no affiliation's or address's text a part could be told by)
8270    let letters = arg.unlist_ref().iter().filter(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::LETTER).count();
8271    let parts = if letters < 2 {
8272      Vec::new()
8273    } else {
8274      crate::latex_constructs::assigned_parts_holding(&assigned, &arg, &[], true)
8275    };
8276    DebugFeature!(
8277      "frontmatter",
8278      "FRONT displaced {name}: assigned [{}], {} part(s)",
8279      assigned.iter().map(|(k, _)| with(*k, ToString::to_string)).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(","),
8280      parts.len()
8281    );
8282    if parts.is_empty() {
8283      return Ok(Vec::new());
8284    }
8285    let mut records = match lookup_value(DISPLACED_RECORDS) {
8286      Some(Stored::VecDequeStored(records)) => records,
8287      _ => VecDeque::new(),
8288    };
8289    for (key, part) in parts {
8290      records.push_back(Stored::VecDequeStored(VecDeque::from([
8291        Stored::String(pin(name.trim())),
8292        Stored::String(key),
8293        Stored::Tokens(Tokens::new(part)),
8294        Stored::Tokens(arg.clone()),
8295      ])));
8296    }
8297    assign_value(DISPLACED_RECORDS, Stored::VecDequeStored(records), Some(Scope::Global));
8298    Ok(Vec::new())
8299  });
8300  // A rerouted contact setter's call that prints its line where it is called ([`class_contact`]).
8301  DefMacro!("\\lx@class@contact{}{}", sub[(api, class)] {
8302    class_contact(api, class, true)
8303  }, protected => true);
8304  // …and the call of a rerouted setter that stores its line for the title page (11lomcon.sty's `\@aabuffer`, jetpl's

8306  // where the call stands decides nothing — it is the API's while the authors are being set, wherever it is called
8307  // (stacs's `\address[uniroma]{…}` after the kernel's `\author` left the class's third argument "Gianni Franceschini"

8321  }, protected => true);
8322  // The kernel's `\author[key]{…}` (sect05.rs): the box the call stands in (`\lx@author@box`, [`at_the_body_level`]),
8323  // a contact line set in it the title block's;

8332  // …the number of author creators before the call (`\lx@author@keys@from`), then its keys and that number after it
8333  // (`\lx@author@keys`): the authors a rerouted setter's `[key]` names ([`optional_contact`]). A short author
8334  // (`\author[B.F. Roukema]{…}`) records as a key too, which only a setter's equal key reads.
8335  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@author@keys@from", {
8336    // (the records of the authors placed or sealed are no new author's: cleared, review rev94b r3 (a))
8337    if author_creators_placed() || frontmatter_entry_sealed("ltx:creator", "author") {
8338      assign_value(
8339        AUTHOR_KEYS,
8340        Stored::from(String::new()),
8341        Some(Scope::Global),
8342      );
8343    }
8344    assign_value(
8345      "lx_author_keys_from",
8346      Stored::Int(author_creator_total()? as i64),
8347      Some(Scope::Global),
8348    );
8349  });
8350  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@author@keys{}", sub[(keys)] {
8351    let keys = keys.to_string();
8352    let keys = keys.trim();
8353    let to = author_creator_total()?;
8354    let from = usize::try_from(lookup_int("lx_author_keys_from")).unwrap_or(to).min(to);
8355    if !keys.is_empty() && to > from {
8356      let mut records = lookup_string(AUTHOR_KEYS);
8357      if !records.is_empty() {
8358        records.push('\u{1}');
8359      }
8360      records.push_str(&s!("{keys}\u{2}{from}\u{2}{to}"));
8361      assign_value(AUTHOR_KEYS, Stored::from(records), Some(Scope::Global));
8362    }
8363  });
8364  // A kept class body's call of a rerouted setter (`class_bodies_call_class_copies`, through the one token
8365  // `\lx@class@contact@dispatch@<name>`): the setter's class definition while the setter means the reroute, else what it
8366  // means now (a document's redefinition) — one token, nothing left after it for the class's look-ahead.
8367  DefMacro!("\\lx@class@contact@dispatch{}", sub[(name)] {
8368    let name = name.to_string();
8369    let name = name.trim();
8370    let cs = T_CS!(&s!("\\{name}"));
8371    Ok(if x_equals(&cs, &T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@class@contact@rerouted@{name}"))) {
8372      Tokens!(T_CS!(&s!("\\lx@class@contact@{name}")))
8373    } else {
8374      Tokens!(cs)
8375    })
8376  });
8377  // A cut entry's font (`observer::emitter::plan::entry_font_tokens`): the font changes in effect where the entry's
8378  // cut starts in its call's content, registered as the `n`-th — the entry digested under them, as in the call
8379  // (review rev94b r3 B1: `\affil{\itshape $^1$Univ A\\ $^2$Univ B}` lost the italic on Univ B).
8380  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@fm@entry@font{}", sub[(n)] {
8381    if let Some(Stored::Font(font)) = lookup_value(&s!("lx_fm_entry_font_{}", n.to_string().trim())) {
8382      merge_font_ref(&font);
8383    }
8384  });
8385  // …and after a `.sty` is loaded raw: its table stores, unless a raw class's read loads it, which reroutes them at
8386  // its end (`reroute_raw_package_stores`; 2307.16262's medima.sty), and its contact setters
8387  // (`reroute_in_place_contact_setters`; 11lomcon.sty, hep-ph0312288).
8388  DefPrimitive!("\\lx@package@loaded@raw{}", sub[(sty)] {
8389    let sty = sty.to_string();
8390    note_raw_register_defaults();
8391    crate::latex_constructs::keep_class_author_reader();
8392    if !in_a_raw_class_read() {
8393      reroute_raw_package_stores(&sty)?;
8394    }
8395    reroute_in_place_contact_setters(&sty)?;
8396    // (a package a raw class's read loads is that class's, its setters K11's to take first at the class's end)
8397    if !in_a_raw_class_read() {
8398      reroute_displaced_setters(&sty)?;
8399    }
8400  });
8401});
